=== docs\streaming.md ===
# Streaming path

## Stream processor (chunk 19)

`src/sentinalops/stream/processor.py`. Raw trades and depth snapshots in (rows in the recorder schema); `FeatureTick` and `StressPrediction` messages out, keyed by symbol, for the `features` and `predictions` topics.

### Decision: no stream framework (task 19.1)

The processor is a plain class with `on_trade` / `on_depth` / `advance`, plus an async `run(processor, events, publish)` that works with any event source. Kafka wiring (chunk 15's aiokafka wrappers) plugs into `run` without touching the processor. Reasons:

- faust-streaming adds a dependency and its own runtime, and we need none of its tables or windows: `compute_tick` already does the windowing.
- A plain class runs in unit tests with no broker, which is how the consistency test replays a recording.

### Event time and when a tick is emitted

- The clock is `recv_time_ms`, the same as the recorder and the offline pipeline.
- The **watermark** is the newest receive time seen on either stream. Tick `t` (a whole second) is computed once the watermark reaches `t + allowed_lateness_ms` (default 500 ms), so the trade and depth topics can arrive slightly skewed from each other.
- `advance(now_ms)` moves the watermark with the wall clock, so ticks keep being produced (and come out invalid) while the feed is silent.

### Late and out-of-order events (task 19.5)

| Arrival | What happens |
| --- | --- |
| Out of order but after the last emitted tick | Inserted in order; no effect on correctness |
| At or before the last emitted tick ("late") | Dropped and counted in `stats.late[kind]` |

Offline, a late event would have been counted, so **late events are the only way online and offline features can differ**. `stats.late` should stay at zero; if it does not, raise `allowed_lateness_ms`. The test replays a recording with one stream delayed by 300-450 ms and gets identical features with the default lateness, and late drops with too little lateness.

### Suspension while the feed is stale (task 18.5)

`compute_tick` returns nothing when the newest snapshot is older than `stale_data_seconds`, and until the feed has been continuous for the longest window again. So a stale feed produces no feature ticks and no predictions, and recovery needs a full 60 s warm-up. These ticks are counted in `stats.suspended_ticks`.

### Buffers (task 19.2)

Trades are kept for the longest window; depth for the longest window plus the one snapshot before it (plus a `stale_ms` safety margin). Memory is bounded by the feed rate times about 63 seconds.

### Online/offline consistency

`tests/test_stream_processor.py` replays a synthetic recording (with a stress episode and a 10 s outage) through the processor in arrival order and compares every feature tick with `compute_ticks` on the whole recording: same ticks, values equal to 1e-9. Shrinking the depth buffer below what `compute_tick` needs makes the test fail.

One intended difference from the training table: the offline pipeline also drops ticks whose window overlaps an outage in the recorder's gap log. The live processor has no gap log; it relies on staleness only.

### Not done yet

- Kafka consumer/producer wiring and topic creation (chunk 15).
- Prometheus metrics for `ProcessorStats` and consumer lag (task 19.6).
- Speed: each tick rebuilds small DataFrames from the buffers, about 8 ms per tick on a laptop, fine at one tick per second. Replaying a day for chunk 21 at high speed will want an incremental version.


=== docs\TASKS.md ===
# Task breakdown: Liquidity Intelligence

Every open chunk from `docs/ROADMAP.md` is split into small tasks. Pick the next unticked task for your name. Task IDs are `chunk.number` (for example `16.3`). Chunks 1, 4, 5 and 6 are finished.

## Summary

- **276 tasks** across 46 chunks.
- Sizes: S=183, M=92, L=1.
- Tasks per person: Jaswanth 53, Kushal 71, Tanuj 35, Vaibhav 71, Yogya 46.

## How to use this file

- **Sizes:** S is about 1-2 hours, M is about half a day, L is about a day. Nothing is bigger than L.
- **Needs:** tasks or chunks that must be finished first. `-` means you can start now.
- **One task = one small pull request** (branch name `chunk-NN-task-M`). Open it as soon as the task is done, do not wait to finish the whole chunk.
- **Done when:** the check that the reviewer will make. If your task meets it and has a test, it is done.
- **Owner `Yogya (rev. Vaibhav)`** means a junior does the task and the named senior reviews it.
- **Stuck for 30 minutes?** Ask in the group chat. Juniors: ask your mentor first, then anyone.

## Words used in the tasks

- **Trade event:** one message from the `btcusdt@aggTrade` stream (price, quantity, trade time, and whether the buyer was the maker).
- **Depth snapshot:** one message from `btcusdt@depth5@100ms` (top five bid and ask levels). It carries no event time, so we stamp it with our receive time.
- **Tick:** one row of features computed once per second.
- **Stress label:** 1 if liquidity stress starts within the next 30 seconds, else 0. Defined in chunk 9.
- **Matured:** a prediction whose 30-second horizon has passed, so its label is known.

=== docs\TEAM_PLAN.md ===
# Team Plan

Chunk numbers match `docs/ROADMAP.md`. The domain is **Liquidity Intelligence** (BTCUSDT on Binance public streams). Chunks 1, 4, 5 and 6 are done; chunks 2, 3, 7, 8 and 9 are reopened for the new domain. Day-to-day work is tracked in [`TASKS.md`](TASKS.md): every open chunk is split into small tasks (about 1â€“4 hours each), with an owner, size, dependencies and a "done when" check.

Nobody's lane changed with the domain. Each chunk number has the same owner as before.

## Roles

| Person | Level | Lane | Chunks |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Kushal** | Lead | Domain switch, self-healing core, serving, verifier, final demo | 2, 3, 28, 30, 31, 33, 34, 38, 39, 42, 43, 44, 45, 47 |
| **Vaibhav** | Senior | Data and ML: recorder, labels, features, models, scenario library | 7, 9, 10, 12, 14, 24, 25, 26, 27, 35, 46, 49 |
| **Jaswanth** | Senior | Streaming and MLOps: Redpanda, processor, Feast, MLflow registry, Prometheus | 11, 15, 19, 20, 22, 23, 29, 32, 37 |
| **Yogya** | Junior | Archive loader, dataset QA, trade producer, dashboards, docs (mentored) | 8, 13, 16, 36, 41, 48 |
| **Tanuj** | Junior | Depth producer, feed health, replay, alert rules, demo video (mentored) | 17, 18, 21, 40, 50 |

## Junior onboarding (Yogya and Tanuj, before their first chunk)

1. Install Python 3.11+, git, VS Code. Learn basic Python (functions, dicts, files) and git (clone, branch, commit, pull request).
2. Clone the repo, run `pip install -e ".[dev]"`, then `pytest` and `ruff check .`. Read `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md`.
3. Read "Domain primer" below, then open the Binance BTCUSDT order book in a browser and watch the best bid, best ask and depth change for five minutes.
4. Open a first pull request: a small test or doc fix. A senior reviews and merges it.
5. Pair with your mentor for 30 minutes before starting your first chunk.

## Domain primer (ten lines everyone should know)

- **Bid / ask:** the highest price a buyer offers and the lowest price a seller asks.
- **Spread:** ask minus bid. A wide spread means trading is expensive.
- **Depth:** how much quantity is resting near the best prices. We use the top five levels on each side.
- **Imbalance:** whether bids or asks dominate, from -1 (all asks) to +1 (all bids).
- **Liquidity stress:** depth falls well below its recent normal, or the spread widens well above it.
- **Regime:** a stretch of time where the market behaves in one style (calm, volatile, thin).
- **Horizon:** how far ahead we forecast (30 seconds).
- **Outcome verifier:** the job that waits 30 seconds, then checks whether the forecast came true.
- **Leakage:** using information from after the prediction time. It makes results look good and is always a bug.
- **Persistence baseline:** the lazy forecast "whatever is true now stays true". Our model has to beat it.

## Working rules

- One branch per task: `chunk-NN-task-M`. Open a pull request into `main`; never push to `main` directly.
- Every pull request needs CI green (ruff and pytest) and one review. Juniors' pull requests are reviewed by their mentor.
- A chunk is done when it has code, at least one test and a line in `docs/ROADMAP.md` ticked.
- Stuck for more than 30 minutes? Ask. Juniors should not struggle alone.
- Public market data only. No API keys, no account endpoints, no order placement, ever.
- Tests never touch the network. Use saved message fixtures; live checks are opt-in with `RUN_NETWORK_TESTS=1`.
- Every feature function takes a timestamp and must ignore events after it.

## Waves (what can run in parallel)

| Wave | Chunks | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0: start recording | 2, 7 | The recorder runs continuously from here on; every later chunk needs its data |
| 1: data and stream foundations | 3, 8, 9, 10, 11, 15, 16, 17, 34 | Juniors onboard first, then 8 and 17. Chunk 9 decides the label before any model work |
| 2: pipelines and first real model | 12, 13, 14, 18, 19, 20, 21, 22, 23, 24, 26, 27 | Needs 12 for 24, 26, 27 |
| 3: models, monitoring, serving | 25, 28, 29, 30, 31, 32, 33, 35, 36, 37, 38, 39, 40, 41 | Needs the champion from 26 and the verifier from 34 |
| 4: self-healing and demo | 42, 43, 44, 45, 46, 47, 48, 49, 50 | Needs matured-label metrics (39) and a recorded regime shift (46) |


=== docs\design\model-slot-and-live-wiring-design.md ===
# Design: model slot (Part A) and Kafka-ready live wiring

Date: 2026-11-27. Status: awaiting approval.

## Intent

SentinalOps watches Binance's public BTCUSDT order book and trades and, once a second, predicts the chance that liquidity stress starts in the next 30 seconds. The owner wants:

1. **The system frozen, the model swappable.** A model upgrade is "add a model package, change one config line, train". No pipeline, serving, streaming or heal code changes.
2. **The heal loop improves the installed model**, retraining the same model type on fresh data, never swapping types.
3. **The system running live 24/7**, on an Oracle Cloud Free Tier VM.
4. **Kafka-ready:** runs today without a broker, switches to Redpanda by one setting, with no rework.

## Goals, non-goals, anti-goals

- **Goals:** a model contract and registry of recipes; live service from Binance to ledger and heal loop; an event-bus interface with in-memory and Redpanda implementations; deployable on an Oracle Ampere A1 (ARM) VM.
- **Non-goals (later chunks):** new model candidates and the evaluation harness (Part B), Feast, MLflow registry, Grafana dashboards, alert rules, the replay simulator.
- **Anti-goals:** any change to feature, label, pipeline or verifier logic; a live path whose features differ from the offline ones; a heal cycle that silently changes model type; losing recorded data.

## Constraints

- Oracle Always Free (checked 2026-11-27 on docs.oracle.com): Ampere A1 = **2 OCPUs and 12 GB RAM total**, 200 GB block storage, 10 TB/month outbound. All dependencies have ARM64 builds (numpy, pandas, scikit-learn, pyarrow, LightGBM 4.7.0, Redpanda image).
- Oracle reclaims an Always Free VM as idle if, over 7 days, 95th-percentile CPU < 20% **and** network < 20% **and** memory < 20% (A1). Our CPU and network load will be far below 20%, so memory use (Redpanda's reservation) or a Pay As You Go upgrade is what keeps the VM.
- The IITM server keeps recording (no Docker there). Training on months of data can happen there; the Oracle VM runs the live system.

## Approaches considered

| | Approach | Trade-off |
|---|---|---|
| A | One direct process, no bus | Simplest; drops the Kafka layer |
| B | Redpanda from day one | Matches the diagram; can't run where Docker is unavailable |
| **C (chosen)** | Components talk through a small bus interface; in-memory bus now, Redpanda bus by setting | Runs anywhere today, Kafka story kept, no rework |

Chosen by the owner: **C**.

## Part A: the model slot

In short:

- `src/sentinalops/models/`: `base.py` (contract), `__init__.py` (`get_recipe(name)`, `list_recipes()`), `gbm_v1/` (today's HistGradientBoosting, unchanged behaviour).
- Fitted models keep the existing shape (`predict_proba -> (n, 2)`, `feature_names_in_`, writable `sentinal_version_`) and add `sentinal_model_name_` and `sentinal_model_params_`, so serving, the stream processor, the registry and the gates need no changes.
- `model:` block in the YAML and `DomainConfig` (default `gbm_v1`), used for manual training only.
- `heal/retrain.py::train_challenger` takes the champion and retrains with **its** recipe; the config is only a fallback for models without metadata. `heal/controller.py::_retrain` passes the champion in.
- `python -m sentinalops.training.train --model <name>`; `train_baseline` stays as a thin wrapper; `scripts/entrypoint.sh` calls the generic command.
- `docs/models.md`: the contract, the frozen-package rule, the 3-step upgrade.

## Live wiring

### Topology

```
BinanceFeed â”€â”€raw_trades / raw_depthâ”€â”€â–º Bus â”€â”¬â”€â–º FeaturesWorker (StreamProcessor) â”€â”€features / predictionsâ”€â”€â–º Bus
(recorder code,                              â”œâ”€â–º OutcomesWorker (OutcomeVerifier) â”€â”€outcomesâ”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â–º Bus
 reconnect + rotation)                       â”‚
                                             â””â”€ LedgerWriter â—„â”€â”€ predictions, outcomes   (SQLite ledger)
HealScheduler: every 5 min -> HealController.tick(now)  (reads the ledger, may promote -> champion.joblib hot-reloads)
Clock: every 1 s -> processor.advance(now), verifier.advance(now)  (ticks keep flowing when the feed is silent)
```

- **Bus interface** (`src/sentinalops/live/bus.py`): `publish(topic, key, value)` and `subscribe(topics) -> async iterator`. Topics: `raw_trades`, `raw_depth`, `features`, `predictions`, `outcomes` (names from chunk 15).
  - `InMemoryBus`: asyncio queues, one per subscriber, bounded (default 10,000 messages, about 8 minutes of feed). On overflow it **drops the oldest** message, counts it per topic and logs a warning. A full queue means a consumer is far behind, and the newest data is what matters for a live forecast. It should never happen at about 20 messages a second.
  - **Clock injection:** the live runner takes a `now_ms()` function. Live it is wall time; replay tests drive the processor and verifier in event time and finish instantly. The processor and verifier already take explicit times, so only the runner needs this.
  - `KafkaBus`: aiokafka producer and consumers, JSON values, key = symbol. Optional dependency (`[stream]` extra). Unit tests use the in-memory bus; one integration test runs only when `KAFKA_BROKER` is set.
- **BinanceFeed:** reuses the recorder's proven connection supervisor (reconnects, stale-feed watchdog, 23-hour rotation, de-duplication). The one change to recorder code: an optional `on_row(kind, row)` callback in `Recorder.handle`, called after de-duplication. With no callback, behaviour is identical. By default the live service does **not** write Parquet; `--record` turns that on so the Oracle VM can also be a backup recorder.
- **Workers** are thin async loops around the existing `StreamProcessor` and `OutcomeVerifier`. No feature or label logic changes, so the existing online/offline consistency and verifier replay tests keep guarding them.
- **Ledger:** the existing SQLite ledger. It already opens each connection with a 30 s busy timeout, indexes `(symbol, ts_ms)`, and writes idempotently (`INSERT OR IGNORE` on `prediction_id` and on the outcome key `(symbol, ts_ms, horizon_s)`). Predictions and outcomes are separate tables joined at read time, so an outcome arriving before its prediction (possible with Kafka) is harmless. Changes:
  - turn on WAL with `synchronous=NORMAL`, so readers never block the writer and two writers wait instead of failing;
  - live predictions get a **deterministic** `prediction_id` (from symbol, tick and model version) instead of a random UUID, so a restarted or replayed features worker can't double-count a tick;
  - **no deletion:** the ledger is append-only by design (database triggers forbid deletes; it is the audit trail of every forecast). Growth is about 90 MB/day, with features stored, and every reader is bounded by time instead. Monthly ledger file rotation is a later option if size ever matters.
- **Heal runs in its own process, always.** Retraining can take seconds to minutes and would freeze the single-process event loop: missed WebSocket pings, a stalled clock, full queues. The heal scheduler is therefore a separate process even in in-memory mode. It needs no bus: it only reads the ledger and writes the file registry, and the live process hot-reloads `champion.joblib` as it does today. Training is limited to one thread (`OMP_NUM_THREADS=1`, `OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1`, `MKL_NUM_THREADS=1`, run under `nice`), leaving the second Oracle CPU to the live feed. The heal process writes its own JSON health line every 60 s (last decision and its reason, last action, champion version, matured labels, live AP), and every action is also an event in `registry.json`.
- **Heal training table from the ledger:** every 5 minutes, `HealController.tick(now)`. Its table comes from the **ledger** (each matured prediction stores its features and outcome), current to the last 30 s; the offline pipeline leaves out the newest hour and would leave the holdout empty. The reader:
  - keeps only matured, non-void rows (`label IS NOT NULL`), so outcomes voided as `already_stressed` (the pipeline's `not_calm`), `gap` or `warmup` never train;
  - keeps one row per tick, deterministically (the earliest-scored prediction; champion and shadow saw identical features);
  - reads only `retrain_window_hours` plus a bounded replay lookback (default 7 days).
  - Warm-up is already guarded: `HealController.decide` does nothing without a champion, with fewer than `min_positives` stress events in the window, or fewer than `min_matured_labels` (2,000) outcomes since promotion; `train_challenger` refuses a window without both classes.
- **Process modes:** always at least two processes.
  - `python -m sentinalops.live` (default `--role pipeline`) runs feed, features, outcomes and ledger writer in one process on the in-memory bus.
  - `python -m sentinalops.live --role heal` runs the heal scheduler.
  - With `--bus kafka`, each pipeline component can also run alone (`--role feed|features|outcomes|ledger`).
- **Health (live process):** a JSON health line every 60 s with events in, ticks, suspended ticks, predictions, outcomes, late events, queue drops and the served model version. For heal status it reads the last heal event from `registry.json`, read-only; the heal process writes its own line too (above). There is also an optional Prometheus port bound to localhost.

### Deployment on Oracle

- One A1 VM, 2 OCPU / 12 GB, Ubuntu 24.04 ARM, 150â€“200 GB boot volume, home region in India (Mumbai or Hyderabad; Binance is reachable from India, as the IITM server shows, and blocks US locations).
- `deploy/oracle/`: a setup script (Python venv; **two systemd units**, `sentinalops-live` and `sentinalops-heal`, both restart on failure and start at boot; optional Docker plus Redpanda) and a `monitor.sh` like the recorder's that shows both.
- Memory budget: live service about 0.5 GB, recorder 0.1 GB, API 0.2 GB, plus Redpanda. Without Redpanda the VM sits near 7% memory use, below Oracle's 20% idle line (2.4 GB). **Idle-reclamation protection, in order of preference:**
  1. upgrade the account to Pay As You Go (Always Free resources stay $0; the owner decides, because it needs a card);
  2. otherwise run Redpanda with `--memory 3G`, which keeps the VM above 20% and is useful anyway.
  The setup script prints which protection is active.
- **Pre-flight check:** before installing services, the setup script opens a 10 s WebSocket connection to Binance from the VM and must receive both depth and trade messages; otherwise it stops with a clear message. Cloud IP ranges can be treated differently from university networks.

## Testing

- All existing tests pass; `ruff` clean.
- **Part A:** every recipe loads through `ChampionModel`; a config-selection test; heal retrains the champion's type even when the YAML names another.
- **Live wiring:**
  - End-to-end replay through the in-memory bus (synthetic recording -> feed -> features -> predictions -> outcomes -> ledger), with features equal to the offline `compute_ticks` and outcomes equal to `build_labels`.
  - The silent-feed clock produces suspended ticks.
  - Heal fires from ledger data.
  - `KafkaBus` passes the same bus contract tests (integration, broker required).
- A 5-minute run against live Binance from a laptop, and later on the Oracle VM.

## Review notes (external review, 2026-11-27)

Accepted: heal in its own process; WAL plus bounded readers; void filtering and deterministic de-duplication in the ledger reader; deterministic prediction IDs; drop-oldest overflow; clock injection; reclamation protection; Binance pre-flight check.

Declined:
- **Pruning ledger rows:** the ledger is append-only on purpose; readers are bounded instead.
- **Adding `model_role` and `label_status` columns:** the role is already identified by `model_version` (the registry knows which version is champion or shadow), and `void_reason` already carries `label_status`. Adding columns would be a migration on existing ledgers for no new information.

## Left to the builder

Exact module names, bus queue sizes, the heal interval default (5 min, configurable), and the health-line fields.


=== docs\design\model-slot-and-live-wiring-plan.md ===
# Model Slot and Kafka-Ready Live Wiring Implementation Plan


**Goal:** Make the model a plug-in that the heal loop retrains by its own recipe, and run the system live from Binance to the ledger and heal loop over a swappable bus.

**Architecture:** Part A adds `sentinalops.models` (recipe registry and contract) and routes training and healing through it without changing serving, streaming or registry code. Part B adds `sentinalops.live`: a `Bus` interface (in-memory, and Kafka via aiokafka), a feed that reuses the recorder's connection supervisor through one optional callback, thin async workers around the existing `StreamProcessor` / `OutcomeVerifier` / `Ledger`, and a separate heal process that trains from the ledger.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.11+, asyncio, websockets, pandas, scikit-learn, SQLite, aiokafka (optional), systemd on Ubuntu 24.04 ARM (Oracle A1).

**Spec:** `docs/design/model-slot-and-live-wiring-design.md`.

## Global Constraints

- `requires-python >=3.11`; ruff line length 110; `ruff check src tests` and the full `pytest` must pass after every task.
- No change to feature, label, pipeline or verifier logic (`features/`, `labels.py`, `data/pipeline.py`, `stream/verifier.py`, the body of `stream/processor.py`).
- The only recorder change is an optional hook; with defaults, recorder behaviour and output are byte-identical.
- Fitted models keep `predict_proba -> (n, 2)`, `feature_names_in_ == cfg.feature_names`, writable `sentinal_version_`; new attributes are `sentinal_model_name_`, `sentinal_model_params_`, `sentinal_holdout_ap_`.
- Default `model.name` is `gbm_v1` = `HistGradientBoostingClassifier(max_iter=200, learning_rate=0.08, random_state=seed)` on `cfg.feature_names` (today's behaviour).
- Topics: `raw_trades`, `raw_depth`, `features`, `predictions`, `outcomes`. In-memory queue default 10,000 per subscriber, drop-oldest on overflow.
- Heal: separate process, every 5 min (configurable), `OMP_NUM_THREADS=OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=MKL_NUM_THREADS=1`, ledger reader bounded to `retrain_window_hours` + 7 days.
- Ledger stays append-only; WAL + `synchronous=NORMAL`.

## Review Focus

1. Binance feed goes silent or disconnects: ticks are suspended (no predictions), the verifier voids horizons with `gap`, and nothing crashes. Test in Task 7.
2. No model file at startup: the live pipeline keeps producing feature ticks, counts scoring errors, and starts predicting as soon as a champion appears. Test in Task 7.
3. Heal process on an empty or new ledger: logs `no_champion` / `too_few_events` and idles. Test in Task 9.
4. The live process restarts mid-day: re-scored ticks don't duplicate ledger rows (deterministic `prediction_id`). Test in Task 6.
5. Champion trained before this refactor (no `sentinal_model_name_`): heal falls back to `cfg.model.name` instead of failing. Test in Task 3.

---

## Part A: the model slot

### Task 1: Recipe contract, registry, and `gbm_v1`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sentinalops/models/__init__.py`, `src/sentinalops/models/base.py`, `src/sentinalops/models/gbm_v1/__init__.py`
- Modify: `src/sentinalops/training/train_baseline.py` (`fit` delegates to `gbm_v1`)
- Test: `tests/test_models.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `base.Recipe` (Protocol): `name: str`; `fit(self, train: pd.DataFrame, cfg: DomainConfig, seed: int = 0, params: dict | None = None) -> object`.
  - `base.stamp(model, name: str, params: dict) -> model` sets `sentinal_model_name_` and `sentinal_model_params_`.
  - `base.check_fitted(model, cfg: DomainConfig) -> None` raises `ValueError` unless the model has `predict_proba`, `feature_names_in_ == cfg.feature_names` and `classes_ == [0, 1]`.
  - `models.get_recipe(name: str) -> Recipe` (`KeyError` listing the known names), `models.list_recipes() -> list[str]`, `models.register_recipe(name: str, import_path: str) -> None`. The registry maps names to `"module:attr"` strings, imported lazily so listing is cheap.
  - `models.model_name(model) -> str | None` reads `sentinal_model_name_`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests:** `test_gbm_v1_is_default_and_loads_in_champion_model` (fit on `make_ticks("calm", 4000)`, `check_fitted` passes, dump with joblib, `ChampionModel(path, BASE.feature_names).predict(row)` returns a probability in [0, 1]); `test_gbm_v1_matches_old_baseline` (same seed â†’ `predict_proba` equal to `HistGradientBoostingClassifier(max_iter=200, learning_rate=0.08, random_state=0)` on the same data); `test_unknown_recipe_lists_known` (`KeyError` message contains `gbm_v1`); `test_stamp_survives_pickle`.
- [ ] **Step 2:** Run `pytest tests/test_models.py -v`. Expected: FAIL (module missing).
- [ ] **Step 3:** Implement the files. `gbm_v1.Recipe.fit` uses only `cfg.feature_names` and `label.astype(int)`, accepts `params` overriding `max_iter`, `learning_rate` and the other HistGradientBoosting arguments, and returns the stamped estimator itself, so pickles stay plain sklearn. `train_baseline.fit(df, seed)` calls `get_recipe("gbm_v1").fit(df, get_domain_config(), seed)`.
- [ ] **Step 4:** Run `pytest -q && ruff check src tests`. Expected: all pass.
- [ ] **Step 5:** Commit: `Model slot: recipe contract, registry and gbm_v1`.

### Task 2: `model:` block in the config

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/sentinalops/config.py` (add `ModelConfig`, field `model` on `DomainConfig`), `configs/domain_liquidity.yaml`
- Test: `tests/test_config.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `ModelConfig(_Strict)`: `name: str = "gbm_v1"`, `params: dict[str, Any] = {}`; `DomainConfig.model: ModelConfig`. A validator rejects names not in `models.list_recipes()`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests:** `test_model_block_defaults_to_gbm_v1`; `test_unknown_model_name_rejected` (`ValidationError` mentions the name); `test_yaml_model_block_loads` (YAML has `model: {name: gbm_v1, params: {}}`).
- [ ] **Step 2:** Run `pytest tests/test_config.py -v`. Expected: the new tests FAIL.
- [ ] **Step 3:** Implement. Add to the YAML, after `alert_threshold`, a commented block: `model:` / `name: gbm_v1   # a package in src/sentinalops/models; manual training only, heal follows the champion` / `params: {}`.
- [ ] **Step 4:** Run `pytest -q && ruff check src tests`. Expected: pass.
- [ ] **Step 5:** Commit: `Config: model block selects the recipe for manual training`.

### Task 3: Heal retrains the champion's own recipe

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/sentinalops/heal/retrain.py` (`train_challenger`, new `resolve_recipe`), `src/sentinalops/heal/controller.py` (`_retrain`: load the champion once, pass it; add `model_name` and `params` to the registered meta)
- Test: `tests/test_heal.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `get_recipe`, `model_name`, `register_recipe`.
- Produces:
  - `resolve_recipe(champion, cfg) -> tuple[str, dict]`: the champion's `sentinal_model_name_` and `sentinal_model_params_` if present, else `(cfg.model.name, cfg.model.params)`.
  - `train_challenger(ts: TrainingSet, cfg: DomainConfig, seed: int = 0, champion=None)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests:**
  - `test_heal_retrains_champion_recipe_not_config`: register a test recipe `logit_test` with `monkeypatch` on the registry dict, so it never leaks into `list_recipes()` (a `LogisticRegression` recipe defined in the test module), stamp the champion as `logit_test`, use a config whose `model.name` is `gbm_v1`, and run `train_challenger(ts, cfg, champion=champ)`. Assert `model_name(result) == "logit_test"`.
  - `test_unstamped_champion_falls_back_to_config`: a plain HistGradientBoosting champion (no attribute) gives `resolve_recipe(...) == ("gbm_v1", {})`.
  - `test_registered_heal_version_records_model_name`: after the main heal story, `registry.meta("v002")["model_name"] == "gbm_v1"`.

### Task 6: Bus, ledger WAL, deterministic IDs, ledger training table

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sentinalops/live/__init__.py`, `src/sentinalops/live/bus.py`, `src/sentinalops/heal/ledger_table.py`
- Modify: `src/sentinalops/serving/ledger.py` (WAL at init; `PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL` in `_connect`)
- Test: `tests/test_live_bus.py`, `tests/test_verifier_ledger.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `bus.TOPICS`; `bus.TOPIC_OF_KIND = {AGG_TRADE: "raw_trades", DEPTH5: "raw_depth"}` and its inverse `KIND_OF_TOPIC`.
  - `class Bus(Protocol)`: `async publish(topic: str, key: str, value: dict) -> None`; `subscribe(topics: list[str], group: str) -> AsyncIterator[tuple[str, dict]]`; `async close() -> None`.
  - `InMemoryBus(maxsize: int = 10_000)`: every subscriber gets every message on its topics, in publish order; `drops: dict[str, int]`; `close()` ends all iterators after draining.
  - `bus.stable_prediction_id(symbol: str, ts_ms: int, model_version: str) -> str`: sha1 hex of `f"{symbol}|{ts_ms}|{model_version}"`, first 32 characters.
  - `ledger_table(ledger: Ledger, since_ms: int, until_ms: int, feature_names: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame`: columns `symbol, ts_ms, <features>, label, label_status="ok", regime="untagged", source="ledger"`. It keeps only matured rows with `label IS NOT NULL`, one row per `(symbol, ts_ms)` (the earliest `scored_at_ms`), sorted by `ts_ms`, and drops rows without features.
- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests:**
  - `test_bus_fanout_and_order`;
  - `test_bus_overflow_drops_oldest` (`maxsize=3`, publish 5, the subscriber gets the last 3, `drops["raw_depth"] == 2`);
  - `test_bus_close_ends_subscription`;
  - `test_stable_prediction_id_deterministic`;
  - `test_ledger_wal_enabled` (`PRAGMA journal_mode` returns `wal`);
  - `test_ledger_table_filters_void_and_dedupes_shadow` (two versions per tick, one void outcome; the result has one row per non-void tick with the earlier-scored features).
- [ ] **Step 2:** Run `pytest tests/test_live_bus.py tests/test_verifier_ledger.py -v`. Expected: the new tests FAIL.
- [ ] **Step 3:** Implement. `ledger_table` does a single bounded SQL query on the existing join (`_JOINED`), then `json.loads` the features.
- [ ] **Step 4:** Run `pytest -q && ruff check src tests`. Expected: pass.
- [ ] **Step 5:** Commit: `Live: bus interface, in-memory bus, ledger WAL and ledger training table`.

### Task 7: Pipeline workers with clock injection

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sentinalops/live/pipeline.py`
- Test: `tests/test_live_pipeline.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6 bus and `stable_prediction_id`; the existing `StreamProcessor(cfg, model, shadow=...)`, `OutcomeVerifier(cfg)` and `Ledger`.
- Produces:
  - `features_worker(bus, processor) -> Coroutine`: consumes raw topics and publishes `features` and `predictions`. Every prediction is re-issued with `prediction_id=stable_prediction_id(...)` via `model_copy`, and the `predictions` message value is `{**pred.model_dump(mode="json"), "features": <that tick's feature dict>}`. This is self-contained, so there is no cross-topic join (Kafka doesn't order across topics).
  - `outcomes_worker(bus, verifier) -> Coroutine`: consumes `raw_depth`, publishes `outcomes`.
  - `ledger_writer(bus, ledger) -> Coroutine`: consumes only `predictions` (features taken from the message) and `outcomes`. It keeps no cache.
  - `InMemoryBus.drain() -> Coroutine` (added to Task 6): waits until every subscriber queue is empty, for deterministic tests.
  - `clock_loop(bus, processor, verifier, now_ms: Callable[[], int], interval_s: float = 1.0) -> Coroutine`: calls `advance(now_ms())` on both and publishes their outputs.
  - `PipelineStats` (dataclass): events per topic, ticks, suspended, predictions, outcomes, scoring errors, drops.
- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests:**
  - `test_replay_matches_offline`: `make_events(H0, 1800, stress=((900, 40),), holes=((1300, 10),))`. Drive time deterministically: for each second, publish that second's events in `recv_time_ms` order and `await bus.drain()`. Only then call the clock step (`clock_step(processor, verifier, t)`, the body of `clock_loop`, exposed for tests). No concurrent `clock_loop` in this test. Assert the ledger features for every tick equal `compute_ticks` to 1e-9, every outcome label equals `build_labels`, and some outcomes are void with `gap`.
  - `test_silent_feed_suspends_ticks`: with no events, advancing the fake clock 10 s produces 0 predictions and counts suspended ticks.
  - `test_no_model_then_model_appears`: start with a missing champion path, and features still flow. Write a model to the path, and predictions start with that model's version.
  - `test_restart_does_not_duplicate_predictions`: replaying the same 120 s twice into one ledger gives the same prediction row count.
- [ ] **Step 2:** Run `pytest tests/test_live_pipeline.py -v`. Expected: FAIL.
- [ ] **Step 3:** Implement. Ledger writes run in `asyncio.to_thread`, so SQLite never blocks the loop.
- [ ] **Step 4:** Run `pytest -q && ruff check src tests`. Expected: pass.
- [ ] **Step 5:** Commit: `Live: feature, outcome, ledger and clock workers`.

=== scripts\bench_pipeline.py ===
"""Speed check for the offline pipeline (task 12.6): build one synthetic recorded day and time it.

    python scripts/bench_pipeline.py [--hours 24] [--trades-per-s 20] [--dir /tmp/bench]

Depth arrives every 100 ms like ``depth5@100ms``; trades at a busy-day BTCUSDT rate.
Target: one day in under 10 minutes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
import tempfile
import time
from pathlib import Path

import pyarrow as pa
import pyarrow.parquet as pq

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path[:0] = [str(ROOT / "src"), str(ROOT / "tests")]

from pipeline_helpers import H0, make_events  # noqa: E402
from sentinalops.config import get_domain_config  # noqa: E402
from sentinalops.data import pipeline as P  # noqa: E402
from sentinalops.data.recorder.messages import AGG_TRADE, DEPTH5, SCHEMAS  # noqa: E402
from sentinalops.data.recorder.sink import partition_of  # noqa: E402


def write_hourly(root: Path, kind: str, df) -> None:
    hours = df["recv_time_ms"] // P.HOUR_MS
    for h, part in df.groupby(hours, sort=True):
        _, symbol, date, hour = partition_of(kind, "BTCUSDT", int(h) * P.HOUR_MS)
        path = root / f"stream={kind}" / f"symbol={symbol}" / f"date={date}" / f"hour={hour}"
        path = path / "part-bench.parquet"
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        pq.write_table(pa.Table.from_pandas(part, schema=SCHEMAS[kind], preserve_index=False), path)


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--hours", type=int, default=24)
    ap.add_argument("--trades-per-s", type=float, default=20.0)
    ap.add_argument("--dir", default=None)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    base = Path(args.dir or tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="bench-"))
    raw, out = base / "raw", base / "out"

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    stress = tuple((s, 20) for s in range(1800, args.hours * 3600, 1800))
    depth, trades = make_events(H0, args.hours * 3600, snapshot_ms=100, trades_per_s=args.trades_per_s,
                                stress=stress)
    write_hourly(raw, DEPTH5, depth)
    write_hourly(raw, AGG_TRADE, trades)
    print(f"generated {len(depth):,} snapshots and {len(trades):,} trades in {time.perf_counter() - t0:.1f}s")

    s = P.run(raw, out, get_domain_config(ROOT / "configs" / "domain_liquidity.yaml"), include_last=True)
    print(P.format_summary(s))
    per_day = s.seconds * 24 / args.hours
    print(f"pipeline: {s.seconds:.1f}s for {args.hours} h -> {per_day:.1f}s per day "
          f"({'PASS' if per_day < 600 else 'FAIL'}: target < 600 s)")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== scripts\entrypoint.sh ===
#!/bin/sh
# Train a baseline champion on first boot so the API is always servable. Also retrain when the
# saved model expects different inputs than the configured features (e.g. a pre-chunk-2 model).
set -e
MODEL="${MODEL_PATH:-models/champion.joblib}"
if ! python - "$MODEL" <<'EOF'
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import joblib

from sentinalops.config import Settings, get_domain_config

path = Path(sys.argv[1])
if not path.exists():
    sys.exit(f"no champion at {path}")
names = list(getattr(joblib.load(path), "feature_names_in_", []))
if names != get_domain_config(Settings.from_env().domain_config).feature_names:
    sys.exit(f"champion at {path} does not match the configured features")
EOF
then
  echo "training the configured model as the first champion"
  python -m sentinalops.training.train --out "$MODEL"
fi
exec "$@"


=== scripts\smoke.py ===
"""End-to-end smoke test against a running stack: python scripts/smoke.py [base_url]."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import time
import urllib.request

from sentinalops.simulator.regimes import example_ticks


def call(base: str, path: str, body: dict | None = None):
    req = urllib.request.Request(
        base + path,
        data=json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else None,
        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
    )
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as r:
        return json.loads(r.read())


def wait_healthy(base: str, tries: int = 30) -> None:
    for _ in range(tries):
        try:
            if call(base, "/health")["status"] == "ok":
                return
        except OSError:
            time.sleep(2)
    sys.exit("API never became healthy")


def main() -> None:
    base = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "http://localhost:8000"
    wait_healthy(base)
    ts_ms = int(time.time() * 1000)
    calm, stressed = example_ticks()
    for i, (label, features) in enumerate((("calm", calm), ("stressed", stressed))):
        body = {"symbol": "BTCUSDT", "ts_ms": ts_ms + i * 1000, "features": features}
        r = call(base, "/score", body)
        print(f"{label:9s} probability={r['probability']:.3f} alert={r['alert']}")
    outcome = {"symbol": "BTCUSDT", "ts_ms": ts_ms + 1000, "horizon_s": 30, "label": 1}
    print("outcome", call(base, "/outcomes", outcome))
    print("smoke OK")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== src\sentinalops\config.py ===
"""Typed configuration: the domain YAML plus environment settings.

``DomainConfig`` is the single typed view of ``configs/domain_liquidity.yaml``. Unknown keys
are rejected, so a typo in the YAML fails loudly instead of silently using a default. The
label and feature modules get their parameters from here (``label_params``,
``feature_params``), so training, serving and the outcome verifier all read one file.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Literal

import yaml
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, model_validator

if TYPE_CHECKING:  # imported lazily at runtime to keep config import cheap
    from sentinalops.features.microstructure import FeatureParams
    from sentinalops.labels import LabelParams

DEFAULT_DOMAIN_CONFIG = Path("configs/domain_liquidity.yaml")


class _Strict(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)


class Sources(_Strict):
    offline: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    live: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    delayed_labels: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


class LabelConfig(_Strict):
    """The stress-label block. See ``docs/labels.md`` for the definition."""

    primary: Literal["depth_depletion"] = "depth_depletion"
    depth_fraction: float = Field(0.5, gt=0, lt=1)
    secondary: Literal["spread_expansion"] | None = "spread_expansion"
    spread_multiple: float = Field(3.0, gt=1)
    reference_window_seconds: int = Field(600, ge=1)
    min_reference_seconds: int = Field(300, ge=1)
    confirm_ticks: int = Field(2, ge=1)
    onset_only: Literal[True] = True  # persistence labels are not implemented, on purpose

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _reference_fits(self) -> LabelConfig:
        if self.min_reference_seconds > self.reference_window_seconds:
            raise ValueError("min_reference_seconds cannot exceed reference_window_seconds")
        return self


class ModelConfig(_Strict):
    """Which model package to train by hand (``python -m sentinalops.training.train``).

    The heal loop does not read this: it retrains the champion with the champion's own recipe.
    """

    name: str = "gbm_v1"
    params: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _known(self) -> ModelConfig:
        from sentinalops.models import list_recipes

        if self.name not in list_recipes():
            raise ValueError(f"unknown model {self.name!r}; known models: {list_recipes()}")
        return self


class HealConfig(_Strict):
    """The self-healing loop: when to retrain, how, and what a challenger must prove."""

    # monitoring (chunk 39)
    metrics_window_minutes: int = Field(60, ge=1)  # rolling window for live scores
    min_positives: int = Field(20, ge=1)  # fewer stress events in the window -> no verdict
    degradation_margin: float = Field(0.15, gt=0, lt=1)  # AP drop vs the champion's reference
    # trigger (chunk 42)
    min_matured_labels: int = Field(2000, ge=1)  # since the champion was promoted
    cooldown_minutes: int = Field(60, ge=0)
    # retraining (chunk 43)
    replay_fraction: float = Field(0.3, ge=0, le=2)  # older rows added, relative to recent rows
    holdout_fraction: float = Field(0.2, gt=0, lt=1)  # newest slice of the window, for the gates
    # gates (chunk 44)
    min_gain: float = Field(0.05, ge=0)  # challenger AP - champion AP on the current regime
    max_regression: float = Field(0.10, ge=0)  # allowed AP loss on older regimes
    min_lift_over_baseline: float = Field(1.5, ge=1)  # challenger AP / base rate
    max_brier_increase: float = Field(0.01, ge=0)
    # promotion (chunk 45)
    shadow_minutes: int = Field(30, ge=0)
    rollback_margin: float = Field(0.05, ge=0)  # new champion worse than the old by this -> rollback


class DomainConfig(_Strict):
    """Everything that defines the domain lives in one YAML file."""

    domain: str
    thesis: str = ""
    sources: Sources = Field(default_factory=Sources)
    features_module: str
    drift_watch: list[str] = Field(min_length=1)
    drift_threshold: float = Field(0.3, ge=0, le=1)
    retrain_window_hours: int = Field(6, gt=0)
    promotion_metric: Literal["avg_precision", "brier", "roc_auc"] = "avg_precision"
    alert_threshold: float = Field(0.5, ge=0, le=1)
    model: ModelConfig = Field(default_factory=ModelConfig)

    symbol: str = Field("BTCUSDT", pattern=r"^[A-Z0-9]{5,20}$")
    prediction_cadence_seconds: int = Field(1, ge=1, le=60)
    label_horizon_seconds: int = Field(30, ge=1, le=3600)
    feature_windows_seconds: tuple[int, ...] = Field((1, 5, 30, 60), min_length=1)
    label: LabelConfig = Field(default_factory=LabelConfig)
    split_embargo_seconds: int = Field(60, ge=0)
    stale_data_seconds: float = Field(3.0, gt=0, le=60)
    heal: HealConfig = Field(default_factory=HealConfig)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _consistent(self) -> DomainConfig:
        cadence = self.prediction_cadence_seconds
        windows = self.feature_windows_seconds
        if len(set(windows)) != len(windows) or list(windows) != sorted(windows) or windows[0] < 1:
            raise ValueError("feature_windows_seconds must be positive, unique and ascending")
        if self.label_horizon_seconds % cadence or self.label.reference_window_seconds % cadence:
            raise ValueError("label horizon and reference window must be multiples of the cadence")
        if self.split_embargo_seconds < self.label_horizon_seconds:
            raise ValueError("split_embargo_seconds must be at least label_horizon_seconds")
        unknown = sorted(set(self.drift_watch) - set(self.feature_names))
        if unknown:
            raise ValueError(f"drift_watch names unknown features: {unknown}")
        return self

    @property
    def feature_names(self) -> list[str]:
        from sentinalops.features.microstructure import feature_names

        return feature_names(self.feature_windows_seconds)

    @property
    def stale_ms(self) -> int:
        return int(round(self.stale_data_seconds * 1000))

    def feature_params(self) -> FeatureParams:
        from sentinalops.features.microstructure import FeatureParams

        return FeatureParams(
            windows_s=self.feature_windows_seconds,
            cadence_s=self.prediction_cadence_seconds,
            stale_ms=self.stale_ms,
        )

    def label_params(self) -> LabelParams:
        from sentinalops.labels import LabelParams

        lab = self.label
        return LabelParams(
            cadence_s=self.prediction_cadence_seconds,
            horizon_s=self.label_horizon_seconds,
            reference_window_s=lab.reference_window_seconds,
            min_reference_ticks=lab.min_reference_seconds // self.prediction_cadence_seconds,
            depth_fraction=lab.depth_fraction,
            spread_multiple=lab.spread_multiple,
            use_spread=lab.secondary is not None,
            confirm_ticks=lab.confirm_ticks,
            stale_ms=self.stale_ms,
        )


class Settings(BaseModel):
    """Runtime settings, read from environment variables."""

    model_path: Path = Path("models/champion.joblib")
    data_dir: Path = Path("data")
    domain_config: Path = DEFAULT_DOMAIN_CONFIG
    log_level: str = "INFO"
    alert_threshold: float | None = Field(None, ge=0, le=1)  # overrides the domain config
    kafka_broker: str = "localhost:9092"
    mlflow_tracking_uri: str = "http://localhost:5000"
    redis_url: str = "redis://localhost:6379"

    @classmethod
    def from_env(cls, env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> Settings:
        e = os.environ if env is None else env
        raw = {
            "model_path": e.get("MODEL_PATH"),
            "data_dir": e.get("DATA_DIR"),
            "domain_config": e.get("DOMAIN_CONFIG"),
            "log_level": e.get("LOG_LEVEL"),
            "alert_threshold": e.get("ALERT_THRESHOLD"),
            "kafka_broker": e.get("KAFKA_BROKER"),
            "mlflow_tracking_uri": e.get("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"),
            "redis_url": e.get("REDIS_URL"),
        }
        return cls(**{k: v for k, v in raw.items() if v not in (None, "")})

    def effective_threshold(self, cfg: DomainConfig) -> float:
        return cfg.alert_threshold if self.alert_threshold is None else self.alert_threshold


def load_domain_config(path: str | Path) -> DomainConfig:
    with Path(path).open(encoding="utf-8") as f:
        raw = yaml.safe_load(f)
    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected a mapping at the top level")
    return DomainConfig(**raw)


@lru_cache(maxsize=8)
def get_domain_config(path: str | Path = DEFAULT_DOMAIN_CONFIG) -> DomainConfig:
    return load_domain_config(path)


=== src\sentinalops\labels.py ===
"""Liquidity-stress labels (chunk 9): the single definition of what the model predicts.

Shared by the offline pipeline (chunk 12) and the outcome verifier (chunk 34), so both
always agree. The pipeline, on a regular one-second grid:

1. ``book_state``   depth snapshots -> mid, relative spread, top-5 depth
2. ``to_ticks``     as-of sample once per second; a tick is unknown if the book is stale
3. ``add_stress``   trailing reference (rolling median of *earlier* ticks), then
                    ``stressed`` and ``confirmed`` (stressed for ``confirm_ticks`` in a row)
4. ``add_labels``   for a calm tick t: label = 1 if confirmed stress occurs in (t, t+H]

Causality rules, each enforced by a test:

- Everything from steps 1-3 at tick t uses only data at or before t.
- The label at t uses only ticks in (t, t+H], so features and labels never overlap.
- Ticks already in stress are not eligible (we forecast onset, not persistence).
- If any tick in the horizon is unknown (feed gap, warm-up, end of data), the label is
  void, never guessed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, replace
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

DEPTH_LEVELS = 5
STATE_COLUMNS = ["mid", "rel_spread", "depth_top5"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LabelParams:
    cadence_s: int = 1
    horizon_s: int = 30
    reference_window_s: int = 600
    min_reference_ticks: int = 300  # ticks needed before the reference is trusted
    depth_fraction: float = 0.5  # stress if depth < fraction * reference depth (primary)
    spread_multiple: float = 3.0  # stress if spread > multiple * reference spread (secondary)
    use_spread: bool = True
    confirm_ticks: int = 2  # consecutive stressed ticks before stress counts
    stale_ms: int = 3000  # a tick is unknown if the newest snapshot is older than this

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if min(self.cadence_s, self.horizon_s, self.reference_window_s, self.confirm_ticks) < 1:
            raise ValueError("cadence, horizon, reference window and confirm_ticks must be >= 1")
        if not 0 < self.depth_fraction < 1 or self.spread_multiple <= 1:
            raise ValueError("need 0 < depth_fraction < 1 and spread_multiple > 1")
        if self.horizon_s % self.cadence_s or self.reference_window_s % self.cadence_s:
            raise ValueError("horizon and reference window must be multiples of the cadence")

    @property
    def horizon_ticks(self) -> int:
        return self.horizon_s // self.cadence_s

    @property
    def reference_ticks(self) -> int:
        return self.reference_window_s // self.cadence_s

    @classmethod
    def from_domain_yaml(cls, path: str | Path) -> LabelParams:
        from sentinalops.config import load_domain_config

        return load_domain_config(path).label_params()


# ---- 1. book state -----------------------------------------------------------------
def book_state(depth: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Per snapshot: mid, relative spread, top-5 depth. Drops crossed or empty books."""
    bid, ask = depth["bid_px_1"].to_numpy(float), depth["ask_px_1"].to_numpy(float)
    qty_cols = [f"{s}_qty_{i}" for s in ("bid", "ask") for i in range(1, DEPTH_LEVELS + 1)]
    total = depth[qty_cols].to_numpy(float).sum(axis=1)
    mid = (bid + ask) / 2
    out = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "recv_time_ms": depth["recv_time_ms"].to_numpy(np.int64),
            "mid": mid,
            "rel_spread": (ask - bid) / mid,
            "depth_top5": total,
        }
    )
    ok = (bid > 0) & (ask > bid) & (total > 0)
    return out[ok].sort_values("recv_time_ms", kind="stable").reset_index(drop=True)


# ---- 2. one-second ticks -------------------------------------------------------------
def to_ticks(state: pd.DataFrame, p: LabelParams = LabelParams()) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """As-of sample: tick t takes the newest snapshot with ``recv_time_ms <= t``.

    Returns a frame indexed by ``ts_ms`` on a gap-free grid. Ticks whose newest snapshot
    is older than ``stale_ms`` are NaN ("unknown").
    """
    step = p.cadence_s * 1000
    times = state["recv_time_ms"].to_numpy(np.int64)
    if len(times) == 0:
        return pd.DataFrame(
            columns=[*STATE_COLUMNS, "age_ms"], index=pd.Index([], name="ts_ms", dtype="int64")
        )
    grid = np.arange(-(-times[0] // step) * step, (times[-1] // step) * step + step, step, dtype=np.int64)
    idx = np.searchsorted(times, grid, side="right") - 1  # newest snapshot at or before each tick
    age = grid - times[idx]
    ticks = pd.DataFrame(
        {c: state[c].to_numpy(float)[idx] for c in STATE_COLUMNS}, index=pd.Index(grid, name="ts_ms")
    )
    ticks["age_ms"] = age
    ticks.loc[age > p.stale_ms, STATE_COLUMNS] = np.nan
    return ticks


# ---- 3. stress state (causal) ----------------------------------------------------------
def add_stress(ticks: pd.DataFrame, p: LabelParams = LabelParams()) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Add the trailing reference and the stress state. Uses only ticks at or before each row."""
    out = ticks.copy()
    roll = dict(window=p.reference_ticks, min_periods=min(p.min_reference_ticks, p.reference_ticks))
    # shift(1): the reference for tick t is built from ticks strictly before t
    out["ref_depth"] = out["depth_top5"].rolling(**roll).median().shift(1)
    out["ref_spread"] = out["rel_spread"].rolling(**roll).median().shift(1)
    out["depth_ratio"] = out["depth_top5"] / out["ref_depth"]
    out["spread_ratio"] = out["rel_spread"] / out["ref_spread"]

    known = out["depth_ratio"].notna() & out["spread_ratio"].notna()
    depth_hit = out["depth_ratio"] < p.depth_fraction
    spread_hit = (out["spread_ratio"] > p.spread_multiple) & p.use_spread
    out["stress_depth"] = depth_hit.astype(float).where(known)
    out["stress_spread"] = spread_hit.astype(float).where(known)
    out["stressed"] = (depth_hit | spread_hit).astype(float).where(known)
    # confirmed at t = stressed at each of the last `confirm_ticks` ticks (NaN if any is unknown)
    out["confirmed"] = out["stressed"].rolling(p.confirm_ticks, min_periods=p.confirm_ticks).min()
    return out


# ---- 4. forward labels -----------------------------------------------------------------
def _forward(series: pd.Series, horizon_ticks: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Rows j=1..H of the values at t+j, shape (H, N). NaN beyond the end of the data."""
    return np.stack([series.shift(-j).to_numpy(float) for j in range(1, horizon_ticks + 1)])


def add_labels(ticks: pd.DataFrame, p: LabelParams = LabelParams()) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Add the onset label and regression targets. Looks only at ticks in (t, t+H]."""
    out = ticks.copy()
    h = p.horizon_ticks
    fwd = _forward(out["confirmed"], h)
    horizon_known = ~np.isnan(fwd).any(axis=0)
    hit = (fwd == 1).any(axis=0)
    calm_now = (out["stressed"] == 0).to_numpy()

    out["eligible"] = calm_now & horizon_known
    out["label"] = np.where(out["eligible"], hit.astype(float), np.nan)
    first = (fwd == 1).argmax(axis=0) + 1
    out["time_to_onset_s"] = np.where(out["eligible"] & hit, first * p.cadence_s, np.nan)

    for name, col, agg in (
        ("fwd_max_rel_spread", "rel_spread", np.max),
        ("fwd_min_depth_ratio", "depth_ratio", np.min),
    ):
        f = _forward(out[col], h)
        full = ~np.isnan(f).any(axis=0)
        out[name] = np.where(full, agg(np.where(np.isnan(f), 0.0, f), axis=0), np.nan)
    return out


def build_labels(depth: pd.DataFrame, p: LabelParams = LabelParams()) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Depth snapshots (recorder ``depth5`` schema) -> labelled one-second ticks."""
    return add_labels(add_stress(to_ticks(book_state(depth), p), p), p)


def label_at(stress: pd.DataFrame, ts_ms: int, p: LabelParams = LabelParams()) -> int | None:
    """Slow, obvious reference for one prediction time. ``None`` means void or not eligible.

    The outcome verifier must give this answer; tests check it against ``add_labels``.
    """
    step = p.cadence_s * 1000
    if ts_ms not in stress.index or stress.at[ts_ms, "stressed"] != 0:
        return None
    future = [ts_ms + j * step for j in range(1, p.horizon_ticks + 1)]
    if any(t not in stress.index or pd.isna(stress.at[t, "confirmed"]) for t in future):
        return None
    return int(any(stress.at[t, "confirmed"] == 1 for t in future))


# ---- thresholds and base rates (tasks 9.3 and 9.6) --------------------------------------
def fit_thresholds(
    stress: pd.DataFrame, p: LabelParams = LabelParams(), stress_share: float = 0.01
) -> LabelParams:
    """Pick thresholds from *training-period* ticks so each rule fires on about ``stress_share`` of them.

    Call once on the training period, then freeze the result in the domain config. If the
    spread is pinned at one tick (the usual case on BTCUSDT) its ratio has no usable tail,
    and the spread rule is switched off.
    """
    depth = stress["depth_ratio"].dropna().to_numpy()
    spread = stress["spread_ratio"].dropna().to_numpy()
    if len(depth) < 1000:
        raise ValueError(f"need at least 1000 ticks with a reference to fit thresholds, got {len(depth)}")
    depth_fraction = float(np.clip(np.quantile(depth, stress_share), 0.05, 0.95))
    spread_multiple = float(np.quantile(spread, 1 - stress_share))
    use_spread = spread_multiple > 1.5
    return replace(
        p,
        depth_fraction=round(depth_fraction, 4),
        use_spread=use_spread,
        spread_multiple=round(spread_multiple, 4) if use_spread else p.spread_multiple,
    )


def average_precision(y: np.ndarray, score: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Area under the precision-recall curve (step-wise), ties broken pessimistically by order."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    if y.sum() == 0:
        return float("nan")
    order = np.argsort(-np.asarray(score, dtype=float), kind="stable")
    hits = y[order]
    precision = np.cumsum(hits) / np.arange(1, len(hits) + 1)
    return float((precision * hits).sum() / hits.sum())


def _episodes(confirmed: pd.Series) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    """(start, end) positions of each run of confirmed stress."""
    c = (confirmed.to_numpy(float) == 1).astype(np.int8)
    edges = np.diff(np.concatenate(([0], c, [0])))
    return list(zip(np.nonzero(edges == 1)[0].tolist(), np.nonzero(edges == -1)[0].tolist(), strict=True))


def base_rate_report(labeled: pd.DataFrame, p: LabelParams = LabelParams()) -> dict:
    """How often the label fires, how bursty it is, and how strong the lazy baseline is."""
    n = len(labeled)
    known = labeled["stressed"].notna()
    elig = labeled["eligible"].to_numpy(bool)
    y = labeled.loc[elig, "label"].to_numpy(float)
    hours = n * p.cadence_s / 3600
    eps = _episodes(labeled["confirmed"])
    durations = [(b - a) * p.cadence_s for a, b in eps]
    # positives belonging to the single busiest hour: 1.0 means every positive came from one burst
    pos_idx = labeled.index[elig][y == 1]
    by_hour = pd.Series(pos_idx // 3_600_000).value_counts() if len(pos_idx) else pd.Series(dtype=float)
    # persistence baseline: "the thinner the book is right now, the likelier stress is"
    score = -labeled.loc[elig, "depth_ratio"].to_numpy(float)
    prevalence = float(y.mean()) if len(y) else float("nan")
    ap = average_precision(y, score) if len(y) else float("nan")
    return {
        "params": asdict(p),
        "ticks": n,
        "hours": round(hours, 3),
        "known_share": round(float(known.mean()), 4) if n else None,
        "stressed_share": round(float(labeled.loc[known, "stressed"].mean()), 5) if known.any() else None,
        "stress_from_depth_share": round(float(labeled.loc[known, "stress_depth"].mean()), 5)
        if known.any()
        else None,
        "stress_from_spread_share": round(float(labeled.loc[known, "stress_spread"].mean()), 5)
        if known.any()
        else None,
        "eligible_ticks": int(elig.sum()),
        "positives": int(y.sum()),
        "positive_rate": round(prevalence, 5) if len(y) else None,
        "episodes": len(eps),
        "episodes_per_hour": round(len(eps) / hours, 3) if hours else None,
        "median_episode_s": float(np.median(durations)) if durations else None,
        "busiest_hour_share_of_positives": round(float(by_hour.iloc[0] / by_hour.sum()), 3)
        if len(by_hour)
        else None,
        "persistence_baseline_ap": round(ap, 4) if ap == ap else None,
        "persistence_lift_over_prevalence": round(ap / prevalence, 2)
        if ap == ap and prevalence > 0
        else None,
    }


def verdict(report: dict) -> list[str]:
    """Plain-language go/no-go notes for task 9.6."""
    notes = []
    if not report["eligible_ticks"]:
        return ["NO-GO: no eligible ticks. Record more data (the reference needs a warm-up) and rerun."]
    rate, episodes = report["positive_rate"] or 0, report["episodes"]
    if episodes < 50:
        notes.append(f"NO-GO for training: only {episodes} stress episodes; aim for a few hundred.")
    if rate < 0.002:
        notes.append(
            "Label is very rare (<0.2%). Try a longer horizon or a softer depth_fraction (task 9.7)."
        )
    elif rate > 0.2:
        notes.append("Label fires on more than 20% of ticks: the threshold is too soft to mean 'stress'.")
    if (report["stress_from_spread_share"] or 0) < 0.1 * (report["stressed_share"] or 0):
        notes.append("The spread rule contributes almost nothing; depth depletion carries the label.")
    if (report["busiest_hour_share_of_positives"] or 0) > 0.5:
        notes.append("More than half of all positives come from one hour: results will hinge on that burst.")
    lift = report["persistence_lift_over_prevalence"]
    if lift is not None:
        notes.append(
            f"Persistence baseline AP is {lift}x the prevalence. A model must beat that, not chance."
        )
    return notes or ["GO: base rate, episode count and burstiness look workable."]


# ---- CLI ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def load_recorded_depth(data_dir: str | Path, symbol: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    import pyarrow.parquet as pq

    files = sorted((Path(data_dir) / "stream=depth5" / f"symbol={symbol}").rglob("part-*.parquet"))
    if not files:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"no depth5 files for {symbol} under {data_dir}")
    return pd.concat([pq.read_table(f).to_pandas() for f in files], ignore_index=True)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Build liquidity-stress labels and the base-rate report")
    ap.add_argument("--data-dir", default="data/raw/binance")
    ap.add_argument("--symbol", default="BTCUSDT")
    ap.add_argument("--config", default="configs/domain_liquidity.yaml")
    ap.add_argument("--fit", action="store_true", help="fit thresholds on this data (training period only!)")
    ap.add_argument("--stress-share", type=float, default=0.01)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="write labelled ticks to this Parquet file")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    p = LabelParams.from_domain_yaml(args.config)
    stress = add_stress(to_ticks(book_state(load_recorded_depth(args.data_dir, args.symbol)), p), p)
    if args.fit:
        p = fit_thresholds(stress, p, args.stress_share)
        stress = add_stress(stress[[*STATE_COLUMNS, "age_ms"]], p)
    labeled = add_labels(stress, p)
    report = base_rate_report(labeled, p)
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    for note in verdict(report):
        print("-", note)
    if args.out:
        labeled.to_parquet(args.out)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== src\sentinalops\logging.py ===
"""Structured JSON logging so every service emits machine-parseable lines."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import sys
import time

_RESERVED = set(logging.LogRecord("", 0, "", 0, "", (), None).__dict__) | {"message", "asctime"}


class JsonFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    def format(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> str:
        payload = {
            "ts": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S", time.gmtime(record.created)) + "Z",
            "level": record.levelname,
            "logger": record.name,
            "msg": record.getMessage(),
        }
        payload.update({k: v for k, v in record.__dict__.items() if k not in _RESERVED})
        if record.exc_info:
            payload["exc"] = self.formatException(record.exc_info)
        return json.dumps(payload, default=str)


def configure_logging(level: str = "INFO") -> None:
    handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    handler.setFormatter(JsonFormatter())
    root = logging.getLogger()
    root.handlers[:] = [handler]
    root.setLevel(level.upper())


def get_logger(name: str) -> logging.Logger:
    return logging.getLogger(name)


=== src\sentinalops\schemas.py ===
"""Event and API schemas shared by the recorder, stream processor, API and outcome verifier.

Conventions (same as the recorder's Parquet files):

- Timestamps are UTC epoch milliseconds (``int``).
- Prices and quantities are floats.
- ``TradeEvent`` and ``DepthSnapshot`` round-trip with the recorder's flat row dicts through
  ``from_row`` / ``to_row``, so the stream and the offline files share one shape.

Feature dictionaries are checked for finite values here; whether they carry the right
feature *names* depends on the domain config, so the API checks that against
``DomainConfig.feature_names``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import time
import uuid
from typing import Annotated, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, StringConstraints, field_validator, model_validator

DEPTH_LEVELS = 5
Symbol = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(pattern=r"^[A-Z0-9]{5,20}$")]


def _now_ms() -> int:
    return int(time.time() * 1000)


class _Event(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", frozen=True)


class TradeEvent(_Event):
    """One aggregate trade (``<symbol>@aggTrade``). Field names match the recorder's trade files."""

    symbol: Symbol
    event_time_ms: int = Field(gt=0)
    trade_time_ms: int = Field(gt=0)
    agg_trade_id: int = Field(ge=0)
    price: float = Field(gt=0)
    quantity: float = Field(gt=0)
    first_trade_id: int = Field(ge=0)
    last_trade_id: int = Field(ge=0)
    buyer_is_maker: bool
    recv_time_ms: int = Field(gt=0)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _ids_ordered(self) -> TradeEvent:
        if self.last_trade_id < self.first_trade_id:
            raise ValueError("last_trade_id must be >= first_trade_id")
        if not (math.isfinite(self.price) and math.isfinite(self.quantity)):
            raise ValueError("price and quantity must be finite")
        return self

    @property
    def is_buy(self) -> bool:
        """Buyer-initiated: the buyer took liquidity, so the seller was the maker."""
        return not self.buyer_is_maker

    @classmethod
    def from_row(cls, row: dict) -> TradeEvent:
        return cls(**row)

    def to_row(self) -> dict:
        return self.model_dump()


class Level(_Event):
    px: float = Field(gt=0)
    qty: float = Field(ge=0)


class DepthSnapshot(_Event):
    """Top-of-book snapshot (``<symbol>@depth5@100ms``), best level first on each side."""

    symbol: Symbol
    last_update_id: int = Field(ge=0)
    recv_time_ms: int = Field(gt=0)
    bids: tuple[Level, ...] = Field(min_length=DEPTH_LEVELS, max_length=DEPTH_LEVELS)
    asks: tuple[Level, ...] = Field(min_length=DEPTH_LEVELS, max_length=DEPTH_LEVELS)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _book_is_sane(self) -> DepthSnapshot:
        bid_px = [lv.px for lv in self.bids]
        ask_px = [lv.px for lv in self.asks]
        if any(b <= c for b, c in zip(bid_px, bid_px[1:], strict=False)):
            raise ValueError("bid prices must strictly decrease from the best level")
        if any(a >= c for a, c in zip(ask_px, ask_px[1:], strict=False)):
            raise ValueError("ask prices must strictly increase from the best level")
        if bid_px[0] >= ask_px[0]:
            raise ValueError("crossed book: best bid must be below best ask")
        return self

    @property
    def mid(self) -> float:
        return (self.bids[0].px + self.asks[0].px) / 2

    @classmethod
    def from_row(cls, row: dict) -> DepthSnapshot:
        def side(name: str) -> tuple[dict, ...]:
            return tuple(
                {"px": row[f"{name}_px_{i}"], "qty": row[f"{name}_qty_{i}"]}
                for i in range(1, DEPTH_LEVELS + 1)
            )

        return cls(
            symbol=row["symbol"],
            last_update_id=row["last_update_id"],
            recv_time_ms=row["recv_time_ms"],
            bids=side("bid"),
            asks=side("ask"),
        )

    def to_row(self) -> dict:
        row: dict = {
            "symbol": self.symbol,
            "last_update_id": self.last_update_id,
            "recv_time_ms": self.recv_time_ms,
        }
        for name, levels in (("bid", self.bids), ("ask", self.asks)):
            for i, lv in enumerate(levels, start=1):
                row[f"{name}_px_{i}"] = lv.px
                row[f"{name}_qty_{i}"] = lv.qty
        return row


def _finite_features(v: dict[str, float]) -> dict[str, float]:
    if not v:
        raise ValueError("features must not be empty")
    bad = sorted(k for k, x in v.items() if not math.isfinite(x))
    if bad:
        raise ValueError(f"features must be finite; bad: {bad}")
    return v


class FeatureTick(_Event):
    """One valid feature tick: the model input for ``symbol`` at ``ts_ms``."""

    symbol: Symbol
    ts_ms: int = Field(gt=0)
    features: dict[str, float]

    @field_validator("features")
    @classmethod
    def check_features(cls, v: dict[str, float]) -> dict[str, float]:
        return _finite_features(v)


class StressPrediction(_Event):
    """A forecast that stress starts in ``(ts_ms, ts_ms + horizon_s]``. Goes to the prediction ledger."""

    prediction_id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: uuid.uuid4().hex)
    symbol: Symbol
    ts_ms: int = Field(gt=0)
    horizon_s: int = Field(gt=0)
    probability: float = Field(ge=0, le=1)
    alert: bool
    threshold: float = Field(ge=0, le=1)
    model_version: str
    scored_at_ms: int = Field(default_factory=_now_ms)

    @property
    def matures_at_ms(self) -> int:
        return self.ts_ms + self.horizon_s * 1000


class OutcomeLabel(_Event):
    """What actually happened in the horizon after ``ts_ms``, produced once the horizon has passed.

    ``label`` is ``None`` when the outcome is unknowable (a feed gap inside the horizon, or the
    market was already stressed at ``ts_ms``); such outcomes are recorded but never scored.
    """

    symbol: Symbol
    ts_ms: int = Field(gt=0)
    horizon_s: int = Field(gt=0)
    label: Literal[0, 1] | None
    void_reason: Literal["gap", "already_stressed", "warmup"] | None = None
    source: str = "outcome_verifier"
    verified_at_ms: int = Field(default_factory=_now_ms)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _void_has_reason(self) -> OutcomeLabel:
        if (self.label is None) != (self.void_reason is not None):
            raise ValueError("void_reason must be set exactly when label is None")
        return self


class ScoreRequest(BaseModel):
    """Body of ``POST /score``: one feature tick."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    symbol: Symbol
    ts_ms: int = Field(gt=0)
    features: dict[str, float]

    @field_validator("features")
    @classmethod
    def check_features(cls, v: dict[str, float]) -> dict[str, float]:
        return _finite_features(v)


=== src\sentinalops\__init__.py ===

=== src\sentinalops\data\audit_recording.py ===
"""Audit recorded Binance stream data (task 7.9).

    python -m sentinalops.data.audit_recording --data-dir data/raw/binance --hours 24

Reports, per symbol and stream: row counts and disk footprint, schema and null checks,
duplicate or out-of-order rows, and gaps. A depth gap is a jump in receive time above
``--depth-gap-ms``. A trade gap is a hole in the aggregate trade IDs, which is the only
reliable sign of lost trades (a quiet market also produces time gaps). Every gap is
cross-referenced with the recorder's own gap log; gaps it did not log are "unexplained".

Exits 1 when it finds integrity problems or unexplained gaps (disable with ``--no-fail``).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
import time
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pyarrow.parquet as pq

from sentinalops.data.recorder.messages import AGG_TRADE, DEPTH5, GAP_SCHEMA, SCHEMAS, SEQUENCE_COLUMN

EXPLAIN_TOLERANCE_MS = 1500


def _iso(ms: int | None) -> str | None:
    if ms is None:
        return None
    return datetime.fromtimestamp(ms / 1000, tz=UTC).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%f")[:-3] + "Z"


def _file_span(path: Path) -> tuple[int, int] | None:
    """(first_ms, last_ms) parsed from ``part-<first>-<last>-<id>.parquet``."""
    parts = path.stem.split("-")
    try:
        return int(parts[1]), int(parts[2])
    except (IndexError, ValueError):
        return None


def load_gap_log(
    root: Path, start_ms: int | None, end_ms: int | None, errors: list[str] | None = None
) -> list[dict]:
    """The recorder's outage log. Unreadable files are appended to ``errors``, never ignored."""
    gaps: list[dict] = []
    for f in sorted((root / "_gaps").glob("gap-*.parquet")):
        try:
            table = pq.read_table(f)
            if not table.schema.equals(GAP_SCHEMA, check_metadata=False):
                raise ValueError(f"unexpected columns {table.schema.names}")
            gaps += table.to_pylist()
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            if errors is None:
                raise
            errors.append(f"{f.relative_to(root)}: {e!r}")
    return [
        g
        for g in gaps
        if (end_ms is None or g["start_ms"] <= end_ms) and (start_ms is None or g["end_ms"] >= start_ms)
    ]


def _explained(start_ms: int, end_ms: int, gap_log: list[dict], scope_symbol: str) -> str | None:
    for g in gap_log:
        if g["scope"] not in ("all", f"depth5:{scope_symbol}"):
            continue
        if g["start_ms"] - EXPLAIN_TOLERANCE_MS <= end_ms and g["end_ms"] + EXPLAIN_TOLERANCE_MS >= start_ms:
            return g["reason"]
    return None


def _audit_stream(root: Path, kind: str, symbol_dir: Path, start_ms, end_ms, depth_gap_ms, gap_log) -> dict:
    symbol = symbol_dir.name.split("=", 1)[1]
    schema = SCHEMAS[kind]
    seq_col = SEQUENCE_COLUMN[kind]
    out: dict = {
        "symbol": symbol,
        "stream": kind,
        "files": 0,
        "rows": 0,
        "bytes": 0,
        "first_recv_ms": None,
        "last_recv_ms": None,
        "schema_errors": [],
        "unreadable": [],
        "null_counts": {},
        "duplicate_or_out_of_order": 0,
        "gaps": [],
    }
    files = sorted(symbol_dir.rglob("part-*.parquet"), key=lambda p: (_file_span(p) or (0, 0), p.name))
    times, seqs = [], []
    for f in files:
        span = _file_span(f)
        if span and (
            (end_ms is not None and span[0] > end_ms) or (start_ms is not None and span[1] < start_ms)
        ):
            continue
        try:
            table = pq.read_table(f)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            out["unreadable"].append(f"{f.relative_to(root)}: {e!r}")
            continue
        out["files"] += 1
        out["bytes"] += f.stat().st_size
        if not table.schema.equals(schema, check_metadata=False):
            out["schema_errors"].append(
                f"{f.relative_to(root)}: {table.schema.names} / types differ from expected"
            )
            if "recv_time_ms" not in table.schema.names or seq_col not in table.schema.names:
                continue
        for name in table.schema.names:
            nulls = table.column(name).null_count
            if nulls:
                out["null_counts"][name] = out["null_counts"].get(name, 0) + nulls
        times.append(table.column("recv_time_ms").to_numpy(zero_copy_only=False))
        seqs.append(table.column(seq_col).to_numpy(zero_copy_only=False))

    if not times:
        return out
    t, s = np.concatenate(times), np.concatenate(seqs)
    keep = np.ones(len(t), dtype=bool)
    if start_ms is not None:
        keep &= t >= start_ms
    if end_ms is not None:
        keep &= t <= end_ms
    t, s = t[keep], s[keep]
    out["rows"] = int(len(t))
    if len(t) == 0:
        return out
    out["first_recv_ms"], out["last_recv_ms"] = int(t[0]), int(t[-1])
    out["duplicate_or_out_of_order"] = int(np.sum(np.diff(s) <= 0))

    if kind == DEPTH5:
        idx = np.nonzero(np.diff(t) > depth_gap_ms)[0]
        for i in idx:
            a, b = int(t[i]), int(t[i + 1])
            out["gaps"].append(
                {
                    "start_ms": a,
                    "end_ms": b,
                    "duration_ms": b - a,
                    "explained_by": _explained(a, b, gap_log, symbol),
                }
            )
    else:
        idx = np.nonzero(np.diff(s) > 1)[0]
        for i in idx:
            a, b = int(t[i]), int(t[i + 1])
            out["gaps"].append(
                {
                    "start_ms": a,
                    "end_ms": b,
                    "duration_ms": b - a,
                    "missing_trades": int(s[i + 1] - s[i] - 1),
                    "explained_by": _explained(a, b, gap_log, symbol),
                }
            )
    return out


def audit(
    data_dir: str | Path, start_ms: int | None = None, end_ms: int | None = None, depth_gap_ms: int = 500
) -> dict:
    root = Path(data_dir)
    gap_errors: list[str] = []
    gap_log = load_gap_log(root, start_ms, end_ms, gap_errors)
    streams = []
    for kind in (AGG_TRADE, DEPTH5):
        for symbol_dir in sorted((root / f"stream={kind}").glob("symbol=*")):
            streams.append(_audit_stream(root, kind, symbol_dir, start_ms, end_ms, depth_gap_ms, gap_log))
    tmp_files = [str(p.relative_to(root)) for p in root.rglob("*.tmp")] if root.exists() else []
    rejected = root / "_rejected" / "frames.jsonl"
    rejected_frames = sum(1 for _ in rejected.open(encoding="utf-8")) if rejected.exists() else 0

    firsts = [s["first_recv_ms"] for s in streams if s["first_recv_ms"] is not None]
    lasts = [s["last_recv_ms"] for s in streams if s["last_recv_ms"] is not None]
    span_s = (max(lasts) - min(firsts)) / 1000 if firsts else 0.0
    total_bytes = sum(s["bytes"] for s in streams)
    unexplained = sum(1 for s in streams for g in s["gaps"] if g["explained_by"] is None)
    problems = []
    if not streams or not any(s["rows"] for s in streams):
        problems.append("no recorded rows found")
    for s in streams:
        tag = f"{s['symbol']}/{s['stream']}"
        if s["schema_errors"]:
            problems.append(f"{tag}: {len(s['schema_errors'])} file(s) with a wrong schema")
        if s["unreadable"]:
            problems.append(f"{tag}: {len(s['unreadable'])} unreadable file(s)")
        if s["null_counts"]:
            problems.append(f"{tag}: null values in {sorted(s['null_counts'])}")
        if s["duplicate_or_out_of_order"]:
