    """Training-table-shaped rows (``symbol, ts_ms, <features>, label, label_status, regime, source``)."""
    with closing(ledger._connect()) as db:
        rows = db.execute(_QUERY, (since_ms, until_ms)).fetchall()
    records, seen = [], set()
    for r in rows:
        key = (r["symbol"], r["ts_ms"])
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        feats = json.loads(r["features"])
        if any(name not in feats for name in feature_names):
            continue  # scored on a different feature set (e.g. before a config change)
        records.append({"symbol": r["symbol"], "ts_ms": r["ts_ms"],
                        **{n: float(feats[n]) for n in feature_names}, "label": int(r["label"])})
    df = pd.DataFrame(records, columns=["symbol", "ts_ms", *feature_names, "label"])
    df["label_status"], df["regime"], df["source"] = "ok", "untagged", "ledger"
    return df.reset_index(drop=True)


=== src\sentinalops\heal\monitor.py ===
"""Live model quality from matured predictions (chunk 39) and the degradation signal.

Everything here reads the prediction ledger: only predictions whose outcome is known and not
void count. A window with too few stress events gives no verdict (NaN scores, ``degraded``
False, reason ``too_few_events``) rather than a noisy one.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass

import numpy as np

from sentinalops.config import HealConfig
from sentinalops.labels import average_precision
from sentinalops.serving.ledger import Ledger


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WindowScores:
    model_version: str | None
    since_ms: int
    until_ms: int
    n: int  # matured, non-void predictions
    positives: int
    ap: float
    brier: float
    base_rate: float
    precision: float
    recall: float

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return asdict(self)


def window_scores(
    ledger: Ledger, since_ms: int, until_ms: int, model_version: str | None = None
) -> WindowScores:
    """Scores of matured, non-void predictions made in ``[since_ms, until_ms)``."""
    rows = [r for r in ledger.predictions(since_ms, until_ms, model_version, with_features=False)
            if r["result"] not in ("pending", "void")]
    y = np.array([r["label"] for r in rows], dtype=float)
    p = np.array([r["probability"] for r in rows], dtype=float)
    alert = np.array([r["alert"] for r in rows], dtype=bool)
    n, pos = len(rows), int(y.sum()) if len(rows) else 0
    hits = int((alert & (y == 1)).sum())
    return WindowScores(
        model_version=model_version, since_ms=since_ms, until_ms=until_ms, n=n, positives=pos,
        ap=average_precision(y, p) if pos else math.nan,
        brier=float(np.mean((p - y) ** 2)) if n else math.nan,
        base_rate=pos / n if n else math.nan,
        precision=hits / int(alert.sum()) if alert.any() else math.nan,
        recall=hits / pos if pos else math.nan,
    )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Degradation:
    degraded: bool
    reason: str  # degraded | healthy | too_few_events | no_reference
    current: WindowScores
    reference_ap: float


def degradation(current: WindowScores, reference_ap: float, cfg: HealConfig) -> Degradation:
    """Degraded when live AP falls more than ``degradation_margin`` below the champion's reference.

    The reference is the AP the champion scored on its holdout when it was promoted.
    """
    if current.positives < cfg.min_positives:
        return Degradation(False, "too_few_events", current, reference_ap)
    if math.isnan(reference_ap):
        return Degradation(False, "no_reference", current, reference_ap)
    if current.ap < reference_ap - cfg.degradation_margin:
        return Degradation(True, "degraded", current, reference_ap)
    return Degradation(False, "healthy", current, reference_ap)


=== src\sentinalops\heal\registry.py ===
"""File-based model registry with champion and shadow aliases (chunk 45).

A stand-in for the MLflow registry (chunk 32) with the same idea: versions are immutable,
aliases move. Layout under ``root``::

    versions/<version>.joblib   every registered model, never changed
    champion.joblib             the served model (what the API and processor load)
    shadow.joblib               the previous champion, during a shadow period only
    registry.json               aliases, version metadata and an event log

``champion.joblib`` and ``shadow.joblib`` are replaced atomically (write, then rename), so a
reader sees the old model or the new one, never half a file; the API and the stream processor
pick the new champion up through their existing hot reload. Each model carries its version
in ``sentinal_version_``, so a prediction's ``model_version`` always names the file that made it.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import shutil
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import joblib

from sentinalops.logging import get_logger

log = get_logger("sentinalops.registry")


@dataclass
class RegistryState:
    champion: str | None = None
    shadow: str | None = None
    shadow_until_ms: int | None = None
    promoted_at_ms: int | None = None
    versions: dict[str, dict] = field(default_factory=dict)
    events: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)


class ModelRegistry:
    def __init__(self, root: str | Path) -> None:
        self.root = Path(root)
        (self.root / "versions").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ---- paths and state ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def champion_path(self) -> Path:
        return self.root / "champion.joblib"

    @property
    def shadow_path(self) -> Path:
        return self.root / "shadow.joblib"

    def version_path(self, version: str) -> Path:
        return self.root / "versions" / f"{version}.joblib"

    def state(self) -> RegistryState:
        path = self.root / "registry.json"
        if not path.exists():
            return RegistryState()
        return RegistryState(**json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")))

    def _save(self, s: RegistryState) -> None:
        path = self.root / "registry.json"
        tmp = path.with_suffix(".json.tmp")
        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(s.__dict__, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        os.replace(tmp, path)

    def _install(self, version: str, dest: Path) -> None:
        tmp = dest.with_suffix(".joblib.tmp")
        shutil.copyfile(self.version_path(version), tmp)
        os.replace(tmp, dest)

    def meta(self, version: str) -> dict:
        return self.state().versions[version]

    def load(self, version: str):
        return joblib.load(self.version_path(version))

    def count(self, kind: str) -> int:
        """Number of ``promote`` or ``rollback`` events so far (``model_promotions_total`` etc.)."""
        return sum(1 for e in self.state().events if e["kind"] == kind)

    # ---- writes ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def register(self, model, meta: dict, now_ms: int) -> str:
        """Store a new immutable version and return its name (``v001``, ``v002``, ...)."""
        s = self.state()
        version = f"v{len(s.versions) + 1:03d}"
        model.sentinal_version_ = version
        tmp = self.version_path(version).with_suffix(".joblib.tmp")
        joblib.dump(model, tmp)
        os.replace(tmp, self.version_path(version))
        s.versions[version] = {**meta, "registered_at_ms": now_ms}
        s.events.append({"ts_ms": now_ms, "kind": "register", "version": version})
        self._save(s)
        return version

    def promote(self, version: str, now_ms: int, reason: str, shadow_ms: int = 0) -> None:
        """Make ``version`` the champion. The old champion scores in shadow for ``shadow_ms``."""
        s = self.state()
        if version not in s.versions:
            raise KeyError(version)
        previous = s.champion
        if previous is not None and shadow_ms > 0:
            self._install(previous, self.shadow_path)
            s.shadow, s.shadow_until_ms = previous, now_ms + shadow_ms
        else:
            self.shadow_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            s.shadow, s.shadow_until_ms = None, None
        self._install(version, self.champion_path)
        s.champion, s.promoted_at_ms = version, now_ms
        s.events.append({"ts_ms": now_ms, "kind": "promote", "version": version, "from": previous,
                         "reason": reason})
        self._save(s)
        log.info("model promoted", extra={"version": version, "from": previous, "reason": reason})

    def end_shadow(self, now_ms: int) -> None:
        s = self.state()
        if s.shadow is None:
            return
        self.shadow_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        s.events.append({"ts_ms": now_ms, "kind": "shadow_end", "version": s.champion, "shadow": s.shadow})
        s.shadow, s.shadow_until_ms = None, None
        self._save(s)

    def rollback(self, now_ms: int, reason: str) -> str:
        """Restore the shadow (previous) champion. Returns the restored version."""
        s = self.state()
        if s.shadow is None:
            raise RuntimeError("nothing to roll back to: no shadow champion")
        bad, good = s.champion, s.shadow
        self._install(good, self.champion_path)
        self.shadow_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        s.champion, s.shadow, s.shadow_until_ms, s.promoted_at_ms = good, None, None, now_ms
        s.events.append({"ts_ms": now_ms, "kind": "rollback", "version": good, "from": bad, "reason": reason})
        self._save(s)
        log.warning("model rolled back", extra={"version": good, "from": bad, "reason": reason})
        return good

    def log_event(self, now_ms: int, kind: str, **details) -> None:
        s = self.state()
        s.events.append({"ts_ms": now_ms, "kind": kind, **details})
        self._save(s)


=== src\sentinalops\heal\retrain.py ===
"""Challenger training data and the challenger itself (chunk 43), and the gates (chunk 44).

Input is the training table from chunk 12 (``ts_ms``, the features, ``label``,
``label_status``, ``regime``). Only rows with ``label_status == 'ok'`` are used.

Split, for a retrain at ``now_ms``::

    ... older rows ...  | recent window (retrain_window_hours)                      |
    replay buffer        | train                      | embargo | holdout (newest)   |

- **Holdout:** the newest ``holdout_fraction`` of the window. The gates judge on it.
- **Embargo:** ``split_embargo_seconds`` (>= the label horizon) between train and holdout, so
  no training label looks into the holdout period.
- **Replay buffer:** a sample of rows older than the window, spread evenly over regimes, so
  the challenger does not forget regimes it has not seen lately. ``older_eval`` is a disjoint
  sample of the same, used by the no-regression gate.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from sentinalops.config import DomainConfig
from sentinalops.labels import average_precision
from sentinalops.models import get_recipe, model_name


@dataclass
class TrainingSet:
    train: pd.DataFrame
    holdout: pd.DataFrame
    older_eval: pd.DataFrame
    window: tuple[int, int]  # [start, end) of the recent window
    counts: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


def _sample_by_regime(df: pd.DataFrame, n: int, rng: np.random.Generator) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if n <= 0 or df.empty:
        return df.iloc[:0]
    groups = list(df.groupby("regime", sort=True))
    per = max(n // len(groups), 1)
    parts = [g.sample(min(per, len(g)), random_state=int(rng.integers(1 << 31))) for _, g in groups]
    return pd.concat(parts)


def assemble(table: pd.DataFrame, now_ms: int, cfg: DomainConfig, seed: int = 0) -> TrainingSet:
    """Recent window + replay buffer for training, the newest slice for the gates."""
    h = cfg.heal
    ok = table[(table["label_status"] == "ok") & (table["ts_ms"] < now_ms)].sort_values("ts_ms")
    start = now_ms - cfg.retrain_window_hours * 3_600_000
    recent, older = ok[ok["ts_ms"] >= start], ok[ok["ts_ms"] < start]
    holdout_start = now_ms - int((now_ms - start) * h.holdout_fraction)
    holdout = recent[recent["ts_ms"] >= holdout_start]
    train_recent = recent[recent["ts_ms"] < holdout_start - cfg.split_embargo_seconds * 1000]

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n_replay = int(len(train_recent) * h.replay_fraction)
    older_shuffled = older.sample(frac=1.0, random_state=seed) if len(older) else older
    half = len(older_shuffled) // 2
    replay = _sample_by_regime(older_shuffled.iloc[:half], n_replay, rng)
    older_eval = older_shuffled.iloc[half:]
    train = pd.concat([train_recent, replay]).sort_values("ts_ms")
    counts = {
        "train_recent": len(train_recent), "replay": len(replay), "holdout": len(holdout),
        "older_eval": len(older_eval), "train_positives": int(train["label"].sum()),
        "holdout_positives": int(holdout["label"].sum()),
        "regime_mix": {str(k): int(v) for k, v in train["regime"].value_counts().items()},
    }
    return TrainingSet(train, holdout, older_eval, (start, now_ms), counts)


def resolve_recipe(champion, cfg: DomainConfig) -> tuple[str, dict]:
    """The recipe to retrain with: the champion's own, else (older models) the configured one."""
    name = model_name(champion) if champion is not None else None
    if name is None:
        return cfg.model.name, dict(cfg.model.params)
    return name, dict(getattr(champion, "sentinal_model_params_", {}) or {})


def train_challenger(ts: TrainingSet, cfg: DomainConfig, seed: int = 0, champion=None):
    """Retrain the champion's model type on the new window (heal never switches model types)."""
    if ts.train.empty or ts.train["label"].nunique() < 2:
        raise ValueError("training window has no positive or no negative labels")
    data = ts.train.assign(label=ts.train["label"].astype(int))
    name, params = resolve_recipe(champion, cfg)
    return get_recipe(name).fit(data[[*cfg.feature_names, "label"]], cfg, seed, params)


# ---- gates (chunk 44) -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _score(model, df: pd.DataFrame, names: list[str]) -> dict:
    if df.empty or df["label"].sum() == 0:
        return {"ap": math.nan, "brier": math.nan, "n": len(df), "positives": 0}
    y = df["label"].to_numpy(float)
    p = model.predict_proba(df[names])[:, 1]
    return {"ap": average_precision(y, p), "brier": float(np.mean((p - y) ** 2)), "n": len(df),
            "positives": int(y.sum())}


@dataclass
class GateReport:
    passed: bool
    gates: dict[str, dict]
    champion: dict
    challenger: dict
    baseline_ap: float
    counts: dict

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return asdict(self)

    def to_json(self) -> str:
        return json.dumps(self.to_dict(), indent=2, default=float)

    def to_markdown(self) -> str:
        lines = ["| Gate | Passed | Value | Needed |", "| --- | --- | --- | --- |"]
        for name, g in self.gates.items():
            lines.append(f"| {name} | {'yes' if g['passed'] else 'no'} | {g['value']:.4f} | {g['needed']} |")
        verdict = "PASSED: promote" if self.passed else "FAILED: keep the champion"
        return f"**{verdict}**\n\n" + "\n".join(lines)


def evaluate_gates(champion, challenger, ts: TrainingSet, cfg: DomainConfig) -> GateReport:
    """Champion and challenger on the same holdout (current regime) and on older regimes."""
    h, names = cfg.heal, cfg.feature_names
    champ = {"current": _score(champion, ts.holdout, names),
             "older": _score(champion, ts.older_eval, names)}
    chall = {"current": _score(challenger, ts.holdout, names),
             "older": _score(challenger, ts.older_eval, names)}
    base_rate = float(ts.holdout["label"].mean()) if len(ts.holdout) else math.nan
    gain = chall["current"]["ap"] - champ["current"]["ap"]
    regression = champ["older"]["ap"] - chall["older"]["ap"]
    lift = chall["current"]["ap"] / base_rate if base_rate else math.nan
    brier_up = chall["current"]["brier"] - champ["current"]["brier"]
    older_known = not math.isnan(regression)
    gates = {
        "improves_current_regime": {"value": gain, "needed": f">= {h.min_gain}",
                                    "passed": gain >= h.min_gain},
        "no_regression_on_older": {"value": regression if older_known else 0.0,
                                   "needed": f"<= {h.max_regression}",
                                   "passed": (not older_known) or regression <= h.max_regression},
        "beats_base_rate_baseline": {"value": lift, "needed": f">= {h.min_lift_over_baseline}x",
                                     "passed": lift >= h.min_lift_over_baseline},
        "calibration_not_worse": {"value": brier_up, "needed": f"<= {h.max_brier_increase}",
                                  "passed": brier_up <= h.max_brier_increase},
    }
    # NaN comparisons are False, so a gate with no data fails closed (except older regimes: none yet)
    passed = all(g["passed"] for g in gates.values())
    return GateReport(passed, gates, champ, chall, base_rate, ts.counts)


=== src\sentinalops\heal\__init__.py ===
"""The self-healing loop: monitor, trigger, retrain, gate, promote, roll back (chunks 39, 42-45)."""


=== src\sentinalops\live\bus.py ===
"""The message bus between live components: one interface, swappable transports.

Topics (chunk 15): ``raw_trades`` and ``raw_depth`` (recorder-schema rows), ``features``
(``FeatureTick``), ``predictions`` (``StressPrediction`` plus the features it was scored on) and
``outcomes`` (``OutcomeLabel``). Messages are JSON-compatible dicts keyed by symbol.

- ``InMemoryBus``: asyncio queues inside one process (the default; no broker needed).
- ``KafkaBus`` (``live/kafka_bus.py``): Redpanda/Kafka, so components can run as separate processes.

Every subscriber sees every message on its topics, in publish order per topic.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import hashlib
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from typing import Protocol

from sentinalops.data.recorder.messages import AGG_TRADE, DEPTH5
from sentinalops.logging import get_logger

log = get_logger("sentinalops.live.bus")

TOPICS = ("raw_trades", "raw_depth", "features", "predictions", "outcomes")
TOPIC_OF_KIND = {AGG_TRADE: "raw_trades", DEPTH5: "raw_depth"}
KIND_OF_TOPIC = {v: k for k, v in TOPIC_OF_KIND.items()}

Message = tuple[str, dict]  # (topic, value)


class Bus(Protocol):
    async def publish(self, topic: str, key: str, value: dict) -> None: ...

    async def subscribe(self, topics: list[str], group: str) -> AsyncIterator[Message]: ...

    async def close(self) -> None: ...


def check_topics(topics) -> None:
    unknown = sorted(set(topics) - set(TOPICS))
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown topic(s) {unknown}; topics are {list(TOPICS)}")


def stable_prediction_id(symbol: str, ts_ms: int, model_version: str) -> str:
    """Same tick, same model -> same id, so a replayed or restarted scorer can't double-count."""
    return hashlib.sha1(f"{symbol}|{ts_ms}|{model_version}".encode()).hexdigest()[:32]


_CLOSED = object()


class _Subscription:
    def __init__(self, topics: frozenset[str], maxsize: int, bus: InMemoryBus) -> None:
        self.topics = topics
        self.queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=maxsize + 1)  # +1 slot for the close marker
        self.maxsize = maxsize
        self.bus = bus
        self._holding = False  # a message was handed out and the consumer has not asked for the next

    def put(self, topic: str, value: dict) -> None:
        if self.queue.qsize() >= self.maxsize:
            self.queue.get_nowait()  # drop the oldest: for a live forecast the newest data matters
            self.queue.task_done()
            self.bus.drops[topic] = self.bus.drops.get(topic, 0) + 1
            if self.bus.drops[topic] in (1, 10, 100) or self.bus.drops[topic] % 1000 == 0:
                log.warning("bus queue full, dropped oldest", extra={"topic": topic,
                                                                    "dropped": self.bus.drops[topic]})
        self.queue.put_nowait((topic, value))

    def __aiter__(self) -> _Subscription:
        return self

    async def __anext__(self) -> Message:
        # The previous message counts as done only now that its consumer is back for more, so
        # ``drain()`` waits for messages to be handled, not just taken off the queue.
        if self._holding:
            self._holding = False
            self.queue.task_done()
        item = await self.queue.get()
        if item is _CLOSED:
            self.queue.task_done()
            raise StopAsyncIteration
        self._holding = True
        return item


class InMemoryBus:
    """Bounded per-subscriber queues; on overflow the oldest message is dropped and counted."""

    def __init__(self, maxsize: int = 10_000) -> None:
        if maxsize < 1:
            raise ValueError("maxsize must be >= 1")
        self.maxsize = maxsize
        self.drops: dict[str, int] = {}
        self._subs: list[_Subscription] = []
        self._closed = False

    async def publish(self, topic: str, key: str, value: dict) -> None:
        self.publish_nowait(topic, key, value)

    def publish_nowait(self, topic: str, key: str, value: dict) -> None:
        check_topics([topic])
        if self._closed:
            raise RuntimeError("bus is closed")
        for sub in self._subs:
            if topic in sub.topics:
                sub.put(topic, value)

    async def subscribe(self, topics: list[str], group: str) -> _Subscription:
        check_topics(topics)
        sub = _Subscription(frozenset(topics), self.maxsize, self)
        self._subs.append(sub)
        return sub

    async def drain(self) -> None:
        """Wait until every subscriber has taken every message published so far."""
        await asyncio.gather(*(s.queue.join() for s in self._subs))

    async def close(self) -> None:
        """Subscribers get what was already published, then their iteration ends."""
        self._closed = True
        for sub in self._subs:
            sub.queue.put_nowait(_CLOSED)


=== src\sentinalops\live\feed.py ===
"""Binance feed for the live pipeline: the recorder's connection handling, publishing to the bus.

Reuses ``Recorder`` whole: reconnects with backoff, the stale-feed watchdog, the 23-hour
connection rotation, de-duplication by sequence id, and the gap log. Every de-duplicated row is
handed to ``on_row``, queued (bounded; the oldest is dropped if the bus stalls) and published to
``raw_trades`` / ``raw_depth``. With ``record=True`` the rows are also archived to Parquet, so a
live machine can double as a backup recorder.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextlib

import websockets

from sentinalops.data.recorder import Recorder, RecorderConfig
from sentinalops.live.bus import TOPIC_OF_KIND, Bus
from sentinalops.logging import get_logger

log = get_logger("sentinalops.live.feed")


class BinanceFeed:
    def __init__(self, bus: Bus, rec_cfg: RecorderConfig, record: bool = False,
                 connect=websockets.connect, maxsize: int = 10_000) -> None:
        self.bus = bus
        self.recorder = Recorder(rec_cfg, connect=connect, on_row=self._on_row, record=record)
        self._queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=maxsize)
        self.feed_drops = 0
        self.published = 0

    def _on_row(self, kind: str, row: dict) -> None:
        if self._queue.full():
            self._queue.get_nowait()  # a stalled bus must not grow memory: drop the oldest row
            self.feed_drops += 1
            if self.feed_drops in (1, 10, 100) or self.feed_drops % 1000 == 0:
                log.warning("feed queue full, dropped oldest row", extra={"dropped": self.feed_drops})
        self._queue.put_nowait((kind, row))

    async def _pump(self) -> None:
        while True:
            kind, row = await self._queue.get()
            await self.bus.publish(TOPIC_OF_KIND[kind], row["symbol"], row)
            self.published += 1

    def request_stop(self) -> None:
        self.recorder.request_stop()

    async def run(self) -> None:
        pump = asyncio.create_task(self._pump())
        try:
            await self.recorder.run()
            while not self._queue.empty() and not pump.done():  # deliver what was already received
                await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
        finally:
            pump.cancel()
            with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):
                await pump


=== src\sentinalops\live\health.py ===
"""Health lines for the live processes (one JSON log line every ``--health-interval-s``)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

HEAL_KINDS = ("heal_attempt", "promote", "rejected", "rollback", "shadow_end")


def last_heal_event(registry_root: str | Path) -> dict | None:
    """The newest heal-related event in ``registry.json`` plus the current champion (read-only)."""
    path = Path(registry_root) / "registry.json"
    try:
        state = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None
    events = [e for e in state.get("events", []) if e.get("kind") in HEAL_KINDS]
    if not events:
        return {"champion": state.get("champion")}
    return {**events[-1], "champion": state.get("champion")}


def pipeline_health(processor, verifier, stats, bus, feed, registry_root) -> dict:
    out: dict = {"published": dict(stats.published), "ledger_predictions": stats.ledger_predictions,
                 "ledger_outcomes": stats.ledger_outcomes, "ledger_errors": stats.ledger_errors,
                 "worker_errors": stats.worker_errors, "bus_drops": dict(getattr(bus, "drops", {}))}
    if processor is not None:
        p = processor.stats
        out.update(events=dict(p.events), late=dict(p.late), ticks=p.ticks, feature_ticks=p.feature_ticks,
                   suspended_ticks=p.suspended_ticks, predictions=p.predictions,
                   shadow_predictions=p.shadow_predictions, scoring_errors=p.scoring_errors,
                   model_version=stats.last_model_version, last_probability=stats.last_probability)
    if verifier is not None:
        out.update(outcomes=dict(verifier.stats.outcomes), verifier_late=verifier.stats.late)
    if feed is not None:
        r = feed.recorder.stats
        out.update(feed_published=feed.published, feed_drops=feed.feed_drops, reconnects=r.reconnects,
                   gaps=r.gaps_written, duplicates=r.duplicates, malformed=r.malformed,
                   hook_errors=r.hook_errors)
    out["heal"] = last_heal_event(registry_root)
    return out


=== src\sentinalops\live\heal_service.py ===
"""The heal service: the self-healing loop as its own process (``python -m sentinalops.live --role heal``).

Every ``interval_min`` it runs ``HealController.tick(now)``, with the training table read from the
prediction ledger (``heal/ledger_table.py``). It never touches the bus: it reads the ledger and
writes the file registry, and the live pipeline hot-reloads ``champion.joblib`` on promotion.

**Bootstrap and manual upgrades.** If ``champion.joblib`` is not the registry's champion (no
registry yet, or the file was replaced by hand, e.g. ``python -m sentinalops.training.train``),
it is registered as a new version and promoted, with the holdout AP the training command stamped
on it as the reference for degradation. Without that stamp the reference is NaN and the loop
will never call the model degraded, so a warning is logged.

Retraining runs single-threaded (the CLI caps OpenMP/BLAS threads) so the live pipeline keeps a CPU.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import joblib

from sentinalops.config import DomainConfig, Settings
from sentinalops.heal.controller import HealController, TickResult
from sentinalops.heal.ledger_table import ledger_table
from sentinalops.heal.registry import ModelRegistry
from sentinalops.live.runner import _install_stop
from sentinalops.logging import get_logger
from sentinalops.serving.ledger import Ledger

log = get_logger("sentinalops.heal.service")
DAY_MS = 86_400_000


class HealService:
    def __init__(self, cfg: DomainConfig, ledger: Ledger, registry: ModelRegistry, replay_days: float = 7,
                 seed: int = 0) -> None:
        self.cfg, self.ledger, self.registry = cfg, ledger, registry
        lookback = int(cfg.retrain_window_hours * 3_600_000 + replay_days * DAY_MS)
        names = cfg.feature_names
        self.controller = HealController(cfg, ledger, registry,
                                         lambda now: ledger_table(ledger, now - lookback, now, names), seed)
        self.last: TickResult | None = None

    def bootstrap_if_needed(self, now_ms: int) -> str | None:
        """Register and promote ``champion.joblib`` if the registry does not know it yet."""
        path = self.registry.champion_path
        if not path.exists():
            return None
        state = self.registry.state()
        model = joblib.load(path)
        if state.champion is not None and getattr(model, "sentinal_version_", None) == state.champion:
            return None
        ap = float(getattr(model, "sentinal_holdout_ap_", math.nan))
        if math.isnan(ap):
            log.warning("champion has no holdout AP: the heal loop cannot detect degradation",
                        extra={"path": str(path)})
        reason = "bootstrap" if state.champion is None else "manual install"
        version = self.registry.register(model, {
            "holdout_ap": ap, "source": reason, "model_name": getattr(model, "sentinal_model_name_", None),
            "params": getattr(model, "sentinal_model_params_", {})}, now_ms)
        self.registry.promote(version, now_ms, reason, shadow_ms=0)
        return version

    def tick(self, now_ms: int) -> TickResult:
        self.bootstrap_if_needed(now_ms)
        self.last = self.controller.tick(now_ms)
        return self.last

    def health(self) -> dict:
        s, r = self.registry.state(), self.last
        d = r.decision if r else None
        return {"champion": s.champion, "shadow": s.shadow, "action": r.action if r else None,
                "reason": d.reason if d else None, "matured": d.matured if d else None,
                "live_ap": d.degradation.current.ap if d and d.degradation else None,
                "promotions": self.registry.count("promote"), "rollbacks": self.registry.count("rollback")}


async def run_heal(
    cfg: DomainConfig, settings: Settings, interval_min: float, health_interval_s: float
) -> None:
    ledger = Ledger(Path(settings.data_dir) / "ledger.sqlite")
    service = HealService(cfg, ledger, ModelRegistry(Path(settings.model_path).parent))
    stop = asyncio.Event()
    _install_stop(stop)
    log.info("heal service started", extra={"interval_min": interval_min,
                                            "registry": str(Path(settings.model_path).parent)})
    while not stop.is_set():
        try:
            await asyncio.to_thread(service.tick, int(time.time() * 1000))
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 - a failed cycle is logged; the next one runs anyway
            log.error("heal cycle failed", extra={"error": repr(e)})
        log.info("heal health", extra=service.health())
        try:
            await asyncio.wait_for(stop.wait(), timeout=interval_min * 60)
        except TimeoutError:
            pass
    log.info("heal service stopped", extra=service.health())


=== src\sentinalops\live\kafka_bus.py ===
"""Kafka / Redpanda transport for the live bus (``--bus kafka``; needs ``pip install -e '.[stream]'``).

Same contract as ``InMemoryBus``: JSON values keyed by symbol, every subscriber group sees every
message on its topics, in order per topic (one partition per topic). Differences:

- Components can run as separate processes (``--role feed|features|outcomes|ledger``).
- Consumers use a group per role and commit offsets, so a restarted role resumes where it left off.
  Delivery is at least once; the ledger and the deterministic prediction ids make replays harmless.
- A new group starts at the newest message (``auto_offset_reset='latest'``): a live forecaster has
  no use for a backlog from before it existed.
- ``subscribe`` returns only after partitions are assigned, so nothing published afterwards is missed.
- ``close`` lets each subscriber read up to the end offsets it saw at close time, then ends it.

Topics are created on first use with one partition; raw topics keep at most ``raw_retention_bytes``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json

from sentinalops.live.bus import TOPICS, Message, check_topics
from sentinalops.logging import get_logger

log = get_logger("sentinalops.live.kafka")
RAW_TOPICS = ("raw_trades", "raw_depth")


def encode(value: dict) -> bytes:
    return json.dumps(value, separators=(",", ":")).encode()


def decode(raw: bytes) -> dict:
    return json.loads(raw)


def _aiokafka():
    try:
        import aiokafka
        import aiokafka.admin
    except ImportError as e:
        raise RuntimeError("the Kafka bus needs aiokafka: pip install -e '.[stream]'") from e
    return aiokafka


class _KafkaSubscription:
    def __init__(self, bus: KafkaBus, consumer) -> None:
        self.bus, self.consumer = bus, consumer
        self._buffer: list[Message] = []
        self._stop_at: dict | None = None  # end offsets captured when the bus closed
        self._positions: dict = {}

    def __aiter__(self) -> _KafkaSubscription:
        return self

    async def __anext__(self) -> Message:
        while not self._buffer:
            if self.bus.closing and self._stop_at is None:
                self._stop_at = await self.consumer.end_offsets(list(self.consumer.assignment()))
            if self._stop_at is not None:
                self._positions = {tp: await self.consumer.position(tp) for tp in self._stop_at}
                if all(self._positions[tp] >= end for tp, end in self._stop_at.items()):
                    await self.consumer.stop()
                    raise StopAsyncIteration
            batches = await self.consumer.getmany(timeout_ms=200)
            for tp, records in batches.items():
                self._buffer += [(tp.topic, decode(r.value)) for r in records]
        return self._buffer.pop(0)


class KafkaBus:
    def __init__(self, broker: str, client_id: str = "sentinalops",
                 raw_retention_bytes: int = 5 * 2**30) -> None:
        self._ak = _aiokafka()
        self.broker, self.client_id = broker, client_id
        self.raw_retention_bytes = raw_retention_bytes
        self.closing = False
        self.drops: dict[str, int] = {}  # the in-memory overflow counter has no Kafka equivalent
        self._producer = None
        self._consumers: list = []
        self._topics_ready = False
        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()

    async def _ensure_topics(self) -> None:
        if self._topics_ready:
            return
        admin = self._ak.admin.AIOKafkaAdminClient(bootstrap_servers=self.broker, client_id=self.client_id)
        await admin.start()
        try:
            existing = set(await admin.list_topics())
            raw_cfg = {"retention.bytes": str(self.raw_retention_bytes)}
            new = [self._ak.admin.NewTopic(t, num_partitions=1, replication_factor=1,
                                           topic_configs=raw_cfg if t in RAW_TOPICS else {})
                   for t in TOPICS if t not in existing]
            if new:
                await admin.create_topics(new)
                log.info("kafka topics created", extra={"topics": [t.name for t in new]})
        finally:
            await admin.close()
        self._topics_ready = True

    async def publish(self, topic: str, key: str, value: dict) -> None:
        check_topics([topic])
        async with self._lock:
            if self._producer is None:
                await self._ensure_topics()
                self._producer = self._ak.AIOKafkaProducer(bootstrap_servers=self.broker, acks="all",
                                                           client_id=self.client_id)
                await self._producer.start()
        await self._producer.send_and_wait(topic, encode(value), key=key.encode())

    async def subscribe(self, topics: list[str], group: str) -> _KafkaSubscription:
        check_topics(topics)
        await self._ensure_topics()
        assigned = asyncio.Event()

        class _Listener(self._ak.ConsumerRebalanceListener):
            async def on_partitions_revoked(self, revoked):
                pass

            async def on_partitions_assigned(self, partitions):
                if partitions:
                    assigned.set()

        consumer = self._ak.AIOKafkaConsumer(
            bootstrap_servers=self.broker, group_id=f"{self.client_id}-{group}", client_id=self.client_id,
            auto_offset_reset="latest", enable_auto_commit=True)
        await consumer.start()
        consumer.subscribe(topics=list(topics), listener=_Listener())
        sub = _KafkaSubscription(self, consumer)
        while not assigned.is_set():  # polling drives the group join; keep whatever arrives
            batches = await consumer.getmany(timeout_ms=100)
            for tp, records in batches.items():
                sub._buffer += [(tp.topic, decode(r.value)) for r in records]
        self._consumers.append(consumer)
        return sub

    async def close(self) -> None:
        self.closing = True
        if self._producer is not None:
            await self._producer.stop()


=== src\sentinalops\live\pipeline.py ===
"""Live pipeline workers: thin async loops around the existing processor, verifier and ledger.

```
raw_trades, raw_depth â”€â”€â–º features_worker (StreamProcessor) â”€â”€â–º features, predictions
raw_depth            â”€â”€â–º outcomes_worker (OutcomeVerifier)  â”€â”€â–º outcomes
predictions, outcomes â”€â”€â–º ledger_writer (Ledger, SQLite)
clock: advance(now) on processor and verifier, so ticks keep coming while the feed is silent
```

No feature or label logic lives here; it is all in ``StreamProcessor`` / ``OutcomeVerifier``,
which the online/offline consistency tests already guard. A ``predictions`` message carries the
features it was scored on, so the ledger writer never joins topics (Kafka does not order across
topics). Prediction ids are deterministic per (symbol, tick, model version).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from sentinalops.live.bus import KIND_OF_TOPIC, Bus, stable_prediction_id
from sentinalops.logging import get_logger
from sentinalops.schemas import FeatureTick, OutcomeLabel, StressPrediction
from sentinalops.serving.ledger import Ledger
from sentinalops.stream.processor import FEATURES_TOPIC, PREDICTIONS_TOPIC, StreamProcessor
from sentinalops.stream.verifier import OUTCOMES_TOPIC, OutcomeVerifier

log = get_logger("sentinalops.live")


@dataclass
class PipelineStats:
    published: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    ledger_predictions: int = 0
    ledger_outcomes: int = 0
    ledger_errors: int = 0
    worker_errors: int = 0
    last_probability: float | None = None
    last_model_version: str | None = None

    def count(self, topic: str) -> None:
        self.published[topic] = self.published.get(topic, 0) + 1


async def publish_processor_outputs(bus: Bus, outputs: list, stats: PipelineStats) -> None:
    """Publish a processor's outputs; predictions carry their tick's features and a stable id."""
    features: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}
    for topic, msg in outputs:
        if topic == FEATURES_TOPIC:
            assert isinstance(msg, FeatureTick)
            features[msg.ts_ms] = msg.features
            value = msg.model_dump(mode="json")
        else:
            assert topic == PREDICTIONS_TOPIC and isinstance(msg, StressPrediction)
            msg = msg.model_copy(update={
                "prediction_id": stable_prediction_id(msg.symbol, msg.ts_ms, msg.model_version)})
            value = {**msg.model_dump(mode="json"), "features": features.get(msg.ts_ms)}
            stats.last_probability, stats.last_model_version = msg.probability, msg.model_version
        await bus.publish(topic, msg.symbol, value)
        stats.count(topic)


async def publish_outcomes(bus: Bus, outcomes: list[OutcomeLabel], stats: PipelineStats) -> None:
    for o in outcomes:
        await bus.publish(OUTCOMES_TOPIC, o.symbol, o.model_dump(mode="json"))
        stats.count(OUTCOMES_TOPIC)


async def features_worker(bus: Bus, sub, processor: StreamProcessor, stats: PipelineStats) -> None:
    async for topic, row in sub:
        try:
            outputs = processor.on_event(KIND_OF_TOPIC[topic], row)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 - one bad row must not stop the pipeline
            stats.worker_errors += 1
            log.error("features worker failed on a row", extra={"error": repr(e)})
            continue
        await publish_processor_outputs(bus, outputs, stats)


async def outcomes_worker(bus: Bus, sub, verifier: OutcomeVerifier, stats: PipelineStats) -> None:
    async for topic, row in sub:
        try:
            outcomes = verifier.on_event(KIND_OF_TOPIC[topic], row)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            stats.worker_errors += 1
            log.error("outcomes worker failed on a row", extra={"error": repr(e)})
            continue
        await publish_outcomes(bus, outcomes, stats)


def _write_batch(ledger: Ledger, batch: list[tuple[str, dict]]) -> tuple[int, int]:
    preds, outs = [], []
    for topic, value in batch:
        if topic == PREDICTIONS_TOPIC:
            value = dict(value)
            features = value.pop("features", None)
            preds.append((StressPrediction(**value), features))
        else:
            outs.append(OutcomeLabel(**value))
    return ledger.add_many(preds, outs)


async def ledger_writer(sub, ledger: Ledger, stats: PipelineStats, max_batch: int = 1000) -> None:
    """Write what has arrived in one SQLite transaction, off the event loop.

    A pump moves messages into a bounded local queue; the writer takes everything waiting there
    (up to ``max_batch``) per transaction, so a burst costs one commit instead of one per message.
    """
    pending: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=10_000)
    end = object()

    async def pump() -> None:
        try:
            async for msg in sub:
                await pending.put(msg)
        finally:
            await pending.put(end)

    pump_task = asyncio.create_task(pump())
    done = False
    while not done:
        batch = [await pending.get()]
        while not pending.empty() and len(batch) < max_batch:
            batch.append(pending.get_nowait())
        if batch[-1] is end:
            batch.pop()
            done = True
        if not batch:
            continue
        try:
            added_p, added_o = await asyncio.to_thread(_write_batch, ledger, batch)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 - keep writing; the error is counted and logged
            stats.ledger_errors += 1
            log.error("ledger write failed", extra={"messages": len(batch), "error": repr(e)})
            continue
        stats.ledger_predictions += added_p
        stats.ledger_outcomes += added_o
    await pump_task


async def clock_step(bus: Bus, processor: StreamProcessor | None, verifier: OutcomeVerifier | None,
                     now_ms: int, stats: PipelineStats) -> None:
    """Move the watermarks to ``now_ms`` and publish whatever ticks became due (None = not here)."""
    if processor is not None:
        await publish_processor_outputs(bus, processor.advance(now_ms), stats)
    if verifier is not None:
        await publish_outcomes(bus, verifier.advance(now_ms), stats)


async def clock_loop(bus: Bus, processor: StreamProcessor | None, verifier: OutcomeVerifier | None,
                     now_ms: Callable[[], int], stats: PipelineStats, interval_s: float = 1.0) -> None:
    while True:
        await asyncio.sleep(interval_s)
        await clock_step(bus, processor, verifier, now_ms(), stats)


async def start_pipeline(
    bus: Bus,
    processor: StreamProcessor | None,
    verifier: OutcomeVerifier | None,
    ledger: Ledger | None,
    stats: PipelineStats | None = None,
) -> tuple[list[asyncio.Task], PipelineStats]:
    """Subscribe every worker first (so nothing published afterwards is missed), then start them.

    Pass ``None`` for a component another process runs (Kafka mode). The tasks end when the bus
    is closed.
    """
    stats = stats or PipelineStats()
    plan = []
    if processor is not None:
        plan.append((features_worker, ["raw_trades", "raw_depth"], "features", (bus,), (processor, stats)))
    if verifier is not None:
        plan.append((outcomes_worker, ["raw_depth"], "outcomes", (bus,), (verifier, stats)))
    if ledger is not None:
        plan.append((ledger_writer, [PREDICTIONS_TOPIC, OUTCOMES_TOPIC], "ledger", (), (ledger, stats)))
    subs = [await bus.subscribe(topics, group=group) for _, topics, group, _, _ in plan]
    tasks = [asyncio.create_task(fn(*pre, sub, *post), name=group)
             for (fn, _, group, pre, post), sub in zip(plan, subs, strict=True)]
    return tasks, stats


=== src\sentinalops\live\runner.py ===
"""Run the live pipeline (or one of its roles) until SIGINT/SIGTERM.

Roles: ``pipeline`` = feed + features + outcomes + ledger in one process. With the Kafka bus each
can also run alone (``feed``, ``features``, ``outcomes``, ``ledger``). The heal loop is always a
separate process (``live/heal_service.py``).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import contextlib
import signal
import time
from pathlib import Path

from sentinalops.config import DomainConfig, Settings
from sentinalops.data.recorder import RecorderConfig
from sentinalops.live.feed import BinanceFeed
from sentinalops.live.health import pipeline_health
from sentinalops.live.pipeline import clock_loop, start_pipeline
from sentinalops.logging import get_logger
from sentinalops.serving.ledger import Ledger
from sentinalops.serving.model import ChampionModel
from sentinalops.stream.processor import StreamProcessor
from sentinalops.stream.verifier import OutcomeVerifier

log = get_logger("sentinalops.live")
ROLES = {"pipeline": {"feed", "features", "outcomes", "ledger"}}


def make_bus(kind: str, settings: Settings):
    if kind == "kafka":
        from sentinalops.live.kafka_bus import KafkaBus

        return KafkaBus(settings.kafka_broker)
    from sentinalops.live.bus import InMemoryBus

    return InMemoryBus()


def now_ms() -> int:
    return int(time.time() * 1000)


def _install_stop(stop: asyncio.Event) -> None:
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    for name in ("SIGINT", "SIGTERM"):
        sig = getattr(signal, name, None)
        if sig is None:
            continue
        try:
            loop.add_signal_handler(sig, stop.set)
        except NotImplementedError:  # Windows
            signal.signal(sig, lambda *_: loop.call_soon_threadsafe(stop.set))


async def run_live(role: str, bus_kind: str, cfg: DomainConfig, settings: Settings, data_dir: Path,
                   record: bool, health_interval_s: float) -> None:
    roles = ROLES.get(role, {role})
    names = cfg.feature_names
    registry_root = Path(settings.model_path).parent
    bus = make_bus(bus_kind, settings)
    proc = verifier = ledger = feed = None
    if "features" in roles:
        proc = StreamProcessor(cfg, model=ChampionModel(settings.model_path, names),
                               shadow=ChampionModel(registry_root / "shadow.joblib", names))
    if "outcomes" in roles:
        verifier = OutcomeVerifier(cfg)
    if "ledger" in roles:
        ledger = Ledger(Path(settings.data_dir) / "ledger.sqlite")
    tasks, stats = await start_pipeline(bus, proc, verifier, ledger)
    if "feed" in roles:
        feed = BinanceFeed(bus, RecorderConfig(symbols=(cfg.symbol,), data_dir=data_dir), record=record)

    stop = asyncio.Event()
    _install_stop(stop)
    side = []
    if proc is not None or verifier is not None:
        side.append(asyncio.create_task(clock_loop(bus, proc, verifier, now_ms, stats)))

    async def health() -> None:
        while True:
            await asyncio.sleep(health_interval_s)
            log.info("live health", extra=pipeline_health(proc, verifier, stats, bus, feed, registry_root))

    side.append(asyncio.create_task(health()))
    feed_task = asyncio.create_task(feed.run()) if feed is not None else None
    log.info("live started", extra={"role": role, "bus": bus_kind, "roles": sorted(roles),
                                    "model_path": str(settings.model_path), "record": record})
    waiters = [asyncio.create_task(stop.wait())] + ([feed_task] if feed_task else [])
    await asyncio.wait(waiters, return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED)

    if feed is not None:
        feed.request_stop()
        await feed_task
    for t in side:
        t.cancel()
    await asyncio.gather(*side, return_exceptions=True)
    await bus.close()
    with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
        await asyncio.wait_for(asyncio.gather(*tasks), 30)
    log.info("live stopped", extra=pipeline_health(proc, verifier, stats, bus, feed, registry_root))


=== src\sentinalops\live\__init__.py ===
"""Live wiring: Binance feed -> bus -> features/predictions/outcomes -> ledger, and the heal service."""


=== src\sentinalops\live\__main__.py ===
"""``python -m sentinalops.live``: the live pipeline, or the heal service with ``--role heal``.

Only the standard library is imported at module level: the heal role must set its thread limits
before NumPy / scikit-learn create their thread pools.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import os
from pathlib import Path

THREAD_VARS = ("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS")
SPLIT_ROLES = ("feed", "features", "outcomes", "ledger")


def parse(argv: list[str] | None) -> argparse.Namespace:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m sentinalops.live", description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument("--role", default="pipeline", choices=["pipeline", "heal", *SPLIT_ROLES])
    ap.add_argument("--bus", default="memory", choices=["memory", "kafka"])
    ap.add_argument("--record", action="store_true", help="also archive raw rows to Parquet")
    ap.add_argument("--data-dir", default="data/live", type=Path,
                    help="feed state, gap log (and Parquet with --record)")
    ap.add_argument("--health-interval-s", type=float, default=60.0)
    ap.add_argument("--heal-interval-min", type=float, default=5.0)
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    if args.bus == "memory" and args.role in SPLIT_ROLES:
        ap.error(f"--role {args.role} runs only with --bus kafka; on the memory bus use --role pipeline")
    return args


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
    args = parse(argv)
    if args.role == "heal":
        for var in THREAD_VARS:  # before any NumPy / scikit-learn import
            os.environ.setdefault(var, "1")

    from sentinalops.config import Settings, get_domain_config
    from sentinalops.logging import configure_logging

    settings = Settings.from_env()
    configure_logging(settings.log_level)
    cfg = get_domain_config(settings.domain_config)
    if args.role == "heal":
        from sentinalops.live.heal_service import run_heal

        asyncio.run(run_heal(cfg, settings, args.heal_interval_min, args.health_interval_s))
    else:
        from sentinalops.live.runner import run_live

        asyncio.run(run_live(args.role, args.bus, cfg, settings, args.data_dir, args.record,
                             args.health_interval_s))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== src\sentinalops\models\base.py ===
"""The model contract: what every model package must provide (see ``docs/models.md``).

A **recipe** knows how to train one kind of model. Its ``fit`` returns a **fitted model** that
the rest of the system uses without knowing what is inside:

- ``predict_proba(X: DataFrame) -> ndarray (n, 2)``; column 1 is the stress probability.
- ``classes_ == [0, 1]`` and ``feature_names_in_ == cfg.feature_names`` (the 34 raw features,
  in order). The API and stream processor refuse a model with other inputs.
- a writable ``sentinal_version_`` (the registry stamps it), and the recipe's own stamp:
  ``sentinal_model_name_`` and ``sentinal_model_params_``. The heal loop reads these to retrain
  the champion with its own recipe.

Released packages are frozen: pickles refer to their classes by import path, so changing a
released package can break loading older registry versions. An upgrade is a new package.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Protocol

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    import pandas as pd

    from sentinalops.config import DomainConfig


class Recipe(Protocol):
    name: str

    def fit(
        self, train: pd.DataFrame, cfg: DomainConfig, seed: int = 0, params: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    ) -> Any: ...


def stamp(model: Any, name: str, params: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
    """Record which recipe (and which parameters) produced ``model``. Returns ``model``."""
    model.sentinal_model_name_ = name
    model.sentinal_model_params_ = dict(params)
    return model


def check_fitted(model: Any, cfg: DomainConfig) -> None:
    """Raise ``ValueError`` unless ``model`` satisfies the contract for ``cfg``."""
    if not callable(getattr(model, "predict_proba", None)):
        raise ValueError("model has no predict_proba")
    names = list(getattr(model, "feature_names_in_", []))
    if names != list(cfg.feature_names):
        raise ValueError("model feature_names_in_ do not match the configured features")
    classes = [int(c) for c in getattr(model, "classes_", [])]
    if classes != [0, 1]:
        raise ValueError(f"model classes_ must be [0, 1], got {classes}")


=== src\sentinalops\models\__init__.py ===
"""Model packages and the recipe registry: the one place a model is plugged in.

Add a model: create ``sentinalops/models/<name>/`` exposing ``recipe`` (see ``base.Recipe``),
then add one line to ``_RECIPES``. Names map to import paths and are imported only when used,
so listing them (the config validator does) stays cheap.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from importlib import import_module
from typing import Any

from sentinalops.models.base import Recipe

_RECIPES: dict[str, str] = {
    "gbm_v1": "sentinalops.models.gbm_v1:recipe",
}


def list_recipes() -> list[str]:
    return sorted(_RECIPES)


def register_recipe(name: str, import_path: str) -> None:
    """Make ``name`` resolvable to ``"module:attribute"`` (for plug-ins outside this package)."""
    if ":" not in import_path:
        raise ValueError("import_path must look like 'package.module:attribute'")
    _RECIPES[name] = import_path


def get_recipe(name: str) -> Recipe:
    try:
        module, _, attr = _RECIPES[name].partition(":")
    except KeyError:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown model {name!r}; known models: {list_recipes()}") from None
    return getattr(import_module(module), attr)


def model_name(model: Any) -> str | None:
    """The recipe that produced ``model``, or None for models trained before the model slot."""
    return getattr(model, "sentinal_model_name_", None)


=== src\sentinalops\models\gbm_v1\__init__.py ===
"""gbm_v1: the original champion, a scikit-learn HistGradientBoostingClassifier.

Frozen: do not change this package once models trained with it are in a registry. Make
``gbm_v2`` instead. The fitted model is the plain scikit-learn estimator (plus the recipe stamp),
so its pickles depend only on scikit-learn.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

import pandas as pd
from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingClassifier

from sentinalops.config import DomainConfig
from sentinalops.models.base import stamp

NAME = "gbm_v1"
DEFAULTS: dict[str, Any] = {"max_iter": 200, "learning_rate": 0.08}


class _Recipe:
    name = NAME

    def fit(
        self, train: pd.DataFrame, cfg: DomainConfig, seed: int = 0, params: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    ) -> HistGradientBoostingClassifier:
        params = dict(params or {})
        model = HistGradientBoostingClassifier(**{**DEFAULTS, **params}, random_state=seed)
        model.fit(train[cfg.feature_names], train["label"].astype(int))
        return stamp(model, NAME, params)


recipe = _Recipe()


=== src\sentinalops\serving\app.py ===
"""FastAPI inference service: /score, /outcomes, /metrics, /health.

``POST /score`` takes one feature tick and returns the probability that liquidity stress
starts within the horizon. Each prediction is stored in the prediction ledger
(``data_dir/ledger.sqlite``, chunk 34). ``POST /outcomes`` stores the verified outcome of an
earlier tick there too.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import time

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from prometheus_client import CollectorRegistry, Counter, Histogram, make_asgi_app

from sentinalops.config import Settings, get_domain_config
from sentinalops.logging import configure_logging, get_logger
from sentinalops.schemas import OutcomeLabel, ScoreRequest, StressPrediction
from sentinalops.serving.ledger import Ledger
from sentinalops.serving.model import ChampionModel, ModelUnavailable


def create_app(settings: Settings | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    settings = settings or Settings.from_env()
    configure_logging(settings.log_level)
    log = get_logger("sentinalops.api")
    cfg = get_domain_config(settings.domain_config)
    threshold = settings.effective_threshold(cfg)
    feature_order = cfg.feature_names
    expected = set(feature_order)

    registry = CollectorRegistry()
    prob_h = Histogram("sentinalops_stress_probability", "Forecast stress probability",
                       buckets=[i / 10 for i in range(11)], registry=registry)
    alerts_c = Counter("sentinalops_alerts_total", "Ticks scored at or above the alert threshold",
                       registry=registry)
    outcomes_c = Counter("sentinalops_outcomes_total", "Verified outcomes received", ["label"],
                         registry=registry)

    app = FastAPI(title="SentinalOps", version="0.2.0")
    app.mount("/metrics", make_asgi_app(registry=registry))

    champion = ChampionModel(settings.model_path, feature_order)

    ledger = Ledger(settings.data_dir / "ledger.sqlite")

    @app.post("/score", response_model=StressPrediction)
    def score(req: ScoreRequest) -> StressPrediction:
        if req.symbol != cfg.symbol:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=f"this service scores {cfg.symbol} only")
        missing, extra = sorted(expected - req.features.keys()), sorted(req.features.keys() - expected)
        if missing or extra:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail={"missing": missing, "unexpected": extra})
        try:
            p, version = champion.predict(req.features)
        except ModelUnavailable as e:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=str(e)) from e
        pred = StressPrediction(symbol=req.symbol, ts_ms=req.ts_ms, horizon_s=cfg.label_horizon_seconds,
                                probability=p, alert=p >= threshold, threshold=threshold,
                                model_version=version)
        prob_h.observe(p)
        if pred.alert:
            alerts_c.inc()
        ledger.add_prediction(pred, req.features)
        log.info("scored", extra={"ts_ms": req.ts_ms, "probability": round(p, 4), "alert": pred.alert})
        return pred

    @app.post("/outcomes")
    def outcomes(outcome: OutcomeLabel) -> dict:
        label = "void" if outcome.label is None else str(outcome.label)
        outcomes_c.labels(label).inc()
        ledger.add_outcome(outcome)
        log.info("outcome recorded", extra={"ts_ms": outcome.ts_ms, "label": label})
        return {"status": "recorded", "ts_ms": outcome.ts_ms, "label": outcome.label}

    @app.get("/health")
    def health() -> dict:
        return {"status": "ok", "model_loaded": settings.model_path.exists(), "domain": cfg.domain,
                "symbol": cfg.symbol, "horizon_s": cfg.label_horizon_seconds,
                "alert_threshold": threshold, "ts": time.time()}

    return app


=== src\sentinalops\serving\ledger.py ===
"""Prediction ledger (chunk 34): every prediction, its outcome once matured, and the scores.

SQLite, append-only: two insert-only tables, nothing is ever updated or deleted.

- ``predictions``: one row per ``StressPrediction`` plus the feature snapshot it was scored on.
- ``outcomes``: one row per verified tick (``OutcomeLabel``), whoever produced it.

A prediction's outcome is the outcome row with the same ``symbol``, ``ts_ms`` and
``horizon_s``. Writes are idempotent (a replayed message is ignored), so the ledger can be fed
from a topic that delivers at least once.

Each matured prediction is one of: ``hit`` (alert, stress came), ``miss`` (no alert, stress
came), ``false_alarm``, ``correct_negative``, or ``void`` (outcome unknowable; excluded from
every metric). Predictions whose outcome has not arrived yet are ``pending``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import sqlite3
from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from sentinalops.schemas import OutcomeLabel, StressPrediction

_SCHEMA = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS predictions (
    prediction_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    symbol        TEXT    NOT NULL,
    ts_ms         INTEGER NOT NULL,
    horizon_s     INTEGER NOT NULL,
    probability   REAL    NOT NULL,
    alert         INTEGER NOT NULL,
    threshold     REAL    NOT NULL,
    model_version TEXT    NOT NULL,
    scored_at_ms  INTEGER NOT NULL,
    features      TEXT
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS predictions_time ON predictions (symbol, ts_ms);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS outcomes (
    symbol         TEXT    NOT NULL,
    ts_ms          INTEGER NOT NULL,
    horizon_s      INTEGER NOT NULL,
    label          INTEGER,
    void_reason    TEXT,
    source         TEXT    NOT NULL,
    verified_at_ms INTEGER NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (symbol, ts_ms, horizon_s)
);
CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS predictions_append_only BEFORE UPDATE ON predictions
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'ledger is append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS predictions_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON predictions
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'ledger is append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS outcomes_append_only BEFORE UPDATE ON outcomes
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'ledger is append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS outcomes_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON outcomes
BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'ledger is append-only'); END;
"""

_JOINED = """
SELECT p.prediction_id, p.symbol, p.ts_ms, p.horizon_s, p.probability, p.alert, p.threshold,
       p.model_version, p.scored_at_ms, p.features, o.label, o.void_reason, o.verified_at_ms,
       o.symbol IS NOT NULL AS matured
FROM predictions p
LEFT JOIN outcomes o ON o.symbol = p.symbol AND o.ts_ms = p.ts_ms AND o.horizon_s = p.horizon_s
"""


def classify(alert: bool, label: int | None, matured: bool) -> str:
    if not matured:
        return "pending"
    if label is None:
        return "void"
    if alert:
        return "hit" if label == 1 else "false_alarm"
    return "miss" if label == 1 else "correct_negative"


@dataclass
class Scores:
    """Confusion table and probability scores over matured, non-void predictions."""

    hit: int = 0
    miss: int = 0
    false_alarm: int = 0
    correct_negative: int = 0
    void: int = 0
    pending: int = 0
    brier: float = math.nan
    base_rate: float = math.nan

    @property
    def scored(self) -> int:
        return self.hit + self.miss + self.false_alarm + self.correct_negative

    @property
    def precision(self) -> float:
        n = self.hit + self.false_alarm
        return self.hit / n if n else math.nan

    @property
    def recall(self) -> float:
        n = self.hit + self.miss
        return self.hit / n if n else math.nan

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {**asdict(self), "scored": self.scored, "precision": self.precision, "recall": self.recall}


class Ledger:
    def __init__(self, path: str | Path) -> None:
        self.path = Path(path)
        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            # WAL: readers never block the writer, and the API and the live pipeline can both write
            # (a second writer waits up to the 30 s timeout instead of failing).
            db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
            db.executescript(_SCHEMA)

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        # One short-lived connection per call: safe from FastAPI's thread pool and async consumers.
        db = sqlite3.connect(self.path, timeout=30)
        db.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL")  # safe with WAL; a crash can lose only the last commit
        db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        return db

    # ---- writes (34.1, 34.3) -------------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_prediction(self, pred: StressPrediction, features: dict[str, float] | None = None) -> bool:
        """Store a prediction. Returns False if this ``prediction_id`` was already stored."""
        with closing(self._connect()) as db, db:
            cur = db.execute(
                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO predictions VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                (pred.prediction_id, pred.symbol, pred.ts_ms, pred.horizon_s, pred.probability,
                 int(pred.alert), pred.threshold, pred.model_version, pred.scored_at_ms,
                 json.dumps(features) if features is not None else None),
            )
            return cur.rowcount == 1

    def add_predictions(self, preds: list[StressPrediction]) -> int:
        """Store many predictions in one transaction (no feature snapshots). Returns rows added."""
        with closing(self._connect()) as db, db:
            cur = db.executemany(
                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO predictions VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                [(p.prediction_id, p.symbol, p.ts_ms, p.horizon_s, p.probability, int(p.alert),
                  p.threshold, p.model_version, p.scored_at_ms, None) for p in preds],
            )
            return cur.rowcount

    def add_many(
        self,
        predictions: list[tuple[StressPrediction, dict[str, float] | None]],
        outcomes: list[OutcomeLabel],
    ) -> tuple[int, int]:
        """Predictions (with feature snapshots) and outcomes in one transaction. Returns rows added."""
        with closing(self._connect()) as db, db:
            before = db.total_changes
            db.executemany(
                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO predictions VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                [(p.prediction_id, p.symbol, p.ts_ms, p.horizon_s, p.probability, int(p.alert),
                  p.threshold, p.model_version, p.scored_at_ms,
                  json.dumps(f) if f is not None else None) for p, f in predictions],
            )
            added_p = db.total_changes - before
            db.executemany(
                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO outcomes VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                [(o.symbol, o.ts_ms, o.horizon_s, o.label, o.void_reason, o.source, o.verified_at_ms)
                 for o in outcomes],
            )
            return added_p, db.total_changes - before - added_p

    def add_outcome(self, outcome: OutcomeLabel) -> bool:
        """Store a verified outcome. The first outcome for a tick wins; replays are ignored."""
        with closing(self._connect()) as db, db:
            cur = db.execute(
                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO outcomes VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                (outcome.symbol, outcome.ts_ms, outcome.horizon_s, outcome.label, outcome.void_reason,
                 outcome.source, outcome.verified_at_ms),
            )
            return cur.rowcount == 1

    def add_outcomes(self, outcomes: list[OutcomeLabel]) -> int:
        with closing(self._connect()) as db, db:
            cur = db.executemany(
                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO outcomes VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                [(o.symbol, o.ts_ms, o.horizon_s, o.label, o.void_reason, o.source, o.verified_at_ms)
                 for o in outcomes],
            )
            return cur.rowcount

    # ---- reads (34.5, 34.6) --------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _rows(self, where: str = "", params: tuple = (), with_features: bool = True) -> list[dict]:
        query = _JOINED if with_features else _JOINED.replace("p.features,", "NULL AS features,")
        with closing(self._connect()) as db:
            rows = db.execute(f"{query} {where} ORDER BY p.ts_ms, p.prediction_id", params).fetchall()
        out = []
        for r in rows:
            d = dict(r)
            d["alert"], d["matured"] = bool(d["alert"]), bool(d["matured"])
            d["features"] = json.loads(d["features"]) if d["features"] else None
            d["result"] = classify(d["alert"], d["label"], d["matured"])
            out.append(d)
        return out

    def get(self, prediction_id: str) -> dict | None:
        rows = self._rows("WHERE p.prediction_id = ?", (prediction_id,))
        return rows[0] if rows else None

    def predictions(self, since_ms: int = 0, until_ms: int | None = None,
                    model_version: str | None = None, with_features: bool = True) -> list[dict]:
        """Joined prediction rows; ``with_features=False`` skips reading and parsing the snapshots."""
        clauses, params = ["p.ts_ms >= ?"], [since_ms]
        if until_ms is not None:
            clauses.append("p.ts_ms < ?")
            params.append(until_ms)
        if model_version is not None:
            clauses.append("p.model_version = ?")
            params.append(model_version)
        return self._rows("WHERE " + " AND ".join(clauses), tuple(params), with_features)

    def scores(self, since_ms: int = 0, until_ms: int | None = None,
               model_version: str | None = None) -> Scores:
        """Confusion table and Brier score for predictions made in ``[since_ms, until_ms)``."""
        s = Scores()
        sq_err, positives = 0.0, 0
        for r in self.predictions(since_ms, until_ms, model_version):
            setattr(s, r["result"], getattr(s, r["result"]) + 1)
            if r["result"] in ("pending", "void"):
                continue
            sq_err += (r["probability"] - r["label"]) ** 2
            positives += r["label"]
        if s.scored:
            s.brier = sq_err / s.scored
            s.base_rate = positives / s.scored
        return s


=== src\sentinalops\serving\model.py ===
"""The promoted champion, shared by the API and the stream processor.

Reloads whenever the model file changes (the 'heal' step: promotion swaps the file) and
refuses a model whose inputs differ from the configured features.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import threading
from pathlib import Path

import joblib
import pandas as pd


class ModelUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    """No servable model: the file is missing or expects different inputs."""


class ChampionModel:
    def __init__(self, path: str | Path, feature_order: list[str]) -> None:
        self.path = Path(path)
        self.feature_order = list(feature_order)
        self._model = None
        self._mtime = 0.0
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    @property
    def available(self) -> bool:
        return self.path.exists()

    def current(self) -> tuple[object, str]:
        """(model, version), reloading if the file changed. Raises ``ModelUnavailable``."""
        if not self.path.exists():
            raise ModelUnavailable("model not available")
        mtime = self.path.stat().st_mtime
        with self._lock:
            if self._model is None or mtime != self._mtime:
                model = joblib.load(self.path)
                if list(getattr(model, "feature_names_in_", [])) != self.feature_order:
                    raise ModelUnavailable("model inputs do not match the configured features")
                self._model, self._mtime = model, mtime
            # The registry stamps a version on the model object, so it always matches the file read.
            return self._model, getattr(self._model, "sentinal_version_", f"champion-{int(self._mtime)}")

    def predict(self, features: dict[str, float]) -> tuple[float, str]:
        """Stress probability for one feature dict, and the model version that produced it."""
        model, version = self.current()
        row = pd.DataFrame([[features[k] for k in self.feature_order]], columns=self.feature_order)
        return float(model.predict_proba(row)[:, 1][0]), version


=== src\sentinalops\serving\__init__.py ===

=== src\sentinalops\simulator\heal_sim.py ===
"""Run the heal loop against a simulated market (synthetic regimes, one tick per second).

Stands in for the live stack in tests and the demo: each step scores the step's ticks with
the served champion (and the shadow model, if any) exactly as the stream processor would,
records predictions in the ledger, records outcomes once their horizon has passed, then calls
``HealController.tick``. The result is the timeline of what the loop did.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import pandas as pd

from sentinalops.config import DomainConfig
from sentinalops.heal.controller import HealController, TickResult
from sentinalops.heal.monitor import window_scores
from sentinalops.heal.registry import ModelRegistry
from sentinalops.schemas import OutcomeLabel, StressPrediction
from sentinalops.serving.ledger import Ledger
from sentinalops.serving.model import ChampionModel
from sentinalops.simulator.regimes import make_ticks

MINUTE_MS = 60_000


def market(segments: list[tuple[str, int]], start_ms: int, seed: int = 0) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A training-table-shaped timeline: ``[(regime, minutes), ...]`` at one tick per second."""
    parts, t = [], start_ms
    for i, (regime, minutes) in enumerate(segments):
        n = minutes * 60
        df = make_ticks(regime, n, seed=seed + 101 * i)
        df.insert(0, "ts_ms", t + 1000 * pd.RangeIndex(n).to_numpy())
        df.insert(0, "symbol", "BTCUSDT")
        df["label_status"], df["regime"], df["source"] = "ok", regime, "synthetic"
        parts.append(df)
        t += n * 1000
    return pd.concat(parts, ignore_index=True)


@dataclass
class Step:
    now_ms: int
    regime: str
    champion: str | None
    live_ap: float
    result: TickResult


def run(
    cfg: DomainConfig,
    table: pd.DataFrame,
    registry: ModelRegistry,
    ledger: Ledger,
    controller: HealController,
    step_minutes: int = 5,
) -> list[Step]:
    names, h = cfg.feature_names, cfg.label_horizon_seconds * 1000
    champion = ChampionModel(registry.champion_path, names)
    shadow = ChampionModel(registry.shadow_path, names)
    threshold = cfg.alert_threshold
    start, end = int(table["ts_ms"].min()), int(table["ts_ms"].max()) + 1000
    matured_until = start
    steps: list[Step] = []
    for lo in range(start, end, step_minutes * MINUTE_MS):
        hi = min(lo + step_minutes * MINUTE_MS, end)
        chunk = table[(table["ts_ms"] >= lo) & (table["ts_ms"] < hi)]
        preds = []
        for served in (champion, shadow):
            if not served.available:
                continue
            model, version = served.current()
            probs = model.predict_proba(chunk[names])[:, 1]
            preds += [
                StressPrediction(symbol="BTCUSDT", ts_ms=int(ts), horizon_s=h // 1000, probability=float(p),
                                 alert=bool(p >= threshold), threshold=threshold, model_version=version)
                for ts, p in zip(chunk["ts_ms"], probs, strict=True)
            ]
        ledger.add_predictions(preds)
        # outcomes are known once the horizon has passed
        matured = table[(table["ts_ms"] >= matured_until) & (table["ts_ms"] + h < hi)]
        ledger.add_outcomes([
            OutcomeLabel(symbol="BTCUSDT", ts_ms=int(ts), horizon_s=h // 1000, label=int(y))
            for ts, y in zip(matured["ts_ms"], matured["label"], strict=True)
        ])
        matured_until = hi - h
        result = controller.tick(hi)
        state = registry.state()
        live = window_scores(ledger, hi - cfg.heal.metrics_window_minutes * MINUTE_MS, hi, state.champion)
        steps.append(Step(hi, str(chunk["regime"].iloc[-1]), state.champion, live.ap, result))
    return steps


def matured_table(table: pd.DataFrame, horizon_s: int):
    """The ``load_table`` callable for the controller: rows whose label has matured by ``now_ms``."""
    return lambda now_ms: table[table["ts_ms"] + horizon_s * 1000 < now_ms]


=== src\sentinalops\simulator\regimes.py ===
"""Synthetic feature ticks for two market regimes (chunk 3): the stand-in for recorded data.

Each row is one feature tick with the columns of ``FEATURE_ORDER`` plus ``label`` (1 if
liquidity stress starts within the horizon). The two regimes differ in *why* stress happens,
which is the shift the self-healing loop has to survive:

- **calm**: stress is preceded by the book thinning out. Top-5 depth falls over the last
  30-60 s and the spread widens; trading is quiet and balanced.
- **volatile**: stress is preceded by aggressive one-sided trading. Trade count and volume
  burst in the last 1-5 s, trade imbalance goes to one side and realized volatility jumps,
  while depth changes carry no information (they are noisy, and the book thins out often
  without stress following).

A model trained only on calm data watches the wrong features in the volatile regime. That is
the thesis test in ``tests/test_thesis.py``. The numbers are plausible for BTCUSDT but not
fitted to anything; real recorded data replaces this once chunk 12 exists.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from sentinalops.features.microstructure import FEATURE_ORDER, WINDOWS_S

REGIMES = ("calm", "volatile")
BASE_RATE = {"calm": 0.03, "volatile": 0.07}


def _window_frac(w: int, scale: float) -> float:
    """How much of a slow build-up a window of ``w`` seconds sees (0 for short, ~1 for long)."""
    return 1 - np.exp(-w / scale)


def make_ticks(regime: str = "calm", n: int = 20_000, seed: int = 0) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """``n`` shuffled feature ticks from ``regime``, with columns ``FEATURE_ORDER + ['label']``."""
    if regime not in REGIMES:
        raise ValueError(f"regime must be one of {REGIMES}, got {regime!r}")
    if n < 1:
        raise ValueError("n must be positive")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    volatile = regime == "volatile"

    y = rng.binomial(1, BASE_RATE[regime], n)
    # Precursor strength. Markets are noisy: a fifth of stress onsets come with no warning,
    # warnings vary in strength, and a few calm-ahead ticks show a warning that fizzles out.
    warned = rng.binomial(1, 0.8, n) * rng.uniform(0.2, 1.4, n)
    false_alarm = rng.binomial(1, 0.03, n) * rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0, n)
    s = np.where(y == 1, warned, false_alarm)
    side = rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0], n)  # direction of a one-sided burst

    # --- book precursors (calm regime) ------------------------------------------------
    if volatile:
        # The book thins out often and for no reason; stress does not depend on it.
