        thinning = rng.binomial(1, 0.25, n) * rng.uniform(0.1, 0.5, n)
    else:
        thinning = 0.4 * s
    depth_noise = 0.25 if volatile else 0.08

    depth_ref = rng.lognormal(np.log(12.0), 0.35, n)  # BTC on the top five levels
    depth_top5 = depth_ref * np.clip(1 - thinning + rng.normal(0, depth_noise / 2, n), 0.05, None)
    book_imb = np.clip(rng.normal(0, 0.25, n) + (0.4 * side * s if volatile else 0), -0.99, 0.99)
    bid_depth = depth_top5 * (1 + book_imb) / 2
    ask_depth = depth_top5 - bid_depth
    l1_imb = np.clip(book_imb + rng.normal(0, 0.3, n), -1, 1)

    spread_ref = 1.2e-7 * (3.0 if volatile else 1.0)
    spread_up = (0.0 if volatile else 1.2 * s) + (0.3 * thinning if volatile else 0.0)
    rel_spread = spread_ref * rng.lognormal(0, 0.25, n) * (1 + spread_up)

    cols: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {
        "rel_spread": rel_spread,
        "bid_depth": bid_depth,
        "ask_depth": ask_depth,
        "depth_top5": depth_top5,
        "depth_imbalance": book_imb,
        "l1_imbalance": l1_imb,
    }

    # --- trade precursors (volatile regime) -------------------------------------------
    base_rate = 9.0 if volatile else 3.0  # trades per second
    burst = 3.0 * s if volatile else 0.0
    base_vol = 0.004 if volatile else 0.0015  # per-sqrt-second mid volatility (log units)
    for w in WINDOWS_S:
        recent = 1 - _window_frac(w, 6.0)  # a burst dominates short windows, washes out of long ones
        build = _window_frac(w, 25.0)  # thinning shows up in longer windows
        rate = base_rate * (1 + burst * (0.3 + 0.7 * recent)) * rng.lognormal(0, 0.3, n)
        count = rng.poisson(rate * w)
        cols[f"trade_intensity_{w}s"] = count / w
        cols[f"trade_volume_{w}s"] = count * rng.lognormal(np.log(0.05), 0.5, n)
        imb = rng.normal(0, 0.3 / np.sqrt(w), n) + (0.7 * side * s * (0.4 + 0.6 * recent) if volatile else 0)
        cols[f"trade_imbalance_{w}s"] = np.where(count > 0, np.clip(imb, -1, 1), 0.0)
        vol_mult = 1 + (2.5 * s * (0.3 + 0.7 * recent) if volatile else 0)
        cols[f"realized_vol_{w}s"] = base_vol * np.sqrt(w) * vol_mult * rng.lognormal(0, 0.3, n)
        cols[f"mid_return_{w}s"] = rng.normal(0, base_vol * np.sqrt(w), n) + (
            side * s * base_vol * 3 * np.sqrt(w) if volatile else 0
        )
        cols[f"depth_change_{w}s"] = -thinning * build + rng.normal(0, depth_noise * np.sqrt(build), n)
        cols[f"spread_change_{w}s"] = rel_spread - rel_spread / (1 + spread_up * build) + rng.normal(
            0, spread_ref * 0.1, n
        )

    df = pd.DataFrame(cols)[FEATURE_ORDER]
    df["label"] = y
    return df


def example_ticks(seed: int = 0) -> tuple[dict[str, float], dict[str, float]]:
    """One clearly calm tick and one clearly pre-stress tick from the calm regime, for demos."""
    df = make_ticks("calm", 4_000, seed)
    calm = df[df["label"] == 0].iloc[0]
    stressed = df[df["label"] == 1].sort_values("depth_change_60s").iloc[0]
    return (
        {k: float(calm[k]) for k in FEATURE_ORDER},
        {k: float(stressed[k]) for k in FEATURE_ORDER},
    )


=== src\sentinalops\simulator\__init__.py ===

=== src\sentinalops\stream\processor.py ===
"""Stream processor (chunk 19): raw trades and depth in, feature ticks and stress predictions out.

The processor is transport-agnostic: feed it events with ``on_trade`` / ``on_depth`` (rows in
the recorder schema) and it returns what to publish. ``run`` wires it to any async event source.

Event time is ``recv_time_ms``, the same clock the offline pipeline uses.

**When a tick is emitted.** Tick ``t`` (a whole multiple of the cadence) is computed once the
watermark, the newest receive time seen on either stream, reaches ``t + allowed_lateness_ms``.
Waiting a little lets the two streams, which arrive through separate topics, catch up with
each other. ``advance(now_ms)`` moves the watermark with the wall clock, so ticks keep
coming (and come out invalid) when the feed goes silent.

**Late events (task 19.5).** An event whose receive time is at or before the last emitted
tick arrived too late to be counted in it. It is dropped and counted in ``stats.late``;
offline it would have been included, so ``late`` must stay at zero for online and offline
features to agree. Out-of-order events that are not late are inserted in order.

**Suspension (task 18.5).** ``compute_tick`` returns nothing while the book is stale and
until the feed has been continuous for the longest window again, so a stale feed yields no
feature ticks and no predictions, and recovery needs a full warm-up. No extra logic.

**Buffers (task 19.2).** Trades are kept for the longest window. Depth is kept for the longest
window plus the one snapshot just before it, which is exactly what ``compute_tick`` needs to
give the same answer as on the full history (tested; dropping that snapshot breaks the test).
A further ``stale_ms`` of depth is kept as a safety margin.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import bisect
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Awaitable, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import pandas as pd

from sentinalops.config import DomainConfig
from sentinalops.data.recorder.messages import AGG_TRADE, DEPTH5, DEPTH_SCHEMA, TRADE_SCHEMA
from sentinalops.features.microstructure import compute_tick
from sentinalops.logging import get_logger
from sentinalops.schemas import FeatureTick, StressPrediction
from sentinalops.serving.model import ChampionModel, ModelUnavailable

log = get_logger("sentinalops.stream")

FEATURES_TOPIC = "features"
PREDICTIONS_TOPIC = "predictions"
Output = tuple[str, FeatureTick | StressPrediction]


@dataclass
class ProcessorStats:
    events: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=lambda: {AGG_TRADE: 0, DEPTH5: 0})
    late: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=lambda: {AGG_TRADE: 0, DEPTH5: 0})
    other_symbol: int = 0
    ticks: int = 0
    feature_ticks: int = 0
    suspended_ticks: int = 0  # ticks with no valid features: stale feed or warm-up
    predictions: int = 0
    shadow_predictions: int = 0
    scoring_errors: int = 0
    last_tick_ms: int | None = None


class _Buffer:
    """Rows of one stream ordered by receive time."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.times: list[int] = []
        self.rows: list[dict] = []

    def insert(self, row: dict) -> None:
        t = row["recv_time_ms"]
        if not self.times or t >= self.times[-1]:
            self.times.append(t)
            self.rows.append(row)
        else:
            i = bisect.bisect_right(self.times, t)
            self.times.insert(i, t)
            self.rows.insert(i, row)

    def drop_through(self, cutoff_ms: int, keep_one_before: bool) -> None:
        """Forget rows with time <= cutoff (keeping the newest such row if asked)."""
        n = bisect.bisect_right(self.times, cutoff_ms)
        if keep_one_before:
            n = max(n - 1, 0)
        if n:
            del self.times[:n]
            del self.rows[:n]

    def frame(self, columns: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
        return pd.DataFrame(self.rows, columns=columns)


class StreamProcessor:
    def __init__(
        self,
        cfg: DomainConfig,
        model: ChampionModel | None = None,
        allowed_lateness_ms: int = 500,
        shadow: ChampionModel | None = None,
        alert_threshold: float | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if allowed_lateness_ms < 0:
            raise ValueError("allowed_lateness_ms must be >= 0")
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.params = cfg.feature_params()
        self.model = model
        self.shadow = shadow  # the previous champion during a shadow period (chunk 45)
        self.lateness = allowed_lateness_ms
        self.threshold = cfg.alert_threshold if alert_threshold is None else alert_threshold
        self.step = self.params.cadence_s * 1000
        self.trades, self.depth = _Buffer(), _Buffer()
        self.watermark: int | None = None
        self.next_tick: int | None = None
        self.stats = ProcessorStats()

    # ---- input -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def on_trade(self, row: dict) -> list[Output]:
        return self._on_event(AGG_TRADE, row)

    def on_depth(self, row: dict) -> list[Output]:
        return self._on_event(DEPTH5, row)

    def on_event(self, kind: str, row: dict) -> list[Output]:
        if kind not in (AGG_TRADE, DEPTH5):
            raise ValueError(f"unknown stream kind {kind!r}")
        return self._on_event(kind, row)

    def _on_event(self, kind: str, row: dict) -> list[Output]:
        if row.get("symbol") != self.cfg.symbol:
            self.stats.other_symbol += 1
            return []
        t = int(row["recv_time_ms"])
        self.stats.events[kind] += 1
        if self.stats.last_tick_ms is not None and t <= self.stats.last_tick_ms:
            self.stats.late[kind] += 1
            return []
        (self.trades if kind == AGG_TRADE else self.depth).insert(row)
        if self.next_tick is None:
            self.next_tick = -(-t // self.step) * self.step  # first whole tick at or after the first event
        return self.advance(t)

    # ---- clock -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def advance(self, now_ms: int) -> list[Output]:
        """Move the watermark to ``now_ms`` (if later) and emit every tick that is now due."""
        if self.watermark is None or now_ms > self.watermark:
            self.watermark = now_ms
        out: list[Output] = []
        while self.next_tick is not None and self.next_tick + self.lateness <= self.watermark:
            out += self._emit(self.next_tick)
            self.next_tick += self.step
        return out

    def _emit(self, ts_ms: int) -> list[Output]:
        self.stats.ticks += 1
        self.stats.last_tick_ms = ts_ms
        # Forget what this tick (and so every later one) can no longer use.
        self.trades.drop_through(ts_ms - self.params.max_window_ms, keep_one_before=False)
        self.depth.drop_through(ts_ms - self.params.buffer_ms, keep_one_before=True)

        feats = self._features(ts_ms)
        if feats is None:
            self.stats.suspended_ticks += 1
            return []
        tick = FeatureTick(symbol=self.cfg.symbol, ts_ms=ts_ms, features=feats)
        self.stats.feature_ticks += 1
        out: list[Output] = [(FEATURES_TOPIC, tick)]
        if self.model is not None:
            try:
                p, version = self.model.predict(feats)
            except ModelUnavailable as e:
                self.stats.scoring_errors += 1
                log.warning("cannot score", extra={"ts_ms": ts_ms, "error": str(e)})
                return out
            out.append((PREDICTIONS_TOPIC, self._prediction(ts_ms, p, version)))
            self.stats.predictions += 1
        if self.shadow is not None and self.shadow.available:
            try:  # shadow scores go to the ledger under their own version; alerting uses the champion's
                p, version = self.shadow.predict(feats)
                out.append((PREDICTIONS_TOPIC, self._prediction(ts_ms, p, version)))
                self.stats.shadow_predictions += 1
            except ModelUnavailable:
                pass
        return out

    def _prediction(self, ts_ms: int, p: float, version: str) -> StressPrediction:
        return StressPrediction(
            symbol=self.cfg.symbol, ts_ms=ts_ms, horizon_s=self.cfg.label_horizon_seconds,
            probability=p, alert=p >= self.threshold, threshold=self.threshold, model_version=version,
        )

    def _features(self, ts_ms: int) -> dict[str, float] | None:
        if not self.depth.rows:
            return None
        depth = self.depth.frame(DEPTH_SCHEMA.names)
        trades = self.trades.frame(TRADE_SCHEMA.names)
        tick = compute_tick(depth, trades, ts_ms, self.params)
        if tick is None:
            return None
        return {name: tick[name] for name in self.params.names}


# ---- wiring ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Publish = Callable[[str, str, dict], Awaitable[None]]  # (topic, key, value)


async def run(
    processor: StreamProcessor, events: AsyncIterator[tuple[str, dict]], publish: Publish
) -> ProcessorStats:
    """Consume ``(kind, row)`` events until the source ends and publish every output keyed by symbol."""
    async for kind, row in events:
        for topic, msg in processor.on_event(kind, row):
            await publish(topic, msg.symbol, msg.model_dump(mode="json"))
    return processor.stats


=== src\sentinalops\stream\verifier.py ===
"""Outcome verifier (chunk 34): the live version of the chunk 9 label.

Fed the raw depth stream, it decides for every tick ``t`` whether liquidity stress started in
``(t, t + H]``, once tick ``t + H`` has been seen, and returns an ``OutcomeLabel``. It
re-implements ``labels.build_labels`` incrementally, step for step:

1. as-of book state per tick: newest snapshot with ``recv_time_ms <= t``; unknown if older
   than ``stale_ms`` (``labels.to_ticks``),
2. trailing reference: median of the previous ``reference_ticks`` ticks, ignoring unknown
   ticks, trusted once ``min_reference_ticks`` are known (``labels.add_stress``),
3. ``stressed`` and ``confirmed`` (stressed for ``confirm_ticks`` ticks in a row),
4. the onset label at ``t`` from ticks ``t+1 .. t+H`` (``labels.add_labels``).

The replay test checks it against ``build_labels`` and ``label_at`` tick by tick.

Void outcomes (``label=None``) carry a reason:

- ``already_stressed``: the market was stressed at ``t``; onset is not defined.
- ``gap``: the book was stale somewhere in ``[t - confirm_ticks + 1, t + H]``.
- ``warmup``: otherwise unknown, i.e. the trailing reference was not established yet.

Clock, watermark and late events follow the stream processor (``docs/streaming.md``).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import bisect
from collections import deque
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np

from sentinalops.config import DomainConfig
from sentinalops.data.recorder.messages import DEPTH5
from sentinalops.schemas import OutcomeLabel
from sentinalops.stream.processor import Publish

OUTCOMES_TOPIC = "outcomes"
NAN = float("nan")


@dataclass
class VerifierStats:
    snapshots: int = 0
    late: int = 0
    other_symbol: int = 0
    rejected_books: int = 0  # crossed or empty, ignored like labels.book_state does
    ticks: int = 0
    outcomes: dict[str, int] = field(
        default_factory=lambda: {"1": 0, "0": 0, "already_stressed": 0, "gap": 0, "warmup": 0}
    )
    last_tick_ms: int | None = None


@dataclass
class _Tick:
    ts_ms: int
    stale: bool
    stressed: float  # 0, 1 or NaN
    confirmed: float  # 0, 1 or NaN


class OutcomeVerifier:
    def __init__(self, cfg: DomainConfig, allowed_lateness_ms: int = 500) -> None:
        if allowed_lateness_ms < 0:
            raise ValueError("allowed_lateness_ms must be >= 0")
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.p = cfg.label_params()
        self.lateness = allowed_lateness_ms
        self.step = self.p.cadence_s * 1000
        self.min_ref = min(self.p.min_reference_ticks, self.p.reference_ticks)
        # snapshot buffer: (recv_time_ms, depth_top5, rel_spread)
        self._snap_t: list[int] = []
        self._snap_v: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
        self._ref_depth: deque[float] = deque(maxlen=self.p.reference_ticks)
        self._ref_spread: deque[float] = deque(maxlen=self.p.reference_ticks)
        self._recent_stressed: deque[float] = deque(maxlen=self.p.confirm_ticks)
        # ticks still needed: t - H (to resolve) .. now, plus confirm_ticks before for the gap reason
        self._history: deque[_Tick] = deque(maxlen=self.p.horizon_ticks + self.p.confirm_ticks + 1)
        self.watermark: int | None = None
        self.next_tick: int | None = None
        self.stats = VerifierStats()

    # ---- input -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def on_depth(self, row: dict) -> list[OutcomeLabel]:
        if row.get("symbol") != self.cfg.symbol:
            self.stats.other_symbol += 1
            return []
        t = int(row["recv_time_ms"])
        self.stats.snapshots += 1
        if self.stats.last_tick_ms is not None and t <= self.stats.last_tick_ms:
            self.stats.late += 1
            return []
        bid, ask = float(row["bid_px_1"]), float(row["ask_px_1"])
        total = sum(float(row[f"{s}_qty_{i}"]) for s in ("bid", "ask") for i in range(1, 6))
        if not (bid > 0 and ask > bid and total > 0):
            self.stats.rejected_books += 1
            return self.advance(t)
        mid = (bid + ask) / 2
        i = bisect.bisect_right(self._snap_t, t)
        self._snap_t.insert(i, t)
        self._snap_v.insert(i, (total, (ask - bid) / mid))
        if self.next_tick is None:
            self.next_tick = -(-t // self.step) * self.step
        return self.advance(t)

    def on_event(self, kind: str, row: dict) -> list[OutcomeLabel]:
        """Accepts the combined stream; only depth matters for the label."""
        return self.on_depth(row) if kind == DEPTH5 else []

    # ---- clock -------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def advance(self, now_ms: int) -> list[OutcomeLabel]:
        if self.watermark is None or now_ms > self.watermark:
            self.watermark = now_ms
        out: list[OutcomeLabel] = []
        while self.next_tick is not None and self.next_tick + self.lateness <= self.watermark:
            resolved = self._tick(self.next_tick)
            if resolved is not None:
                out.append(resolved)
            self.next_tick += self.step
        return out

    # ---- one tick ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _state(self, ts_ms: int) -> tuple[float, float, bool]:
        """(depth_top5, rel_spread, stale) as of ``ts_ms``; drops snapshots no later tick needs."""
        i = bisect.bisect_right(self._snap_t, ts_ms) - 1
        if i < 0:
            return NAN, NAN, True
        snap_t, (depth, spread) = self._snap_t[i], self._snap_v[i]
        del self._snap_t[:i], self._snap_v[:i]  # keep the as-of snapshot for the next tick
        if ts_ms - snap_t > self.p.stale_ms:
            return NAN, NAN, True
        return depth, spread, False

    def _reference(self, values: deque[float]) -> float:
        arr = np.fromiter(values, float, len(values))
        known = arr[~np.isnan(arr)]
        return float(np.median(known)) if len(known) >= self.min_ref else NAN

    def _tick(self, ts_ms: int) -> OutcomeLabel | None:
        self.stats.ticks += 1
        self.stats.last_tick_ms = ts_ms
        depth, spread, stale = self._state(ts_ms)

        ref_depth, ref_spread = self._reference(self._ref_depth), self._reference(self._ref_spread)
        self._ref_depth.append(depth)  # the reference for later ticks includes this one
        self._ref_spread.append(spread)
        depth_ratio, spread_ratio = depth / ref_depth, spread / ref_spread
        if np.isnan(depth_ratio) or np.isnan(spread_ratio):
            stressed = NAN
        else:
            hit = depth_ratio < self.p.depth_fraction or (
                self.p.use_spread and spread_ratio > self.p.spread_multiple
            )
            stressed = float(hit)
        self._recent_stressed.append(stressed)
        recent = list(self._recent_stressed)
        if len(recent) < self.p.confirm_ticks or any(np.isnan(recent)):
            confirmed = NAN
        else:
            confirmed = min(recent)
        self._history.append(_Tick(ts_ms, stale, stressed, confirmed))
        return self._resolve(ts_ms - self.p.horizon_ticks * self.step)

    def _resolve(self, t: int) -> OutcomeLabel | None:
        """Outcome of tick ``t`` now that tick ``t + H`` is known, or None if ``t`` predates the stream."""
        hist = list(self._history)
        idx = next((i for i, h in enumerate(hist) if h.ts_ms == t), None)
        if idx is None:
            return None
        at, future = hist[idx], hist[idx + 1 :]
        label: int | None
        if at.stressed == 1:
            label, reason = None, "already_stressed"
        elif np.isnan(at.stressed) or any(np.isnan(h.confirmed) for h in future):
            label = None
            window = hist[max(idx - self.p.confirm_ticks + 1, 0) :]
            reason = "gap" if any(h.stale for h in window) else "warmup"
        else:
            label, reason = int(any(h.confirmed == 1 for h in future)), None
        self.stats.outcomes[str(label) if label is not None else reason] += 1
        return OutcomeLabel(
            symbol=self.cfg.symbol, ts_ms=t, horizon_s=self.p.horizon_s, label=label, void_reason=reason
        )


async def run_verifier(
    verifier: OutcomeVerifier, events: AsyncIterator[tuple[str, dict]], publish: Publish
) -> VerifierStats:
    """Consume ``(kind, row)`` events and publish every outcome to ``outcomes`` keyed by symbol."""
    async for kind, row in events:
        for outcome in verifier.on_event(kind, row):
            await publish(OUTCOMES_TOPIC, outcome.symbol, outcome.model_dump(mode="json"))
    return verifier.stats


=== src\sentinalops\stream\__init__.py ===
"""Streaming path: stream processor and helpers (chunks 15-20)."""


=== src\sentinalops\training\train.py ===
"""Train any registered model by hand: ``python -m sentinalops.training.train``.

This is the manual upgrade path (``docs/models.md``). The model comes from ``--model`` or the
config's ``model.name``; the data is either synthetic regimes or a training table built by the
offline pipeline (``data/processed/features``).

- **Table data:** only ``label_status == 'ok'`` rows; the newest 20% is held out, after a gap of
  ``split_embargo_seconds`` so no training label looks into the holdout.
- **Synthetic data:** rows are independent draws, so a stratified random split is fine.

The holdout average precision is stamped on the model as ``sentinal_holdout_ap_``; the heal loop
uses it as the reference when this model becomes the champion.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path

import joblib
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.metrics import average_precision_score
from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split

from sentinalops.config import DEFAULT_DOMAIN_CONFIG, DomainConfig, load_domain_config
from sentinalops.models import get_recipe
from sentinalops.models.base import check_fitted

DEFAULT_OUT = Path("models/champion.joblib")


def time_split(table: pd.DataFrame, cfg: DomainConfig, holdout_fraction: float = 0.2):
    """(train, holdout): chronological, usable rows only, with the embargo between them."""
    ok = table[table["label_status"] == "ok"].sort_values("ts_ms")
    if ok.empty:
        raise ValueError("no usable rows (label_status == 'ok') in the table")
    cut = int(ok["ts_ms"].iloc[int(len(ok) * (1 - holdout_fraction))])
    train = ok[ok["ts_ms"] < cut - cfg.split_embargo_seconds * 1000]
    return train, ok[ok["ts_ms"] >= cut]


def load_data(data: str, rows: int) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if data == "synthetic":
        from sentinalops.simulator.regimes import make_ticks

        return make_ticks("calm", rows)
    from sentinalops.data.pipeline import read_table

    table = read_table(data)
    if table.empty:
        raise SystemExit(f"no training table under {data}")
    return table


def train(data: pd.DataFrame, cfg: DomainConfig, name: str, params: dict, seed: int = 0):
    """Fit ``name`` and return (model, holdout AP, training rows)."""
    if "ts_ms" in data and "label_status" in data:
        tr, ho = time_split(data, cfg)
    else:
        tr, ho = train_test_split(data, test_size=0.2, stratify=data["label"], random_state=seed)
    model = get_recipe(name).fit(tr, cfg, seed, params)
    check_fitted(model, cfg)
    y = ho["label"].astype(int)
    p = model.predict_proba(ho[cfg.feature_names])[:, 1]
    ap = float(average_precision_score(y, p)) if y.any() else float("nan")
    model.sentinal_holdout_ap_ = ap
    return model, ap, len(tr)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Train a registered model and save it as the champion file")
    ap.add_argument("--config", default=os.environ.get("DOMAIN_CONFIG", str(DEFAULT_DOMAIN_CONFIG)))
    ap.add_argument("--model", default=None, help="registered model name (default: config model.name)")
    ap.add_argument("--params", default=None, help="JSON object overriding the recipe's parameters")
    ap.add_argument("--data", default="synthetic", help="'synthetic' or a training-table directory")
    ap.add_argument("--rows", type=int, default=20_000, help="synthetic rows")
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=os.environ.get("MODEL_PATH", str(DEFAULT_OUT)))
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    cfg = load_domain_config(args.config)
    name = args.model or cfg.model.name
    params = json.loads(args.params) if args.params else dict(cfg.model.params)
    model, holdout_ap, n = train(load_data(args.data, args.rows), cfg, name, params, args.seed)

    out = Path(args.out)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    tmp = out.with_suffix(out.suffix + ".tmp")
    joblib.dump(model, tmp)
    os.replace(tmp, out)
    print(f"model={name} holdout_ap={holdout_ap:.4f} rows={n} out={out}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== src\sentinalops\training\train_baseline.py ===
"""Train the baseline stress-forecast champion on calm-regime ticks and save it.

Until recorded data flows through chunk 12, training uses the synthetic regimes in
``sentinalops.simulator.regimes``. The saved model is a fitted scikit-learn classifier whose
``feature_names_in_`` is ``FEATURE_ORDER``; the API refuses a model with different inputs.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path

import joblib
import pandas as pd
from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingClassifier
from sklearn.metrics import average_precision_score
from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split

from sentinalops.features.microstructure import FEATURE_ORDER
from sentinalops.simulator.regimes import make_ticks

MODEL_PATH = Path("models/champion.joblib")


def fit(df: pd.DataFrame, seed: int = 0) -> HistGradientBoostingClassifier:
    """The ``gbm_v1`` recipe on the default features (kept for existing callers)."""
    from types import SimpleNamespace

    from sentinalops.models import get_recipe

    return get_recipe("gbm_v1").fit(df, SimpleNamespace(feature_names=FEATURE_ORDER), seed)


def evaluate(model, df: pd.DataFrame) -> float:
    """Average precision (PR-AUC) of ``model`` on ``df``."""
    return float(average_precision_score(df["label"], model.predict_proba(df[FEATURE_ORDER])[:, 1]))


def train(df: pd.DataFrame, seed: int = 0) -> tuple[HistGradientBoostingClassifier, float]:
    """Fit on 80% of ``df`` and return the model with its average precision on the other 20%."""
    tr, te = train_test_split(df, test_size=0.2, stratify=df["label"], random_state=seed)
    model = fit(tr, seed)
    return model, evaluate(model, te)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
    ap_ = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap_.add_argument("--out", default=str(MODEL_PATH))
    ap_.add_argument("--rows", type=int, default=20_000)
    args = ap_.parse_args(argv)
    model, ap = train(make_ticks("calm", args.rows))
    out = Path(args.out)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    joblib.dump(model, out)
    print(f"champion saved to {out}  (calm-regime AP={ap:.3f})")
    try:
        import mlflow

        with mlflow.start_run(run_name="champion-baseline"):
            mlflow.log_metric("avg_precision", ap)
            mlflow.sklearn.log_model(model, "model")
    except Exception as e:  # MLflow is optional locally
        print(f"(mlflow skipped: {e.__class__.__name__})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

=== tests\pipeline_helpers.py ===
"""Synthetic recordings in the recorder's on-disk layout, for pipeline tests and the speed check."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from sentinalops.data.recorder.messages import AGG_TRADE, DEPTH5
from sentinalops.data.recorder.sink import ParquetSink

H0 = 1_763_200_800_000  # 2025-11-15T10:00:00Z, an hour boundary


def make_events(
    start_ms: int,
    seconds: int,
    *,
    symbol: str = "BTCUSDT",
    snapshot_ms: int = 500,
    trades_per_s: float = 2.0,
    stress: tuple[tuple[int, int], ...] = (),
    holes: tuple[tuple[int, int], ...] = (),
    seed: int = 0,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
    """(depth, trades) in the recorder schema.

    ``stress`` is a list of ``(offset_s, duration_s)`` episodes where the book thins to 20%.
    ``holes`` are ``(offset_s, duration_s)`` stretches with no events of either kind.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t = start_ms + np.arange(0, seconds * 1000, snapshot_ms, dtype=np.int64)
    t = t + rng.integers(0, 40, len(t))  # receive jitter
    n = len(t)
    thin = np.ones(n)
    for off, dur in stress:
        thin[(t >= start_ms + off * 1000) & (t < start_ms + (off + dur) * 1000)] = 0.2
    mid = 90_000 + np.cumsum(rng.normal(0, 0.5, n)).round(2)
    depth = {"symbol": symbol, "last_update_id": np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64) + 1_000, "recv_time_ms": t}
    for k in range(1, 6):
        depth[f"bid_px_{k}"] = mid - 0.005 - (k - 1) * 0.01
        depth[f"bid_qty_{k}"] = rng.uniform(1.0, 3.0, n) * thin
        depth[f"ask_px_{k}"] = mid + 0.005 + (k - 1) * 0.01
        depth[f"ask_qty_{k}"] = rng.uniform(1.0, 3.0, n) * thin
    depth = pd.DataFrame(depth)

    m = rng.poisson(trades_per_s * seconds)
    tt = np.sort(start_ms + rng.integers(0, seconds * 1000, m)).astype(np.int64)
    trades = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "symbol": symbol,
            "event_time_ms": tt - 5,
            "trade_time_ms": tt - 6,
            "agg_trade_id": np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64) + 50_000,
            "price": 90_000.0 + rng.normal(0, 5, m).round(2),
            "quantity": rng.uniform(0.001, 0.2, m),
            "first_trade_id": np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64) * 3,
            "last_trade_id": np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64) * 3 + 2,
            "buyer_is_maker": rng.random(m) < 0.5,
            "recv_time_ms": tt,
        }
    )
    for off, dur in holes:
        lo, hi = start_ms + off * 1000, start_ms + (off + dur) * 1000
        depth = depth[(depth.recv_time_ms < lo) | (depth.recv_time_ms >= hi)]
        trades = trades[(trades.recv_time_ms < lo) | (trades.recv_time_ms >= hi)]
    return depth.reset_index(drop=True), trades.reset_index(drop=True)


def write_recording(root: Path, depth: pd.DataFrame, trades: pd.DataFrame, chunk_s: int = 600) -> None:
    """Write through the real ``ParquetSink``, several files per hour like a live recorder."""
    sink = ParquetSink(root)
    for kind, df in ((DEPTH5, depth), (AGG_TRADE, trades)):
        if len(df) == 0:
            continue
        bucket = df["recv_time_ms"] // (chunk_s * 1000)
        for _, part in df.groupby(bucket, sort=True):
            for row in part.to_dict("records"):
                sink.add(kind, row)
            res = sink.flush()
            assert not res.errors, res.errors


=== tests\recorder_helpers.py ===
"""Fake Binance combined-stream server and message builders for recorder tests."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import time
from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable

import websockets

from sentinalops.data.recorder import Recorder, RecorderConfig

LOCAL = frozenset({"127.0.0.1"})


def trade_frame(i: int, symbol: str = "BTCUSDT") -> str:
    now = int(time.time() * 1000)
    data = {
        "e": "aggTrade",
        "E": now,
        "s": symbol,
        "a": i,
        "p": "100.50",
        "q": "0.25",
        "f": i * 2,
        "l": i * 2 + 1,
        "T": now - 1,
        "m": i % 2 == 0,
        "M": True,
    }
    return json.dumps({"stream": f"{symbol.lower()}@aggTrade", "data": data})


def depth_frame(i: int, symbol: str = "BTCUSDT") -> str:
    data = {
        "lastUpdateId": i,
        "bids": [[f"{100 - k * 0.01:.2f}", "1.5"] for k in range(5)],
        "asks": [[f"{100.01 + k * 0.01:.2f}", "2.5"] for k in range(5)],
    }
    return json.dumps({"stream": f"{symbol.lower()}@depth5@100ms", "data": data})


class FakeExchange:
    """Local WebSocket server. ``script(ws, connection_number)`` drives each connection."""

    def __init__(self, script: Callable[[object, int], Awaitable[None]]) -> None:
        self.script = script
        self.connections = 0
        self.port = 0
        self._server = None
        self._handlers: set[asyncio.Task] = set()

    async def _handler(self, ws, *_):
        self.connections += 1
        self._handlers.add(asyncio.current_task())
        try:
            await self.script(ws, self.connections)
        except websockets.ConnectionClosed:
            pass
        finally:
            self._handlers.discard(asyncio.current_task())

    async def __aenter__(self) -> FakeExchange:
        self._server = await websockets.serve(self._handler, "127.0.0.1", 0)
        self.port = self._server.sockets[0].getsockname()[1]
        return self

    async def __aexit__(self, *exc) -> None:
        for task in list(self._handlers):  # scripts that sleep must not hold up teardown
            task.cancel()
        self._server.close()
        await asyncio.wait_for(self._server.wait_closed(), 10)

    @property
    def base_url(self) -> str:
        return f"ws://127.0.0.1:{self.port}"


def make_recorder(tmp_path, exchange: FakeExchange, **overrides) -> Recorder:
    cfg = dict(
        symbols=("BTCUSDT",),
        data_dir=tmp_path,
        base_url=exchange.base_url,
        allowed_hosts=LOCAL,
        flush_interval_s=3600,
        stale_after_s=5,
        backoff_base_s=0.02,
        backoff_max_s=0.05,
        stats_interval_s=3600,
        open_timeout_s=5,
    )
    cfg.update(overrides)
    return Recorder(RecorderConfig(**cfg))


async def run_until(recorder: Recorder, condition: Callable[[], bool], timeout: float = 15.0) -> None:
    """Run the recorder until ``condition()`` is true, then stop it gracefully."""
    task = asyncio.create_task(recorder.run())
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    try:
        while not condition():
            if task.done():
                task.result()
                raise AssertionError("recorder exited before the condition was met")
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                raise AssertionError(f"timed out; stats={recorder.stats}")
            await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
    finally:
        recorder.request_stop()
        await asyncio.wait_for(task, 10)


=== tests\test_api.py ===
import os

import joblib
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from sentinalops.config import Settings
from sentinalops.serving.app import create_app
from sentinalops.serving.ledger import Ledger
from sentinalops.simulator.regimes import example_ticks, make_ticks
from sentinalops.training.train_baseline import fit

TS = 1_763_000_000_000


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def champion():
    return fit(make_ticks("calm", 8_000, seed=3))


@pytest.fixture
def client(tmp_path, champion):
    joblib.dump(champion, tmp_path / "m.joblib")
    settings = Settings(model_path=tmp_path / "m.joblib", data_dir=tmp_path / "data")
    return TestClient(create_app(settings)), tmp_path


def _body(features, ts_ms=TS, symbol="BTCUSDT"):
    return {"symbol": symbol, "ts_ms": ts_ms, "features": features}


def test_health(client):
    c, _ = client
    r = c.get("/health").json()
    assert r["status"] == "ok" and r["model_loaded"] is True
    assert r["domain"] == "market_liquidity" and r["symbol"] == "BTCUSDT" and r["horizon_s"] == 30


def test_score_calm_and_stressed_ticks_and_ledger(client):
    c, tmp = client
    calm, stressed = example_ticks()
    r_calm = c.post("/score", json=_body(calm)).json()
    r_stress = c.post("/score", json=_body(stressed, ts_ms=TS + 1000)).json()
    assert r_calm["probability"] < 0.2 and r_calm["alert"] is False
    assert r_stress["probability"] > 0.5 and r_stress["alert"] is True
    assert r_calm["horizon_s"] == 30 and r_calm["model_version"].startswith("champion-")
    rows = Ledger(tmp / "data" / "ledger.sqlite").predictions()
    assert [r["ts_ms"] for r in rows] == [TS, TS + 1000] and rows[0]["result"] == "pending"
    assert rows[0]["prediction_id"] == r_calm["prediction_id"] and rows[0]["features"] == calm


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "mutate",
    [
        lambda f: {k: v for k, v in f.items() if k != "rel_spread"},  # missing feature
        lambda f: {**f, "bogus": 1.0},  # unexpected feature
        lambda f: {},
    ],
)
def test_score_rejects_wrong_feature_sets(client, mutate):
    c, _ = client
    calm, _ = example_ticks()
    assert c.post("/score", json=_body(mutate(calm))).status_code == 422


def test_score_rejects_other_symbols_and_bad_bodies(client):
    c, _ = client
    calm, _ = example_ticks()
    assert c.post("/score", json=_body(calm, symbol="ETHUSDT")).status_code == 422
    assert c.post("/score", json={"files": {}, "meta": {"name": "x"}}).status_code == 422


def test_outcomes_and_metrics(client):
    c, tmp = client
    _, stressed = example_ticks()
    c.post("/score", json=_body(stressed))
    ok = {"symbol": "BTCUSDT", "ts_ms": TS, "horizon_s": 30, "label": 1}
    assert c.post("/outcomes", json=ok).status_code == 200
    void = {"symbol": "BTCUSDT", "ts_ms": TS + 1000, "horizon_s": 30, "label": None, "void_reason": "gap"}
    assert c.post("/outcomes", json=void).status_code == 200
    assert c.post("/outcomes", json={**ok, "label": None}).status_code == 422
    rows = Ledger(tmp / "data" / "ledger.sqlite").predictions()
    assert [(r["label"], r["result"]) for r in rows] == [(1, "hit")]
    text = c.get("/metrics/").text
    assert "sentinalops_stress_probability_count 1.0" in text
    assert "sentinalops_alerts_total 1.0" in text
    assert 'sentinalops_outcomes_total{label="1"} 1.0' in text
    assert 'sentinalops_outcomes_total{label="void"} 1.0' in text


def test_missing_model_returns_503(tmp_path):
    c = TestClient(create_app(Settings(model_path=tmp_path / "nope.joblib", data_dir=tmp_path)))
    assert c.get("/health").json()["model_loaded"] is False
    assert c.post("/score", json=_body(example_ticks()[0])).status_code == 503


def test_model_with_other_inputs_is_refused(tmp_path, champion):
    """An old champion (e.g. the supply-chain model) must not be served against liquidity features."""
    df = make_ticks("calm", 500, seed=4).rename(columns={"rel_spread": "base64_entropy"})
    from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression

    wrong = LogisticRegression().fit(df.drop(columns="label"), df["label"])
    joblib.dump(wrong, tmp_path / "m.joblib")
    c = TestClient(create_app(Settings(model_path=tmp_path / "m.joblib", data_dir=tmp_path)))
    r = c.post("/score", json=_body(example_ticks()[0]))
    assert r.status_code == 503 and "do not match" in r.json()["detail"]


def test_hot_reload_picks_up_new_champion(tmp_path, champion):
    path = tmp_path / "m.joblib"
    joblib.dump(champion, path)
    c = TestClient(create_app(Settings(model_path=path, data_dir=tmp_path)))
    calm, _ = example_ticks()
    v1 = c.post("/score", json=_body(calm)).json()["model_version"]
    os.utime(path, (path.stat().st_atime, path.stat().st_mtime + 10))
    v2 = c.post("/score", json=_body(calm)).json()["model_version"]
    assert v1 != v2


=== tests\test_config.py ===
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
import yaml
from pydantic import ValidationError

from sentinalops.config import DomainConfig, Settings, get_domain_config, load_domain_config
from sentinalops.features.microstructure import FEATURE_ORDER
from sentinalops.labels import LabelParams

REPO_CONFIG = "configs/domain_liquidity.yaml"


def _raw() -> dict:
    return yaml.safe_load(Path(REPO_CONFIG).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def test_repo_config_loads_every_key_typed():
    cfg = load_domain_config(REPO_CONFIG)
    assert cfg.domain == "market_liquidity" and cfg.symbol == "BTCUSDT"
    assert cfg.features_module == "sentinalops.features.microstructure"
    assert cfg.feature_windows_seconds == (1, 5, 30, 60)
    assert cfg.label_horizon_seconds == 30 and cfg.stale_ms == 3000
    assert cfg.label.depth_fraction == 0.5 and cfg.label.secondary == "spread_expansion"
    assert cfg.sources.live == ["binance_ws_aggTrade", "binance_ws_depth5_100ms"]
    assert cfg.feature_names == FEATURE_ORDER


def test_default_domain_is_liquidity():
    assert get_domain_config().domain == "market_liquidity"
    assert get_domain_config() is get_domain_config()


def test_label_and_feature_params_come_from_the_config():
    cfg = load_domain_config(REPO_CONFIG)
    lp = cfg.label_params()
    assert lp == LabelParams.from_domain_yaml(REPO_CONFIG)
    assert (lp.horizon_s, lp.reference_window_s, lp.min_reference_ticks, lp.stale_ms) == (30, 600, 300, 3000)
    assert lp.use_spread is True
    fp = cfg.feature_params()
    assert fp.windows_s == (1, 5, 30, 60) and fp.stale_ms == 3000 and fp.names == FEATURE_ORDER


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "patch",
    [
        {"unknown_key": 1},  # typo protection
        {"alert_threshold": 1.5},
        {"symbol": "btc-usdt"},
        {"label_horizon_seconds": 0},
        {"feature_windows_seconds": [60, 30]},
        {"feature_windows_seconds": [5, 5]},
        {"split_embargo_seconds": 10},  # shorter than the horizon leaks labels across the split
        {"drift_watch": ["no_such_feature"]},
        {"drift_watch": []},
        {"stale_data_seconds": 0},
        {"label": {"depth_fraction": 1.2}},
        {"label": {"spread_multiple": 0.9}},
        {"label": {"onset_only": False}},
        {"label": {"primary": "something_else"}},
        {"label": {"reference_window_seconds": 100, "min_reference_seconds": 300}},
        {"label": {"typo": 1}},
        {"prediction_cadence_seconds": 7},  # 30 s horizon is not a multiple of 7 s
    ],
)
def test_bad_values_raise(patch):
    raw = _raw()
    for k, v in patch.items():
        raw[k] = {**raw[k], **v} if isinstance(v, dict) and isinstance(raw.get(k), dict) else v
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        DomainConfig(**raw)


def test_spread_label_can_be_switched_off():
    raw = _raw()
    raw["label"]["secondary"] = None
    assert DomainConfig(**raw).label_params().use_spread is False


def test_non_mapping_yaml_is_rejected(tmp_path):
    p = tmp_path / "bad.yaml"
    p.write_text("- just\n- a list\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        load_domain_config(p)


def test_settings_defaults_and_env_override():
    s = Settings.from_env({})
    assert s.model_path == Path("models/champion.joblib") and s.alert_threshold is None
    assert s.domain_config == Path("configs/domain_liquidity.yaml")
    s = Settings.from_env({"ALERT_THRESHOLD": "0.8", "LOG_LEVEL": "DEBUG", "KAFKA_BROKER": ""})
    assert s.alert_threshold == 0.8 and s.log_level == "DEBUG"
    assert s.kafka_broker == "localhost:9092"  # empty string ignored


def test_effective_threshold_prefers_env_over_domain():
    cfg = load_domain_config(REPO_CONFIG)
    assert Settings().effective_threshold(cfg) == cfg.alert_threshold
    assert Settings(alert_threshold=0.9).effective_threshold(cfg) == 0.9


def test_model_block_defaults_to_gbm_v1():
    raw = _raw()
    raw.pop("model", None)
    cfg = DomainConfig(**raw)
    assert cfg.model.name == "gbm_v1" and cfg.model.params == {}


def test_unknown_model_name_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="unknown model 'no_such_model'"):
        DomainConfig(**{**_raw(), "model": {"name": "no_such_model"}})


def test_yaml_model_block_loads():
    assert _raw()["model"] == {"name": "gbm_v1", "params": {}}
    assert load_domain_config(REPO_CONFIG).model.name == "gbm_v1"


=== tests\test_deploy_files.py ===
"""The Oracle deployment kit: unit files, setup script syntax, preflight and monitor."""

import importlib.util
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

KIT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "deploy" / "oracle"


def load(name):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, KIT / f"{name}.py")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


@pytest.mark.parametrize("unit", ["sentinalops-live.service", "sentinalops-heal.service"])

=== tests\test_heal.py ===
"""The self-healing loop (chunks 39, 42-45)."""

import math

import joblib
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from sentinalops.config import DomainConfig, get_domain_config
from sentinalops.heal import controller as C
from sentinalops.heal.monitor import WindowScores, degradation, window_scores
from sentinalops.heal.registry import ModelRegistry
from sentinalops.heal.retrain import assemble, evaluate_gates, train_challenger
from sentinalops.schemas import OutcomeLabel, StressPrediction
from sentinalops.serving.ledger import Ledger
from sentinalops.serving.model import ChampionModel
from sentinalops.simulator import heal_sim as S
from sentinalops.simulator.regimes import make_ticks
from sentinalops.training.train_baseline import train

T0 = 1_763_200_800_000
MIN = 60_000
BASE = get_domain_config()


def config(**heal) -> DomainConfig:
    h = {**BASE.heal.model_dump(), "metrics_window_minutes": 20, "min_matured_labels": 1500,
         "cooldown_minutes": 20, "shadow_minutes": 20, **heal}
    return DomainConfig(**{**BASE.model_dump(), "retrain_window_hours": 1, "heal": h})


CFG = config()


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def calm_model():
    return train(make_ticks("calm", 20_000, seed=7))


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def volatile_model():
    return train(make_ticks("volatile", 20_000, seed=8))


# ---- 39 monitoring --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def scores(ap=0.5, positives=50):
    return WindowScores(None, 0, 1, 1000, positives, ap, 0.05, 0.05, 0.5, 0.5)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "ap, positives, reference, expected",
    [
        (0.20, 50, 0.60, (True, "degraded")),
        (0.50, 50, 0.60, (False, "healthy")),  # within the 0.15 margin
        (0.05, 5, 0.60, (False, "too_few_events")),  # no verdict on thin evidence
        (0.20, 50, math.nan, (False, "no_reference")),
    ],
)
def test_degradation_truth_table(ap, positives, reference, expected):
    d = degradation(scores(ap, positives), reference, CFG.heal)
    assert (d.degraded, d.reason) == expected


def test_window_scores_from_the_ledger(tmp_path):
    led = Ledger(tmp_path / "l.sqlite")
    rows = [(0.9, 1), (0.8, 0), (0.3, 1), (0.1, 0), (0.2, 0)]
    for i, (p, y) in enumerate(rows):
        ts = T0 + i * 1000
        led.add_prediction(StressPrediction(symbol="BTCUSDT", ts_ms=ts, horizon_s=30, probability=p,
                                            alert=p >= 0.5, threshold=0.5, model_version="v1"))
        led.add_outcome(OutcomeLabel(symbol="BTCUSDT", ts_ms=ts, horizon_s=30, label=y))
    led.add_prediction(StressPrediction(symbol="BTCUSDT", ts_ms=T0 + 9000, horizon_s=30, probability=0.5,
                                        alert=True, threshold=0.5, model_version="v1"))  # pending
    w = window_scores(led, T0, T0 + 10_000, "v1")
    assert (w.n, w.positives) == (5, 2)
    assert w.ap == pytest.approx((1 / 1 + 2 / 3) / 2)
    assert w.precision == 0.5 and w.recall == 0.5
    assert w.brier == pytest.approx(sum((p - y) ** 2 for p, y in rows) / 5)
    empty = window_scores(led, T0 + 60_000, T0 + 70_000)
    assert empty.n == 0 and math.isnan(empty.ap) and math.isnan(empty.brier)


# ---- 45 registry ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_registry_promote_shadow_rollback(tmp_path, calm_model, volatile_model):
    reg = ModelRegistry(tmp_path)
    v1 = reg.register(calm_model[0], {"holdout_ap": 0.6}, T0)
    v2 = reg.register(volatile_model[0], {"holdout_ap": 0.7}, T0 + 1)
    assert (v1, v2) == ("v001", "v002")
    reg.promote(v1, T0, "bootstrap")
    served = ChampionModel(reg.champion_path, BASE.feature_names)
    assert served.current()[1] == "v001" and not reg.shadow_path.exists()

    reg.promote(v2, T0 + MIN, "heal", shadow_ms=10 * MIN)
    s = reg.state()
    assert (s.champion, s.shadow, s.shadow_until_ms) == ("v002", "v001", T0 + 11 * MIN)
    assert served.current()[1] == "v002"  # hot reload picks up the swap
    assert ChampionModel(reg.shadow_path, BASE.feature_names).current()[1] == "v001"

    assert reg.rollback(T0 + 5 * MIN, "test") == "v001"
    assert served.current()[1] == "v001" and not reg.shadow_path.exists()
    assert (reg.count("promote"), reg.count("rollback")) == (2, 1)
    assert joblib.load(reg.version_path("v002")).sentinal_version_ == "v002"  # versions never change
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        reg.rollback(T0 + 6 * MIN, "nothing to roll back to")
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        reg.promote("v999", T0, "missing")


# ---- 43 training data -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_assemble_respects_embargo_window_and_regimes():
    table = S.market([("calm", 60), ("volatile", 30), ("calm", 60)], T0)
    table.loc[table.index[::50], "label_status"] = "void_gap"
    now = T0 + 150 * MIN
    ts = assemble(table, now, CFG, seed=1)
    start = now - 60 * MIN
    assert (ts.holdout["ts_ms"] >= start).all() and (ts.holdout["ts_ms"] < now).all()
    recent_train = ts.train[ts.train["ts_ms"] >= start]
    assert recent_train["ts_ms"].max() + CFG.split_embargo_seconds * 1000 < ts.holdout["ts_ms"].min()
    replay = ts.train[ts.train["ts_ms"] < start]
    assert set(replay["regime"]) == {"calm", "volatile"}  # older regimes are kept in the mix
    assert len(replay) == pytest.approx(len(recent_train) * CFG.heal.replay_fraction, rel=0.05)
    assert set(replay.index).isdisjoint(ts.older_eval.index)
    for part in (ts.train, ts.holdout, ts.older_eval):
        assert (part["label_status"] == "ok").all()
    assert ts.counts["holdout"] == len(ts.holdout) and ts.counts["regime_mix"]


# ---- 44 gates -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_gates_pass_for_a_real_improvement_and_fail_for_none(calm_model):
    table = S.market([("calm", 60), ("volatile", 70)], T0)
    ts = assemble(table, T0 + 130 * MIN, CFG)
    challenger = train_challenger(ts, CFG)
    good = evaluate_gates(calm_model[0], challenger, ts, CFG)
    assert good.passed, good.to_markdown()
    same = evaluate_gates(calm_model[0], calm_model[0], ts, CFG)
    assert not same.passed and not same.gates["improves_current_regime"]["passed"]
    assert "FAILED" in same.to_markdown() and '"passed": false' in same.to_json()


def test_regression_gate_blocks_a_model_that_forgot_the_old_regime(calm_model, volatile_model):
    table = S.market([("calm", 60), ("volatile", 70)], T0)
    ts = assemble(table, T0 + 130 * MIN, config(max_regression=0.05))
    report = evaluate_gates(calm_model[0], volatile_model[0], ts, config(max_regression=0.05))
    assert report.gates["improves_current_regime"]["passed"]
    assert not report.gates["no_regression_on_older"]["passed"] and not report.passed


# ---- 42 trigger rule --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
@pytest.fixture
def ctl(tmp_path, calm_model, monkeypatch):
    reg = ModelRegistry(tmp_path / "reg")
    c = C.HealController(CFG, Ledger(tmp_path / "l.sqlite"), reg, lambda now: pd.DataFrame())
    c.bootstrap(calm_model[0], 0.6, T0)
    state = {"ap": 0.6, "n": 5000}

    def fake_scores(ledger, since, until, version=None):
        return WindowScores(version, since, until, state["n"], 100, state["ap"], 0.05, 0.05, 0.5, 0.5)

    monkeypatch.setattr(C, "window_scores", fake_scores)
    return c, state


def test_trigger_needs_degradation_and_enough_labels(ctl):
    c, state = ctl
    assert c.decide(T0 + 60 * MIN).reason == "healthy"
    state["ap"] = 0.2
    state["n"] = 100
    assert c.decide(T0 + 60 * MIN).reason == "too_few_matured_labels"
    state["n"] = 5000
    assert c.decide(T0 + 60 * MIN).fire


def test_cooldown_and_idempotency(ctl):
    c, state = ctl
    state["ap"] = 0.2
    c.registry.log_event(T0 + 60 * MIN, "heal_attempt", champion="v001", matured=5000)
    assert c.decide(T0 + 70 * MIN).reason == "cooldown"  # 20 min cool-down
    assert c.decide(T0 + 90 * MIN).reason == "already_attempted"  # same evidence: no second retrain
    state["n"] = 5000 + CFG.heal.min_matured_labels
    assert c.decide(T0 + 90 * MIN).fire  # enough new evidence


def test_no_trigger_without_a_champion_or_during_shadow(tmp_path, calm_model):
    reg = ModelRegistry(tmp_path)
    c = C.HealController(CFG, Ledger(tmp_path / "l.sqlite"), reg, lambda now: pd.DataFrame())
    assert c.decide(T0).reason == "no_champion"
    v1 = c.bootstrap(calm_model[0], 0.6, T0)
    v2 = reg.register(calm_model[0], {"holdout_ap": 0.6}, T0)
    reg.promote(v2, T0, "test", shadow_ms=MIN)
    assert c.decide(T0).reason == "shadow_period" and reg.state().shadow == v1
    assert c.tick(T0 + 1000).action == "waiting"


# ---- the whole loop ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_heal_loop_recovers_from_a_regime_shift(tmp_path, calm_model):
    """Calm, then volatile: the champion degrades, a challenger passes the gates, the score recovers."""
    table = S.market([("calm", 90), ("volatile", 150)], T0)
    reg, led = ModelRegistry(tmp_path / "reg"), Ledger(tmp_path / "l.sqlite")
    c = C.HealController(CFG, led, reg, S.matured_table(table, 30))
    c.bootstrap(calm_model[0], calm_model[1], T0)
    steps = S.run(CFG, table, reg, led, c, step_minutes=5)
    actions = [(int((s.now_ms - T0) / MIN), s.result.action) for s in steps]
    shift = 90

    assert all(a == "idle" for m, a in actions if m <= shift), "no retraining while the market is calm"
    promoted = [m for m, a in actions if a == "retrained_promoted"]
    assert len(promoted) == 1 and shift < promoted[0] <= shift + 60
    assert any(a == "shadow_kept" for _, a in actions) and reg.count("rollback") == 0

    worst = min(s.live_ap for s in steps if shift + 10 <= (s.now_ms - T0) / MIN < promoted[0])
    healed = [s.live_ap for s in steps if (s.now_ms - T0) / MIN >= promoted[0] + 25]
    assert worst < 0.25, "the champion should degrade after the shift"
    assert min(healed) > 0.45 and sum(healed) / len(healed) > worst + 0.35, "and recover after healing"
    report = (reg.root / "reports" / f"{reg.state().champion}.md").read_text()
    assert "PASSED" in report
    healed_versions = [v for v, m in reg.state().versions.items() if m.get("source") == "heal"]
    assert healed_versions and all(reg.meta(v)["model_name"] == "gbm_v1" for v in healed_versions)


# ---- the model slot: heal retrains the champion's own recipe ---------------------------------------
class _LogitRecipe:
    name = "logit_test"

    def fit(self, train, cfg, seed=0, params=None):
        from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
        from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
        from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler

        from sentinalops.models.base import stamp

        model = make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), LogisticRegression(max_iter=500))
        model.fit(train[cfg.feature_names], train["label"].astype(int))
        return stamp(model, self.name, params or {})


LOGIT = _LogitRecipe()


def test_heal_retrains_champion_recipe_not_config(monkeypatch):
    from sentinalops import models
    from sentinalops.heal.retrain import resolve_recipe

    monkeypatch.setitem(models._RECIPES, "logit_test", f"{__name__}:LOGIT")
    table = S.market([("calm", 60)], T0)
    ts = assemble(table, T0 + 60 * MIN, CFG)
    champion = LOGIT.fit(ts.train, CFG)
    assert CFG.model.name == "gbm_v1"  # the config names another recipe
    assert resolve_recipe(champion, CFG) == ("logit_test", {})
    challenger = train_challenger(ts, CFG, champion=champion)
    assert models.model_name(challenger) == "logit_test"


def test_unstamped_champion_falls_back_to_config():
    from sklearn.ensemble import HistGradientBoostingClassifier

    from sentinalops.heal.retrain import resolve_recipe

    assert resolve_recipe(HistGradientBoostingClassifier(), CFG) == ("gbm_v1", {})
    assert resolve_recipe(None, CFG) == ("gbm_v1", {})


def test_bad_promotion_is_rolled_back_after_the_shadow_period(tmp_path, calm_model, volatile_model):
    table = S.market([("volatile", 40)], T0)
    reg, led = ModelRegistry(tmp_path / "reg"), Ledger(tmp_path / "l.sqlite")
    c = C.HealController(CFG, led, reg, S.matured_table(table, 30))
    good = c.bootstrap(volatile_model[0], volatile_model[1], T0)
    bad = reg.register(calm_model[0], {"holdout_ap": calm_model[1]}, T0)
    reg.promote(bad, T0, "forced, to test rollback", shadow_ms=20 * MIN)  # skips the gates on purpose
    steps = S.run(CFG, table, reg, led, c, step_minutes=5)
    rolled = [s for s in steps if s.result.action == "rolled_back"]
    assert len(rolled) == 1 and rolled[0].result.version == good
    assert reg.state().champion == good and reg.count("rollback") == 1
    assert rolled[0].result.details["new"]["ap"] < rolled[0].result.details["old"]["ap"]


def test_processor_scores_with_the_shadow_model_too(tmp_path, calm_model, volatile_model):
    from pipeline_helpers import H0, make_events
    from sentinalops.stream.processor import PREDICTIONS_TOPIC, StreamProcessor

    reg = ModelRegistry(tmp_path)
    reg.promote(reg.register(calm_model[0], {}, T0), T0, "a")
    reg.promote(reg.register(volatile_model[0], {}, T0), T0, "b", shadow_ms=MIN)
    names = BASE.feature_names
    proc = StreamProcessor(BASE, model=ChampionModel(reg.champion_path, names),
                           shadow=ChampionModel(reg.shadow_path, names))
    d, t = make_events(H0, 75, seed=3)
    events = [("depth5", r) for r in d.to_dict("records")] + [("aggTrade", r) for r in t.to_dict("records")]
    events.sort(key=lambda e: e[1]["recv_time_ms"])
    preds = [m for k, r in events for topic, m in proc.on_event(k, r) if topic == PREDICTIONS_TOPIC]
    assert preds and {p.model_version for p in preds} == {"v001", "v002"}
    assert proc.stats.shadow_predictions == proc.stats.predictions


=== tests\test_labels.py ===
"""Tests for the liquidity-stress label: semantics, and proof that nothing looks ahead."""

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

from sentinalops.labels import (
    LabelParams,
    add_labels,
    add_stress,
    average_precision,
    base_rate_report,
    book_state,
    build_labels,
    fit_thresholds,
    label_at,
    to_ticks,
    verdict,
)

T0 = 1_763_210_000_000  # a whole second
SMALL = LabelParams(horizon_s=5, reference_window_s=20, min_reference_ticks=10, confirm_ticks=2)
CAUSAL = [
    "mid",
    "rel_spread",
    "depth_top5",
    "ref_depth",
    "ref_spread",
    "depth_ratio",
    "spread_ratio",
    "stressed",
    "confirmed",
]


def make_depth(
    seconds: int, depth=lambda s: 100.0, spread_ticks=lambda s: 1, skip=lambda s: False
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Snapshots every 100 ms. ``depth(s)`` is total top-5 quantity at second-offset ``s`` (float)."""
    rows = []
    for k in range(seconds * 10):
        s = k / 10
        if skip(s):
            continue
        bid, ask = 100.00, 100.00 + 0.01 * spread_ticks(s)
        row = {"symbol": "BTCUSDT", "last_update_id": k, "recv_time_ms": T0 + k * 100}
        for i in range(1, 6):
            row[f"bid_px_{i}"], row[f"bid_qty_{i}"] = bid - 0.01 * (i - 1), depth(s) / 10
            row[f"ask_px_{i}"], row[f"ask_qty_{i}"] = ask + 0.01 * (i - 1), depth(s) / 10
        rows.append(row)
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def at(labeled: pd.DataFrame, second: int) -> pd.Series:
    return labeled.loc[T0 + second * 1000]


def random_depth(seconds: int, seed: int = 7) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """A noisy book with sudden liquidity withdrawals and a couple of feed gaps."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    level = 100 * np.exp(np.cumsum(rng.normal(0, 0.01, seconds)))
    crash = np.zeros(seconds, dtype=bool)
    for start in rng.choice(np.arange(60, seconds - 20), size=seconds // 60, replace=False):
        crash[start : start + rng.integers(1, 12)] = True
    level = np.where(crash, level * 0.3, level)
    gaps = [(200, 209), (400, 404)] if seconds > 420 else []
    return make_depth(
        seconds, depth=lambda s: float(level[int(s)]), skip=lambda s: any(a <= s < b for a, b in gaps)
    )


# ---- book state and ticks ----------------------------------------------------------------
def test_book_state_values_and_bad_books_dropped():
    depth = make_depth(1, depth=lambda s: 80.0, spread_ticks=lambda s: 2)
    depth.loc[3, "ask_px_1"] = depth.loc[3, "bid_px_1"]  # crossed book
    state = book_state(depth)
    assert len(state) == 9
    assert state["mid"].iloc[0] == pytest.approx(100.01)
    assert state["rel_spread"].iloc[0] == pytest.approx(0.02 / 100.01)
    assert state["depth_top5"].iloc[0] == pytest.approx(80.0)


def test_ticks_never_use_a_snapshot_from_after_the_tick():
    state = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "recv_time_ms": [T0 - 50, T0 + 1, T0 + 999, T0 + 1000],
            "mid": [1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0],
            "rel_spread": 0.1,
            "depth_top5": 5.0,
        }
    )
    ticks = to_ticks(state.assign(recv_time_ms=state["recv_time_ms"]), LabelParams())
    assert ticks.index.tolist() == [T0, T0 + 1000]
    assert ticks["mid"].tolist() == [1.0, 4.0]  # tick T0 ignores the snapshot 1 ms later
    assert ticks["age_ms"].tolist() == [50, 0]


def test_stale_book_makes_ticks_unknown_and_grid_stays_regular():
    depth = make_depth(20, skip=lambda s: 5 <= s < 12)
    ticks = to_ticks(book_state(depth), LabelParams(stale_ms=3000))
    assert np.all(np.diff(ticks.index) == 1000) and len(ticks) == 20
    unknown = [int((t - T0) / 1000) for t in ticks.index[ticks["depth_top5"].isna()]]
    assert unknown == [8, 9, 10, 11]  # 5-7 still within 3 s of the last snapshot at 4.9 s


# ---- causality ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_reference_excludes_the_current_tick():
    depth = make_depth(40, depth=lambda s: 5.0 if s >= 30 else 100.0)
    stress = add_stress(to_ticks(book_state(depth), SMALL), SMALL)
    assert at(stress, 30)["ref_depth"] == 100.0  # the collapse at 30 s is not in its own reference
    assert at(stress, 30)["depth_ratio"] == pytest.approx(0.05)
    assert np.isnan(at(stress, 5)["ref_depth"])  # warm-up: fewer than min_reference_ticks


@pytest.mark.parametrize("cut", [120, 333, 571])
def test_stress_state_does_not_change_when_the_future_changes(cut):
    """Truncate the data at ``cut``: every causal column at or before the cut is identical."""
    depth = random_depth(600)
    full = add_stress(to_ticks(book_state(depth), SMALL), SMALL)
    past = depth[depth["recv_time_ms"] <= T0 + cut * 1000]
    truncated = add_stress(to_ticks(book_state(past), SMALL), SMALL)
    assert truncated.index[-1] == T0 + cut * 1000
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(truncated[CAUSAL], full.loc[truncated.index, CAUSAL])


@pytest.mark.parametrize("cut", [150, 420])
def test_label_depends_only_on_its_own_horizon(cut):
    depth = random_depth(600)
    full = build_labels(depth, SMALL)
    truncated = build_labels(depth[depth["recv_time_ms"] <= T0 + cut * 1000], SMALL)
    settled = truncated.index[: -SMALL.horizon_ticks]  # horizon ends at or before the cut
    pd.testing.assert_series_equal(truncated.loc[settled, "label"], full.loc[settled, "label"])
    assert truncated["label"].iloc[-SMALL.horizon_ticks :].isna().all()  # incomplete horizon is void


def test_vectorised_labels_match_the_slow_reference_everywhere():
    labeled = build_labels(random_depth(600), SMALL)
    assert labeled["label"].sum() > 20 and (labeled["label"] == 0).sum() > 100  # the test has teeth
    for ts, value in labeled["label"].items():
        expected = label_at(labeled, ts, SMALL)
        assert (expected is None and np.isnan(value)) or expected == value, ts


# ---- onset semantics -----------------------------------------------------------------------
def test_onset_label_by_hand():
    depth = make_depth(60, depth=lambda s: 30.0 if 40 <= s < 45 else 100.0)
    lab = build_labels(depth, SMALL)
    stressed = [s for s in range(60) if at(lab, s)["stressed"] == 1]
    confirmed = [s for s in range(60) if at(lab, s)["confirmed"] == 1]
    assert stressed == [40, 41, 42, 43, 44] and confirmed == [41, 42, 43, 44]

    positives = [s for s in range(60) if at(lab, s)["label"] == 1]
    assert positives == [36, 37, 38, 39]  # calm ticks whose next 5 s contain confirmed stress
    assert [at(lab, s)["time_to_onset_s"] for s in positives] == [5, 4, 3, 2]
    for s in range(40, 45):  # already stressed: not a forecast, so not eligible
        assert not at(lab, s)["eligible"] and np.isnan(at(lab, s)["label"])
    assert at(lab, 35)["label"] == 0 and at(lab, 46)["label"] == 0
    assert at(lab, 36)["fwd_min_depth_ratio"] == pytest.approx(0.3)
    assert np.isnan(at(lab, 58)["label"])  # horizon runs past the end of the data


def test_one_tick_flicker_is_not_an_event():
    lab = build_labels(make_depth(60, depth=lambda s: 30.0 if 40 <= s < 41 else 100.0), SMALL)
    assert at(lab, 40)["stressed"] == 1 and (lab["confirmed"] == 1).sum() == 0
    assert lab["label"].sum() == 0


def test_feed_gap_voids_every_label_whose_horizon_touches_it():
    depth = make_depth(80, depth=lambda s: 30.0 if 50 <= s < 55 else 100.0, skip=lambda s: 30 <= s < 40)
    lab = build_labels(depth, SMALL)
    unknown = [s for s in range(20, 80) if np.isnan(at(lab, s)["stressed"])]  # after the warm-up
    assert unknown == [33, 34, 35, 36, 37, 38, 39]  # 30-32 are still within stale_ms of the last snapshot
    for s in range(28, 40):  # horizon (s, s+5] overlaps an unknown tick, or the tick itself is unknown
        assert np.isnan(at(lab, s)["label"]) and not at(lab, s)["eligible"], s
    assert at(lab, 27)["label"] == 0 and at(lab, 40)["label"] == 0 and at(lab, 47)["label"] == 1


def test_spread_rule_is_secondary_and_switchable():
    depth = make_depth(60, spread_ticks=lambda s: 5 if 40 <= s < 45 else 1)
    on = build_labels(depth, SMALL)
    off = build_labels(depth, LabelParams(**{**SMALL.__dict__, "use_spread": False}))
    assert on["label"].sum() == 4 and at(on, 41)["stress_spread"] == 1 and at(on, 41)["stress_depth"] == 0
    assert off["label"].sum() == 0


# ---- thresholds, baseline, report ----------------------------------------------------------
def test_fit_thresholds_targets_the_requested_share_and_drops_a_pinned_spread():
    stress = add_stress(to_ticks(book_state(random_depth(3000, seed=3)), SMALL), SMALL)
    fitted = fit_thresholds(stress, SMALL, stress_share=0.05)
    assert fitted.use_spread is False  # spread never leaves one tick, so it has no tail to use
    refit = add_stress(stress[["mid", "rel_spread", "depth_top5", "age_ms"]], fitted)
    assert refit["stressed"].mean() == pytest.approx(0.05, abs=0.01)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        fit_thresholds(stress.iloc[:200], SMALL)


def test_average_precision():
    assert average_precision(np.array([1, 0, 1, 0]), np.array([0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.1])) == pytest.approx(5 / 6)
    assert average_precision(np.array([0, 1, 1]), np.array([0.1, 0.9, 0.8])) == 1.0
    assert np.isnan(average_precision(np.zeros(3), np.arange(3)))


def test_base_rate_report_and_verdict():
    lab = build_labels(make_depth(60, depth=lambda s: 30.0 if 40 <= s < 45 else 100.0), SMALL)
    rep = base_rate_report(lab, SMALL)
    assert rep["episodes"] == 1 and rep["positives"] == 4 and rep["median_episode_s"] == 4
    assert rep["stress_from_spread_share"] == 0 and rep["busiest_hour_share_of_positives"] == 1.0
    notes = " ".join(verdict(rep))
    assert "NO-GO" in notes and "spread rule" in notes and "one hour" in notes

    empty = base_rate_report(build_labels(make_depth(5), SMALL), SMALL)
    assert empty["eligible_ticks"] == 0 and verdict(empty)[0].startswith("NO-GO")


def test_params_validate_and_load_from_the_domain_config():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        LabelParams(depth_fraction=1.2)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        LabelParams(horizon_s=0)
    p = LabelParams.from_domain_yaml("configs/domain_liquidity.yaml")
    assert (p.horizon_s, p.reference_window_s, p.depth_fraction, p.stale_ms) == (30, 600, 0.5, 3000)
    assert p.use_spread is True


def test_labels_accept_rows_exactly_as_the_recorder_writes_them():
    messages = pytest.importorskip("sentinalops.data.recorder.messages")
    from recorder_helpers import depth_frame

    rows = [messages.parse_message(depth_frame(i), T0 + i * 100)[1] for i in range(300)]
    lab = add_labels(add_stress(to_ticks(book_state(pd.DataFrame(rows)), SMALL), SMALL), SMALL)
    assert len(lab) == 30 and lab["stressed"].iloc[-1] == 0


=== tests\test_live_bus.py ===
"""The live bus: shared contract (memory and Kafka) and the in-memory overflow policy."""

import asyncio
import os

import pytest

from sentinalops.live.bus import InMemoryBus, stable_prediction_id

BUSES = ["memory"] + (["kafka"] if os.environ.get("KAFKA_BROKER") else [])


def make_bus(kind: str, **kw):
    if kind == "memory":
        return InMemoryBus(**kw)
    from sentinalops.live.kafka_bus import KafkaBus

    return KafkaBus(os.environ["KAFKA_BROKER"], client_id=f"test-{os.getpid()}")


async def take(sub, n, timeout=10.0):
    out = []
    async with asyncio.timeout(timeout):
        async for msg in sub:
            out.append(msg)
            if len(out) == n:
                break
    return out


@pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", BUSES)
def test_bus_fanout_and_order(kind):
    async def main():
        bus = make_bus(kind)
        a = await bus.subscribe(["raw_depth", "raw_trades"], group="a")
        b = await bus.subscribe(["raw_depth"], group="b")
        for i in range(5):
            await bus.publish("raw_depth", "BTCUSDT", {"i": i})
        await bus.publish("raw_trades", "BTCUSDT", {"i": 99})
        got_a, got_b = await take(a, 6), await take(b, 5)
        await bus.close()
        return got_a, got_b

    got_a, got_b = asyncio.run(main())
    assert [v["i"] for t, v in got_a if t == "raw_depth"] == [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]
    assert ("raw_trades", {"i": 99}) in got_a
    assert [v["i"] for _, v in got_b] == [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", BUSES)
def test_bus_close_ends_subscription(kind):
    async def main():
        bus = make_bus(kind)
        sub = await bus.subscribe(["outcomes"], group="c")
        await bus.publish("outcomes", "BTCUSDT", {"x": 1})
        await bus.close()
        async with asyncio.timeout(10):
            return [m async for m in sub]

    assert asyncio.run(main()) == [("outcomes", {"x": 1})]


def test_bus_overflow_drops_oldest():
    async def main():
        bus = InMemoryBus(maxsize=3)
        sub = await bus.subscribe(["raw_depth"], group="slow")
        for i in range(5):
            await bus.publish("raw_depth", "BTCUSDT", {"i": i})
        got = await take(sub, 3)
        return bus, got

    bus, got = asyncio.run(main())
    assert [v["i"] for _, v in got] == [2, 3, 4]
    assert bus.drops["raw_depth"] == 2


def test_bus_rejects_unknown_topic():
    async def main():
        bus = InMemoryBus()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unknown topic"):
            await bus.publish("nope", "k", {})
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unknown topic"):
            await bus.subscribe(["nope"], group="g")

    asyncio.run(main())


def test_drain_waits_for_consumers():
    async def main():
        bus = InMemoryBus()
        sub = await bus.subscribe(["features"], group="d")
        seen = []

        async def consume():
            async for _, v in sub:
                await asyncio.sleep(0)
                seen.append(v["i"])

        task = asyncio.create_task(consume())
        for i in range(20):
            await bus.publish("features", "BTCUSDT", {"i": i})
        await bus.drain()
        n = len(seen)
        await bus.close()
        await task
        return n

    assert asyncio.run(main()) == 20


def test_stable_prediction_id_deterministic():
    a = stable_prediction_id("BTCUSDT", 1_763_200_800_000, "v001")
    assert a == stable_prediction_id("BTCUSDT", 1_763_200_800_000, "v001")
    assert a != stable_prediction_id("BTCUSDT", 1_763_200_801_000, "v001")
    assert a != stable_prediction_id("BTCUSDT", 1_763_200_800_000, "v002")
    assert len(a) == 32 and int(a, 16) >= 0


def test_kafka_bus_import_error_message(monkeypatch):
    import sys

    from sentinalops.live import kafka_bus

    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "aiokafka", None)  # as if aiokafka were not installed
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match=r"pip install .*\[stream\]"):
        kafka_bus.KafkaBus("localhost:9092")


def test_kafka_bus_serialises_json_and_rejects_unknown_topics():
    from sentinalops.live.kafka_bus import decode, encode

    assert decode(encode({"a": 1.5, "b": None})) == {"a": 1.5, "b": None}

    async def main():
        from sentinalops.live.kafka_bus import KafkaBus

        bus = KafkaBus("localhost:1")  # never connects: the topic check comes first
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unknown topic"):
            await bus.publish("nope", "k", {})

    asyncio.run(main())


=== tests\test_live_feed.py ===
"""Binance feed -> bus, the live CLI's argument rules, and the health line."""

import asyncio
import json

import pytest
from sentinalops.live.feed import BinanceFeed

from recorder_helpers import LOCAL, FakeExchange, depth_frame, trade_frame
from sentinalops.data.recorder import RecorderConfig
from sentinalops.live.bus import InMemoryBus


def test_feed_publishes_raw_topics_via_fake_exchange(tmp_path):
    async def script(ws, n):
        for i in (1, 2, 3, 3):  # the last one is a duplicate update id
            await ws.send(depth_frame(i))
        for i in (1, 2):
            await ws.send(trade_frame(i))
        await asyncio.sleep(30)

    async def main():
        async with FakeExchange(script) as ex:
            cfg = RecorderConfig(symbols=("BTCUSDT",), data_dir=tmp_path, base_url=ex.base_url,
                                 allowed_hosts=LOCAL, flush_interval_s=3600, stale_after_s=5,
                                 stats_interval_s=3600, open_timeout_s=5)
            bus = InMemoryBus()
            sub = await bus.subscribe(["raw_depth", "raw_trades"], group="t")
            feed = BinanceFeed(bus, cfg, record=False)
            task = asyncio.create_task(feed.run())
            got = []
            async with asyncio.timeout(15):
                async for msg in sub:
                    got.append(msg)
                    if len(got) == 5:
                        break
            feed.request_stop()
            await asyncio.wait_for(task, 10)
            return got, feed

    got, feed = asyncio.run(main())
    assert [t for t, _ in got].count("raw_depth") == 3 and [t for t, _ in got].count("raw_trades") == 2
    assert feed.recorder.stats.duplicates == 1 and feed.feed_drops == 0
    assert not list(tmp_path.glob("stream=*/**/*.parquet"))  # record=False: nothing archived


def test_feed_queue_drops_oldest_when_full(tmp_path):
    cfg = RecorderConfig(symbols=("BTCUSDT",), data_dir=tmp_path, base_url="ws://127.0.0.1:1",
                         allowed_hosts=LOCAL)

    async def main():
        feed = BinanceFeed(InMemoryBus(), cfg, record=False, maxsize=2)
        for i in range(5):
            feed._on_row("depth5", {"symbol": "BTCUSDT", "i": i})
        return feed, [feed._queue.get_nowait()[1]["i"] for _ in range(2)]

    feed, kept = asyncio.run(main())
    assert kept == [3, 4] and feed.feed_drops == 3


def test_cli_rejects_split_role_on_memory_bus(capsys):
    from sentinalops.live.__main__ import main

    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        main(["--role", "features", "--bus", "memory"])
    assert "only with --bus kafka" in capsys.readouterr().err


def test_health_line_reads_last_heal_event(tmp_path):
    from sentinalops.live.health import last_heal_event

    (tmp_path / "registry.json").write_text(json.dumps({
        "champion": "v002", "events": [{"ts_ms": 1, "kind": "register", "version": "v002"},
                                       {"ts_ms": 2, "kind": "heal_attempt", "champion": "v001"},
                                       {"ts_ms": 3, "kind": "promote", "version": "v002"}]}))
    ev = last_heal_event(tmp_path)
    assert ev == {"ts_ms": 3, "kind": "promote", "version": "v002", "champion": "v002"}
    assert last_heal_event(tmp_path / "missing") is None


=== tests\test_live_heal.py ===
"""The heal service: bootstraps the served champion, trains from the ledger, idles without evidence."""

import math

import joblib
import pytest

from sentinalops.config import DomainConfig, get_domain_config
from sentinalops.heal.registry import ModelRegistry
from sentinalops.live.heal_service import HealService
from sentinalops.schemas import OutcomeLabel, StressPrediction
from sentinalops.serving.ledger import Ledger
from sentinalops.serving.model import ChampionModel
from sentinalops.simulator import heal_sim as S
from sentinalops.simulator.regimes import make_ticks
from sentinalops.training.train import train

T0 = 1_763_200_800_000
MIN = 60_000
BASE = get_domain_config()
H = {**BASE.heal.model_dump(), "metrics_window_minutes": 20, "min_matured_labels": 1500,
     "cooldown_minutes": 20, "shadow_minutes": 20}
CFG = DomainConfig(**{**BASE.model_dump(), "retrain_window_hours": 1, "heal": H})


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def calm_model():
    model, _, _ = train(make_ticks("calm", 20_000, seed=7), CFG, "gbm_v1", {})
    return model


def test_heal_idles_on_empty_ledger(tmp_path, calm_model):
    reg, led = ModelRegistry(tmp_path / "models"), Ledger(tmp_path / "l.sqlite")
    svc = HealService(CFG, led, reg)
    r = svc.tick(T0)
    assert (r.action, r.decision.reason) == ("idle", "no_champion")
    joblib.dump(calm_model, reg.champion_path)
    r = svc.tick(T0 + MIN)
    assert (r.action, r.decision.reason) == ("idle", "too_few_events")


def test_bootstrap_registers_existing_champion_with_holdout_ap(tmp_path, calm_model):
    reg = ModelRegistry(tmp_path / "models")
    joblib.dump(calm_model, reg.champion_path)
    svc = HealService(CFG, Ledger(tmp_path / "l.sqlite"), reg)
    assert svc.bootstrap_if_needed(T0) == "v001"
    assert reg.state().champion == "v001"
    assert reg.meta("v001")["holdout_ap"] == pytest.approx(calm_model.sentinal_holdout_ap_)
    assert reg.meta("v001")["model_name"] == "gbm_v1"
    assert svc.bootstrap_if_needed(T0 + 1) is None  # already registered: nothing to do


def test_bootstrap_without_holdout_ap_is_nan(tmp_path, calm_model):
    reg = ModelRegistry(tmp_path / "models")
    m = joblib.load(joblib.dump(calm_model, tmp_path / "x.joblib")[0])
    del m.sentinal_holdout_ap_
    joblib.dump(m, reg.champion_path)
    HealService(CFG, Ledger(tmp_path / "l.sqlite"), reg).bootstrap_if_needed(T0)
    assert math.isnan(reg.meta("v001")["holdout_ap"])


def test_heal_fires_from_ledger_data(tmp_path, calm_model):
    """Calm then volatile, scored into the ledger with features; the service retrains from it."""
    table = S.market([("calm", 90), ("volatile", 150)], T0)
